## v6.52 — 연막탄 (smoke.js, built last)
- WPN.smoke (slot 4, kind 'nade', model GUNS.smkg: a grey canister with white bands). H_NADES = he · frost · flare · smoke is the one list of human grenades (4 key, wheel, touch bar, HUD 'SMOKE', loadouts LD_NADES, kits); the buy menu's Equipment row, PVP.GUNS, the supply crate refill and the scenario's Full-ammo reward carry it. Once a round like the other grenades.
- The grenade (updNade): fuse 3 s, it pops when it lies still (after 0.35 s) or at the fuse; the canister stays (a thin jet for 6 s) until its cloud is gone. SMK.pop → SMK.add: the cloud's space is built once (SMK.build): a Dijkstra flood over 0.8 m columns from the canister out to R + EDGE/2 (5.95 m) of travel, a column needing a floor (≤ 1.45 m above the canister; a drop or nothing below: it hangs 1.7 m under the canister) and 0.75 m of headroom, a step from one to the next an open line at knee or head height (losClear, so fences and railings let it through) and no climb over 1.2 m; diagonals need both sides open. Each column keeps its ground, ceiling, travel distance d, its wall distance wd (BFS from columns with a blocked side) and a dome top (TOP 3.8 m at the canister, 35 % lower at the rim, under any ceiling).
- Density (SMK.dens): the column's (Re − d) / EDGE (Re grows over GROW 2.4 s), times the height factor (0.9 m under the top, 0.35 m over the ground), times k (0 → 1 over 0.5 s, LIFE 18 s, FADE 3.2 s). SMK.blocks(a, b): the smoke summed along the line in 0.34 m steps (clouds whose 7.4 m bounding sphere the line crosses) ≥ THR 1.6 m → cut. SMK.hides(a, t): eye to chest and eye to head both cut (never within 1.4 m).
- Who it blinds: PVP.botSee and the bots' line of fire; AI.human's zombie sight and its back-off scan; zombie bots' straight run (B.los); the scenario's spitters and giant; RADAR.look; both aim-name readouts (ui.js, pvp.js). ESMK.at / ESMK.blocks (epbase.js) count the clouds too (SMK.atXZ, SMK.blocks2 at each cloud's middle height), so the chapters' bosses lose a human standing in one and bots do not see an epHide zombie through one.
- The look (SMK.draw, every drawn frame after FX): camera-facing puff quads in one mesh (renderOrder 6.5: over FX, tracers and sparks), sorted far to near, a 2×2 atlas of soft blobs (linear filtered), lit at birth from the light probe (smkLit: light grey by day, darker indoors and at night), faded within 1.4 m of the eye. A cloud keeps np = 70 % of its columns (18–104) puffs of radius 1.4–2.1 m, capped by the wall distance, the floor and the ceiling so none pokes through. While it grows each puff rides out along the flood's parent chain (as far as the front Re) to a column of the whole cloud; later ones come up in place, fewer at the rim. The eye inside: a full-screen veil quad (renderOrder 6.6, no depth test: over the world, under the first-person gun) as thick as the smoke at the camera (0.88 at most), lit like the puffs, with drifting noise; inside a cloud puffs nearer than 2 m are not drawn. The radar shows each cloud as a grey patch (SMK.mini, under the pings). Sound 'smoke': a thump and a 3 s hiss.
- Network: every page grows its own cloud from its copy of the grenade (the 'n' fx); the host sends smk {o, p} where it popped there. A client moves its unconfirmed cloud of that thrower there (rebuilt, its puffs replaced) or, when the host's word comes first, makes the cloud and lets its own grenade claim it. clearNades clears the clouds (round starts, the title, the lobby).
- Bots (대인전 only; SMK.botThink at the bots' thinking rate, SMK.botThrow before anything else in PVP.bot): SMK.aim finds yaw and pitch by flying the grenade (SMK.sim, updNade's flight and bounces in 1/120 s steps): a coarse pitch sweep, a finer one round the best, a little to either side; it must come to rest within 3.2 m (one aim a frame across all bots, 1.5 s before the same bot tries again). The throw: equip, turn, fire, hold still until it leaves the hand; given up after 3 s or for an enemy within 32 m (a forced throw goes on). 폭파미션: attackers past their waypoint, 2–32 m short of the site, smoke the site's spots (bomb.smk on the map, else the side toward the defenders' spawn, S.r + 2.5 m out), two a round per site; defenders smoke a planted bomb from 6–26 m (10 s apart), then defuse inside it. Any 대인전 bot under 50 HP in a fight at 12–30 m (half the time, every 5 s at most) smokes the gap and walks back to a spot out of the way for 4 s. PVP.botLd: a smoke 75 % in 폭파미션, 30 % otherwise; a bot's throw puts its gun back up.
- Tests: the cloud in the port's lane and inside the customs hall (no puff through walls or the roof; the grid follows the gate pillar and the hall's doors); a red board behind a cloud at 16 m hidden from 0.5 to 3.5 m; the growth sequence; client/host matching both ways and the host's smk; 10-minute 폭파미션 sims (17–21 smokes thrown, most within 2.5 m of the aim, no stuck bot), TDM and FFA retreat smokes; zombie mode, the scenario and chapters 1, 2, 4 with clouds dropped on the humans every 9 s (no errors); the buy menu, the HUD, the radar patch.

## v6.53 — 대인전 전용맵 「사막 바자르」 (mapbz.js, built after mapport.js)
- MAPDEFS.bazaar: pvp:1 (대인전 modes only), bomb data, bounds −45…45 × −47…40, probe levels .9 / 2.2 / 3.6 / 4.9 / 6.4, the radar split at 2 m. x runs east, z south. A walled town: the town wall (8 m, merlons) west and east, the south wall with three gates (the west gate between two towers, the main gate's arch, the broken east gate with rubble), the kasbah (10 m, two towers, a great gate, lattices) and rows of houses closing the north. Outside the south wall the caravan camp (tents, a truck, crates, pots, palms) where the attackers start; dunes and rock beyond; far sand plateaus, mesas and palms as scenery (coarse lighting cells: bzSandF / bzStoneF, cs 6 / 4).
- Ways: CT forecourt (fountain, palms) → the north lane west to B's north door / the north road east to A's north gate / the arch street south to mid. Mid is the well square (a round well, palms, a stall, lantern wires) with the tea house (its roof a terrace at 3.2 m behind parapets, stairs up from the square; it overlooks the square and the tannery alley). The spice alley leaves the square west and turns north to B's east door; the tannery alley east and north to A's west door. The west gate opens on the carpet street (stalls, a parked car, carpets on lines) up to the B front yard and the hall's arch; the broken east gate on A long (palms along the wall, a trough, a cart) up to the A front yard and the caravanserai's gate.
- A (31.5, −17, r 6.5): the caravanserai — 6.5 m walls, the north gate, the south gate (sign), the west door. The north gallery (roof at 3.6–4 m on four pillars) carries the terrace behind a parapet; stairs up along the east wall from the courtyard (13 steps, a stepped parapet) and, for the defenders, inside the gallery beside the north gate (12 steps along the north wall through a hole in the gallery roof, rails round the hole). Cover: the crates in the middle, a trough, sacks, a cart, a palm.
- B (−31, −16.2, r 6.5): the covered bazaar hall (6.5 m, roof with three skylights, beams, four pillars, lanterns): the south arch (an awning and the BAZAAR sign over it), the east door, the north door in the north-east corner. The mezzanine (3 m) along the north wall with a balustrade (pass: seen and shot through), stairs down the west wall (the attackers' side) and from the north door along the north wall (the defenders'). Cover: the heap of rolled carpets in the middle, stalls, crates, sacks, pots.
- Houses: blk() splits a rectangle into houses of random width (4–8 m), height and plaster colour (four adobe colours), each a solid box with a roof, a parapet, sometimes a water tank (a round prop) and an aerial, windows (grilles, lintels, now and then an awning) and doors only on the faces listed as street faces. Palms: a round bark trunk (deco cylinder), a solid core, a crown of cut-out fronds (four frond textures: along u, along v and the two diagonals). Stalls: a counter (solid, 1 m), posts, a striped canopy and goods (not solid). Textures (painted on first load): sand, sandstone flags, terracotta floor, roofs, four adobe plasters, two dressed stones, planks, four painted doors, the kasbah gate, windows, lattices, balustrades, four awnings, three carpets, glazed tiles, water, fronds, bark, pots, tanks, fruit, sacks, spices, signs (texPtSign / texPtDec from mapport.js) and the sky (dusty blue, a warm haze, the sun low in the west-south-west matching MAP.moon (−.62, .5, .6), thin cloud, mesas and dunes).
- 폭파미션: way A (37, 12) A long / (17.5, −8) the tannery alley, B (−32.5, 12) the carpet street / (−16.5, −8) the spice alley; holds A: the terrace (two), the north gate, the courtyard's corners; B: the mezzanine (two), the west stairs' foot, the north door, the south-east corner, under the mezzanine; smoke spots (bomb.smk, new: SMK.spots reads them) A (31, −23) in front of the terrace and (37.4, −15) the east stairs, B (−31, −20.6) in front of the mezzanine and (−26.4, −23.6) the north door.
- Bots' smoke throws (smoke.js): the flight is now simulated in 1/180 s steps and a landing is taken only when a throw 0.015 rad higher or lower lands within 5 m too (no throws that a bounce off an edge would send astray).
- Tests: stats (1700 boxes, 377k vertices like the port, loads in 1.7 s, 7254 nav nodes, every hold / spot / way / spawn on a node reachable from the starts), paths (CT → A 41 m, CT → B 42, CT → the terrace 48, CT → the mezzanine 43, T → A 69 / B 72 / mid 36), views (every lane, both sites, the terrace, the mezzanine, the tea house, the kasbah, the camp, the overviews, the title orbit), smoke clouds in the hall, TDM (4 min, 106 kills, respawns fine) and 폭파미션 sims (plants on both sites, a defuse, smokes on the map's spots, nobody stuck).

## v6.54 — 좀비모드 신규 맵 「새벽 종합병원 Saebyeok General Hospital」 (mapho_tex.js + mapho.js, built after mapbz.js)
- MAPDEFS.hospital: a zombie-mode map (no pvp flag: Mutation, Original and the PvP modes except 폭파미션), bounds −75…75 × −52…52 (15 600 m², 1.6× the rest stop), dawn: no sun, noStars, skyTex hoSky (navy overhead, pink and gold low in the east-south-east, stratus lit from below, mountains and the city's apartment blocks with their first lit windows), fog #3c4664 / .0125, MAP.moon = the dawn glow (.86, .3, .18) #c8a8b8 × .42. Probe levels .9 / 2.6 / 5.1 / 9.3 / 13.5 / 15.4 (one a storey: 1F, 2F/P2, 3F/P3, the roof), radar cuts 2.6 / 6.8 / 11 (four bands).
- Storeys are 4.2 m (1F 0, 2F 4.2, 3F 8.4, roof 12.6). Wall textures tile with the storey (sv 4.2, vo 0): window bands, handrails and skirting line up on every floor. hoFacade (outside: white panels, four windows per 8.4 m, blinds), hoWallWin (the inside of an outer wall: the same four windows, the dawn city in them), hoWall / hoWallB / hoWallC (mint, beige, blue-grey: a wooden handrail at 0.9 m, a vinyl wainscot), hoTile (theatres). cs 1–2 m on the big faces keeps it at ~440k vertices.
- The main building x −32…32, z −38…−8. deck(x0,z0,x1,z1,y,floor,zones,holes) lays a slab with a floor per zone (later zones win) and holes (the atrium void x −10…10 × z −28…−16, the stairwells' flights). grid(y,…) builds the same rooms on every floor: north rooms (z −37.6…−31), corridor N (−31…−28), middle rooms (−28…−22), corridor S (−22…−19), south rooms (−19…−8.4); per wing a cross corridor at the far end (x ±28.6…±31.6): every wing is a racetrack. pX / pZ: 0.2 m partitions a storey high with doors [u0,u1], [u0,u1,h] or hatches/windows [u0,u1,y0,y1].
- wallX / wallZ cannot stack openings in one column (the first hole's lintel runs to the top, the next one's sill from the bottom): walls with a door on several floors (the stairwells, the east wall's ER doors) are built a storey at a time.
- stairwell(x0,z0,levels,decals,wall): 3.4 × 6.6 m, door side south. Per storey a flight of 8 (0.2625 m risers, 0.4 m treads: the 1 m nav grid never sees more than 0.79 m between neighbours) up north in the west lane, a half landing, 8 back south in the east lane; a wall between the lanes; the first flight's east lane is filled under; the walls rise 3 m over the top level (the hut on the roof), the decals (1F…RF, P1…P3) on each landing. Lifts: a solid block with door decals.
- 1F: the lobby (granite) under the three-storey atrium (skylight hoSkyG, pass: light and sight through), reception, the floor guide, seats, a ficus, kiosks, vending; the escalator 1F→2F (16 steps, stepped glass balustrades) up the west side to the north gallery, 2F→3F across the void from the south gallery. West: linen store, administration (a service window onto corridor S), records, the convenience store (shelves, a fridge wall, a street door), the pharmacy (its counter along the lobby). East: X-ray, blood draw, the lab, the outpatient waiting room, a clinic, the door into the emergency wing. Behind the lobby: the stores and the dock's two roll-up doors.
- 2F: the ICU behind a glass wall (six beds), radiology (CT gantry, control, reading, waiting, MRI) with the skybridge door, clinics, the staff lounge, the lounge over the entrance with the door out onto the canopy. 3F: the ward (301 four beds, 303 two, the nurse station with a hatch onto corridor S, 305 six, 306 four; a bed barricade in corridor N with a gap), the operating suite behind steel double doors at x 13 (theatres 1–3, scrub, recovery), the conference room. Roof: the helipad (raised 0.4 m, its H, edge lights, the helicopter), air handlers, tanks, solar panels, the aerial, the big sign facing the plaza.
- Outside: the emergency wing x 32…58 (resuscitation, three isolation rooms behind glass, bays with curtains, the nurse station island, triage), its roof a terrace level with 2F (skylights, planters, condensers, a tank); the canopy over the ambulance bay; one ambulance stands half out from under it so zombies jump from its roof (2.7) onto the canopy (4.2: 1.5 m, beyond people's 1.45). The parking tower x −72…−42 (P1 0, P2 4.2, P3 8.4): ramp 1 (21 steps of 0.2) up north on the west lane, ramp 2 up south on the east lane, a wall between, stepped side walls, slots railed; a stairwell in the north-east corner; carY() = car() on a deck. The skybridge (glass walls, pass) at 2F over the service road. The plaza: the canopy, the loop round the fountain island (the sunrise sculpture, the name on a low wall), the bus stop; the car park (stalls, a burnt car); the checkpoint (two tents, sandbags, barriers, the truck, floodlights, the burning car at the shut gate: MAP.fires, MAP.smoke); the healing garden (pond, pergola, pavilion on a 0.8 m floor); the service yard (the dock and a box truck, the oxygen tank in its fence, generators, bins). Brick walls 3.2 m with a fence on top (hoFence, coarse); the city outside (hoApt blocks, shops).
- Spawns: 16 in the lobby; 27 zombie entries over every level (yard, garden, car park, P1–P3, ER, every corridor, the roof, the ER roof): the zombie modes draw everyone's start from both lists. 18 camps (helipad, roof west, theatres, ward, nurse station, ICU, CT, canopy, ER roof, ER station, pharmacy, store, P3, P2, skybridge, pavilion, dock, tents).
- map.js: the round props' light (drums, trees, tanks) is cached per corner (the cylinders are not indexed: each corner was lit up to six times): every map's load is shorter, the result is the same.
- Tests: stats (2990 boxes, 438k vertices, 173 lights, 19.3k nav nodes, loads in ~4.7 s here (rest stop 2.7)), every area reachable from the lobby by people and zombies (an unpruned flood: no sealed floor left), every spawn / entry / camp on a reachable node, the zombie shortcut (bay → ER roof 30 m for zombies, 84 for people), views of every area, the radar's four bands; sims: Mutation 15 bots (round 1 the zombies win with 31 s left, nobody stuck, ~7 ms a frame), Original 15 bots (2:2 over four rounds), Mutation 7 bots, TDM / FFA / Arms Race (kills, nobody standing still, no errors). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.55 — JANUS-2 (janus2.js, built right after janus.js)
- Made to the owner's design sheet (a JSON from the gun-design prompt, its id written as janus1, renamed janus2): parts, points, colours, numbers, motions, sounds as written. Changes kept small and named in janus2.js's head: the figure of Christ (12 × 18 × 10 mm) and its eyes moved 7 mm back so they stand out of the sight's back face (the sheet buried them in it, the face flush with the back: z-fighting), a 5 cm bridge under the sight (it floated 2.5 cm over the frame), the inner gold barrel 162 mm (sheet 160) with a bore so its mouth no longer shares the shroud's face. Mary (only named in the sheet) is ours: blue veil, face, robe, folded hands, lit eyes, seven gold rays.
- Atlas: j2Blue, j2Gold (brushed, the sheet's three tones), j2Green (grain), j2Skin, j2Glow (self-lit, 232); janus_engraved is realguns.js's deGold. The atlas is full now: 128 of 128 slots (GA 1024 × 2048, 128 px). The next material needs more rows (GA.H 4096 keeps it a power of two) and every slot check (GA_SLOTS) follows from GA.H.
- FS_VM: a texel with alpha under .97 (≤ 247) is drawn at least at its own colour (self-lit) in the first-person view, whatever realguns.js's comment says about 240…254: a sheen that should not glow in the view model needs 248…254 (FS_CHAR reads (a − 239) / 15 as gloss on world models). j2Blue 248, j2Gold 251, the rest matte.
- First-person model (GUNS.janus2): tag 'mag' = what breaks (inner barrel, bore, bridge, sight, its gold strip) about G.hinge [0,.02,−.07] (the sheet's pivot); the outer barrel, the engraved strip under it and the rail are halved into j2L / j2R; four thorns thR1/2, thL1/2 (pivot = their centre); j2J (Christ), j2E (eyes), j2M (Mary), shell. j2Rig (a buildVM hook) hangs all of those under the 'mag' group (their positions made relative to it) so they turn with the break and still move on their own in the form; base positions are taken after that (VM.set). World models janus2n (= janus2s) / janus2b (the form baked: halves ±1 cm, thorns turned +0.5 rad about z through their centre with j2Turn — a quaternion so a thorn's own rx survives — Mary for Christ).
- One-handed (the sheet's sup / leftHand null): buildVM hides armL for a gun with no sup; j2Rig gives it a rest pose out of sight below, VMX.janus2 shows it only while a clip with a hand track runs (the reload). FDS.break and rlBrk are wrapped: a gun with no sup gets fdBreak1 (thumb latch, the barrel drops 45°, rolled over for a look (roll), a flick of the wrist shuts it on the 'mag' spring with the gun's shut sound) and rlBrk1 (the same, plus the extractor throwing the case back along the bore — 'shell' pz / py / rx / s keys — the free hand bringing a grenade (FD_PROPS.j2nade) from the belt to the chamber's mouth riding the barrel, the case sliding back in). GUN_DESIGN.janus2 carries the sheet's first draw plus open .785 and its sounds (opS / shut).
- janus.js generalised: JNW[id] holds what differs between JANUS weapons (the form's round wb, sOpen / sOn / sClose, the open / close clips, burst, the light lit / spark, the view model's glow mz, the crosshair class cx, bfx / sfx / cfx); WPN[id].jn = the id ('janus1' now a string; anything that tested it for truth still works). JNP[kind] = whose grenade, speed, model (jnProj builds any of them, the nyProj hook takes any JNP kind). jnHeld(a) replaces a.cur === 'janus1'; a.jnLeft is per id ([mag, spares]); a.jnWid = the JANUS weapon the actor's charge belongs to — another one in the pistol slot clears it (and shuts a form). The bot opener waits out a reload.
- Ammo: res 0 as the sheet says. A supply crate (SUP.give hook) gives back what is missing as spares (never past 5 in all), an ammo box (EPK.give) half of the magazine, the range tops it up; the reload (W.brk, 1 s) loads them. The buy menu's ammo / buying it again do nothing (supOnly, as JANUS-1); the chapter's 탄약 가득 reward and the resets fill it directly.
- Blast: detonate 'gl' takes n.sp, the share left at the edge (default .35 = the old .35 + .65 f): JANUS-2 .5 (the sheet's splash ×0.5) with dmg 80 (× 2.5 in the chapters, as JANUS-1), R 4.4, kb .5, stag .35, up .8. The sheet's "human 20" has nothing to hit (no friendly fire, the JANUS guns are not in PVP.GUNS).
- HUD: the jn / jnb crosshair and the ring gain the weapon's cx class: j2 = gold charge and signal, the form #8A2BE2. Sounds j2fire (deep 펑 + crack, a 0.8 s tail), j2fireb, j2open (철깍), j2shut (텅), j2draw (웅- + 철컥, W.drawS), j2awake (우웅-, the form opening), j2end; jnsig / jncyc shared.
- Shop: sidearm decoder only (SHOP_NOTSOLD, gun_prices janus2 0 / not sold); side_lines swaps one coins:1000 for gun:janus2 — schema.sql changes the column default, the config row if it is still the v6.36 list, and every open card's first coins:1000 line not done yet (the v6.13 Salamander pattern, placed after side_boards is created). Checked on a local Postgres 16: an old database with an open card (one coins:1000 line done) → the other one became gun:janus2, the config and a new card carry it, a second run changes nothing; a fresh database too.
- Tests: the model from every side (normal and form), the view model's idle, first draw, reload (case out, hand in, flick), the form opening / on / closing, third person in hand; unit (blast 80 → 40.9 at 4.3 m, nothing at 4.5; five counted blasts → signal; the form 8 shots in 2 s, nothing spent; 5 then dry; supply → spares → reload 5/0; ammo box +3; buy menu ammo full / in hand own; taken again 2/1; JANUS-1 beside it: charge, form, its own rounds, the crosshair without j2); bot sims Mutation 240 s / Original 200 s with JANUS-2 (and JANUS-1) in every hand: no errors; pvp/reg.py.

## v6.55.1 — JANUS-2 shown as 「이태훈 딕건」
- The owner's word: WPN.janus2.n = ['이태훈 딕건', 'Lee Tae-hoon Dick Gun'] (the buy menu, the kill feed, the decoder's cards and the admin page read it), the new-content banner and the lobby's news line follow. Only the name: the ids (janus2, j2*), the sidearm card's gun:janus2 line and the gun_prices row stay, so no schema change. The v6.55 notes keep the old name; v6.55.1's says it changed.

## v6.56 — three crate weapons of our own: SENTINEL · Q7 VACCINE LAUNCHER · BLOODHOUND (sentinel.js, q7vac.js, bhound.js, built after janus2.js)
- The owner asked for original supply-crate weapons "like Event Horizon", each with a clear idea and a clear merit; of the concepts offered he kept 1, 2 and 4: a machine gun that turns into a sentry, a vaccine that cures zombies back to the human side, a sniper rifle that marks. All three are crate guns (WPN.crate, CRATE_GUNS, the buy-menu category for an account that owns one, lent in the range, never in 대인전), SHOP_NOTSOLD and gun_prices rows (0, not sold) like ak60r / dmp7. Supply crate: Event Horizon 30 %, crate guns 35 % (from 30), heavy 15 % (from 20), kit 20 %.
- Atlas full (128 / 128): only existing materials. The view model's FS_VM reads unsaturated light texels as bare metal (a sheen, then the flashlight) — 'white' on a big surface blows out in first person, so the vaccine gun's body is sl_medic (pale teal) and its stock mtP; white stays on the vial's cap.
- SENTINEL (sentinel.js): WPN 150 / 300, 33 × 720/min, belt reload (rlKind hook → 'belt', GUN_DESIGN act belt). Right click (W.alt 'sentry', nyAlt hook) → sntDeploy: a spot ahead (sntSpot: floor within ±0.6 m, room for it), a.stOut = true, the gun stays in inv[1] (drops, loadouts, round keep and crates work as for any gun), the sidearm comes out (bestWeapon skips a sentry that is out). The sentry (SNT.list): GUNS.sntBase (tripod, turntable, battery box) + sntHead (the gun without folded legs) on a yaw group (SNN.H) and a pitch group (pivot SNT_PIV = the box's bottom), red eye sntEyeR while on a target. SNN: 30 a round (×2 head), 600/min, kb 3.4, stag .25, 24 m, turns 5 / 3 rad/s, fires within .14 rad, 45 s watch, .7 s to set up, .55 to fold. It feeds on a.ammo.sentinel (box, then spares; a supply kit refills it while it stands).
- Calling back: equip(sentinel) while it is out (1, the wheel, Q) → sntRecall (folds, then equips); a bot's own wish to switch back is ignored (its sentry returns by itself and the bot takes it then). It folds and returns on empty / after the watch; it is removed with its owner (fallen, turned, gone) or when inv[1] changes; NY.clear removes all. A page that takes the owner over (host moved, a player became a bot) puts the gun back in his hands.
- Network: the owner's page aims and deals the damage (sntShot → damageActor: a client's hits are claims as any shot); every other page runs the same aiming as a ghost (look and sound, NET.ghost) from ['st', id, 1, x, y, z, yaw] / ['st', id, 0] (netFxPush; relayed by the host as the actor's own fx). HUD: .sntMk over the player's own sentry (rounds · seconds; red while on a target). Bots: a bot holding it sets it down when its seen zombie is 5–26 m away (4 s apart). Sounds sentfire (gunshot GUNDEF.sentinel), sntdep, sntfold, sntlock, sntempty.
- Q7 VACCINE LAUNCHER (q7vac.js): WPN 5 / 15 darts, 90 (×2 head), 66/min semi with a pump stroke (W.pump; GUN_DESIGN act pump, rl mag — the vial is the 'mag' tag), hitscan. The host / solo decides in a damageActor hook: a q7vac hit on a zombie that vaxCurable (Original / Mutation only, not a host, not reviving, hp ≤ 50 %) → vaxCure instead of damage; otherwise the damage and the burn (vaxSlow: maxSpeed × .55 for 2 s, netEv 'vxs' so a player zombie's own page slows too).
- vaxCure → vaxHuman (every page; clients through CLIH.cure under netWithEv): team TH, maxHp 100, hp 60, no armour, everything zombie cleared, inv = the loadout's own sidearm and melee (a.ldo[2] / [3] when they are plain guns of that slot, else p9 / knife) so the next round's loadout (ldCur falls back to a.ldo for an empty slot) is kept; setHull, ensureRig, equip; the player's VM and the infection tint; AI.onTeam; immunity a.vaxImm = 4 s (an infect hook ignores the host's own infections meanwhile). The curer: +3 score, scEarn 400, a.cures; Mutation morale +2 points (as a kill). Feed: a row of its own (the name in human colour) with a green cross (HUD.feed / renderFeed hooks; MINI.vax).
- The bio-scanner (vaxHud): while the player holds it in an infection mode, up to six zombies in sight within 40 m get .vaxMk: health %, '+ 치료' (green) when a dart would cure, '면역' on a host. FX: green burst / ring / light on a cure, green drips on a slowed zombie, a shimmer on the immune. Sounds vaxfire (valve hiss, thump, pump), vaxcure (chime over a chord), vaxhit.
- BLOODHOUND (bhound.js): WPN 5 / 20, 105 (×3 head), 50/min bolt, zoom 28 / 10 like the AWP, GUN_DESIGN act bolt. A damageActor hook on the host: a hit by it marks (hndMark: t.hndT = 10 s, t.hndBy, netEv 'hnd'); a marked zombie takes ×1.5 from any human (×1.25 a boss: ZBIG or the chapter's boss). killZombie hook: brought down by someone else while marked → the hunter's assist (+1 score, a.assists). Every human page: .hndMk (◆ distance, blinking in its last 2 s) over marked zombies whether or not they are in sight, RADAR.aware true for them, a red tint (updateVisual hook, over no other tint). A marked player zombie gets a note. NY.clear and a cure clear the marks. Sounds bhfire (GUNDEF.bhound), bhmark (sonar ping).
- Tests: models from every side, the view models (idle, the belt reload, the pump, the vial reload), the sentry set up in the world; unit — sentry: deploy, 24 hits in 3 s off the gun's rounds, recall with the rounds left, empty fold, the 45 s watch, removed on a new primary / death, right click, a kit while it stands, NY.clear, a bot deploying and not recalling; vaccine (Mutation and Original): 90 and the slow (4.75 → 2.61), the cure (human, 60 / 100, p9 + its own melee, 3.8 s immunity, feed row with the cross), immunity then infection after 4 s, a host only hurt, the scanner marks, a player zombie cured by a bot, no cure in the scenario; Bloodhound: 105 and a 10 s mark, a teammate's 100 → 150, radar and screen mark, gone after 10 s, the assist, a boss 125, NY.clear. Sims 420 s Mutation / Original with 12 bots carrying the three (sentries 11–12, cures 3, marks 24–36, assists, no errors); JANUS sim; reg.py (대인전 lists without them, footer v6.56); crate odds over 10 000 opens; schema on local Postgres 16 (old → new and fresh).

## v7.5 — a new original map, 은행나무 아파트 Ginkgo Apartments (mapapt.js new, build.py, lobby.js, newsban.js)
- The owner's word after v7.4: 「다음패치 ㄱㄱ」; of the options the owner chose 「새 오리지널 맵」 — made from scratch with no reference, for the zombie modes and PvP alike. Map id 'apt', after the hospital in build.py's order (so MAPLIST, and with it NET_VER, take it in automatically; no r bump). No pvp flag, no bomb sites: Evolved / Classic Infection, Team Deathmatch, Free-for-all, Gun Race.
- The idea: a Korean corridor-type apartment complex (복도식 아파트) on an autumn afternoon, emptied by the evacuation; a quarantine line closes the outer roads, which is where the zombies come from. Everything is painted on first load (bakeAptTex: ~70 textures; the sky with a skyline of blocks and the sun low in the south-west, u .153 — the sun MAP.moon (-.487, .53, .695), elevation 32°, so the tall blocks throw their shadows north-east, out of the garden). Bounds x -42..42, z -42..40; storeys of 3.6 m (slab .3, so 3.3 m of headroom — the trapper is 2.05 m tall); probes at -2.7, -1.2, .9, 2.4, 4.5, 6.2, 8.1, 9.8; minimap bands B1 / 1F / 2F / 3F (cuts -1.5, 3, 6.6).
- 101동 (north, x -30..30, z -32..-22): ten storeys, 2F and 3F real and the rest a shell painted with the corridor face (south) and the balconies (north), the end walls with the number and a ginkgo badge. 1F is pilotis — columns, bikes, two parked cars, eight ceiling lamps — with a stair core at each end (switchback, landings at 1.8 / 3.6 / 5.4 / 7.2; the 1F and 2F flights solid, the ones above thin steps so the flight under them keeps 3.28 m; the way on up is blocked by furniture on the 3F landing) and the lift lobby in the middle (a see-through glass front with a door each side, mailboxes, lift doors, a back door north onto the outer road, an 18-step stair down to the car park). The open corridors of 2F and 3F run the whole 60 m between the cores (a 1.1 m parapet, a lamp every second flat, the evacuation banner on 3F). Locked flats are solid boxes with the door and window painted on; two 2F flats (x -13.5..-3) are open and knocked into one (the wall between them holed at z -30.4..-28.6 with rubble; the west one keeps no TV stand in front of the hole) and one 3F flat (x 8.25..13.5) is open: a door from the corridor, a small window, kitchen, fridge, bathroom box, table, sofa, the sliding-door frame and the balcony opening north (a sill at .95 m). A passage under the corridor front and the north internal road joins the lobby stair to the car park. The facade repainting was half done when the evacuation came: in a scaffold at the north-east corner of the garden (x 15.3..17.9, z -22..-16; tubes, a green safety net you see and shoot through, a works sign) a builders' stair (18 steps) climbs from the corner of the roads to a gap in the 2F parapet — the third way up to 2F, in the middle of the block.
- The arcade (상가, east, x 20..32, z -12.25..10): brick, three shops below with see-through windows and their signs (은행나무 마트 · 바삭 치킨 · 깨끗한 세탁; names made up) — the mart with shelves, a fridge, the counter and a storeroom, the chicken shop with tables and a fryer (steam), the laundry with machines and the clothes rail; 2F a hallway along the open west windows, the taekwondo studio (용 태권도: mats, a mirror wall, punching bags) and the study room (새싹 공부방: desks); a stair hall in the north-west corner (9-step flights, 1F → 2F → the roof through a hut); the green roof with a water tank, air conditioners, a dish, a 1 m parapet; an 18-step steel fire escape at the back from the alley up to the studio's back door (it runs north from the landing, clear of the chicken shop's back door — it first ran south and stood right in front of it). Behind it the back alley (dumpsters, drums, pallets, tyres, a scooter) and the site wall east.
- The garden (x -24..14, z -16..22) over the car park: grass with paver paths, 16 ginkgo trees (a trunk, an oval crown of five ragged tiers — alpha-tested gaps — and two clumps, no collision; leaves drifted round the foot), the playground (a play tower: deck A at 1.2 m up a stair from the south, deck B at 2.4 m up a short flight from A, a climbing ramp down to the east, a roof on posts; swings; a sandbox), the wisteria pergola with benches, the exercise corner, hedges along the north edge, benches, lamp posts (off: it is afternoon). The glass kiosk (x 2..6, z -12..-6) holds the car-park stair (18 steps; doors north, west and east).
- The car park (B1, y -3.6, x -24..8, z -12..16): green floor, bays, pillars with B1 signs, eight strip lights, seven cars (one burnt, one left in the aisle), a puddle; three ways in — the ramp from the west lot down a trench (28 steps of .5 m, 1 m parapets, a height bar with the sign) and a tunnel east, the kiosk stair, the lobby stair and passage. Every opening goes the full height to the ceiling slab: a box reaching below -.99 gets no bottom face (map.js skips them as ground), so a lintel there would show the sky from below.
- The west strip: the recycling shed (bins in four colours), the senior centre (은행나무 경로당: brick, a door east under a canopy, low tables, cushions, a TV, a shoe rack), the site wall; beyond it 102동 (36 m, its balcony face towards us, far enough west that its shadow takes only the north-west corner).
- Outside: the north road behind 101동 closed by concrete barriers with a fence on the zombies' face (four gaps, sandbags, cones, a 격리구역 Q-7 sign, an army truck) — the fence first stood in the middle of the barriers, and the ledge in front of it let a zombie hop onto the barrier and over the fence (1.6 m from the ledge); the complex wall (brick and a fence on its outer face) with the gate (an arch with the complex name, a raised boom) and the guard booth; the south road with a checkpoint line (three gaps), a parked car, a floodlight; tower blocks round about, low blocks south (kept low for the shadows), far ground in big cells (cs 10).
- Sides: the humans start in the middle of the garden (16 points facing north); the zombies on the north road behind the line (6), on the south road behind the checkpoint (5), in the car park (1), the north-east lot and the alley (2), the west lot by the ramp and behind the senior centre (2). Camps: flat2 (.2), corr2 (.12), corr3 (.14), flat3 (.12), the arcade roof (.18), the studio (.1), the study room, the mart, the play tower, the pergola, the car park, the lobby, the senior centre, the booth.
- Glass (texture apGlassT) is alpha-tested — a frame and two streaks, the rest clear — and pass: seen and shot through, solid to walk into (the lobby front, the kiosk, the shop and booth windows, the senior centre). Faces that came out coplanar in the first build were moved apart: the arcade's roof slabs now sit inside its walls (their edges shared the walls' outer faces), the storeroom wall stops under the 2F slab, the passage walls stop at the car park's wall, the shed's back posts went, the play tower's posts sit on its deck's corners.
- Nav (1 m cells at the half-metres): stairs start and end on whole metres (a flight starting .1 from a cell centre clips the small clearance box of the floor cell before it, and the landing loses its link — the cores first had flights from z -29.4 and nothing above 1.8 m survived the prune); flights are at most .667 m rise per metre (with 1 m cells a steeper flight samples steps more than .9 apart); doors are 1.2 m or wider at the half-metres; the arcade sits a step further north (z -12.25) so the stair hall's landing keeps a row of cells clear of the wall and the divider.
- Headroom: every opening is 2.2 m or more (the trapper is 2.05); the hole through the knocked-through wall is 2.6 m, since the rubble in it stands .32 m (2.1 left 1.78 over it). Nodes with less than 2.15 m over them are only car roofs under the pilotis and in the car park, a crate and a desk.
- Checks: 7,957 nav nodes, 7,941 reachable on foot from the first human spawn (2F 463, 3F 356, B1 975); every spawn, zombie spawn and camp point has a node and is reachable (humans with the 1.45 m cap); every zombie spawn has a path to the humans' start; no node above 1.6 m outside 101동, the arcade and the play tower, none below -.5 outside the car park, the ramp, the passage and the stairs.
- Tests (one page each — no multiplayer here, the owner tests that): smoke runs — Classic and Evolved Infection 240 s, team deathmatch 180 s (34–48, 82 kills), free-for-all and Gun Race 150 s (83 and 82 kills): no errors. Bot matches, 240–900 s (rounds humans–zombies): without the builders' stair Classic Infection 0–2 · 2–1 · 3–4 · 1–4 and Evolved Infection 1–0 · 2–1 · 2–3 · 4–3 (15–18 in all), with it Classic 1–4 · 3–4 and Evolved 3–2 · 4–2 (11–12) — about even either way, where the bots favour the zombies on the other maps (v7.4: Whitehill 1–4 and 0–4, Red Mesa Ranch 0–4 and 2–2). Instrumented (where the humans are every 2 s, where they turn or die; scratchpad q105/ana.js): no hold decides it — the bots kite on the roads and in the garden most of the time (50–73 %) and fall all over the map; with the stair they spend more of it up on 101동 (14–25 % against 8–10 %) and fare no worse, so it stays. Screenshots: the overview, the garden, the 2F corridor, the flats, the pilotis, the lobby, the car park and its tunnel, the ramp, the kiosk, the arcade front and its studio, study room, roof and fire escape, the play tower, the pergola, the senior centre, the gate from outside and the booth, the north road, the alley, the scaffold from both ends. The patch notes, the banner card and the notice panel read back in the page (Classic / Evolved Infection, team deathmatch, free-for-all and Gun Race list the map; Bomb Defusal does not). From a fresh page the map loads in 4.0 s (Whitehill 4.4, the hospital 5.5, the rest stop 2.7) with 179k triangles (the rest stop 204k, the hospital 146k, Whitehill 112k): the first build had 263k, so the big outdoor surfaces, the ginkgo crowns, the brick, the car-park walls and map-own aliases of the shared barrier and fence (apBarrier, apFence) bake their light in 1 m cells (the fence 2 m) instead of .5 m.

## v7.4 — Whitehill and Red Mesa Ranch: new ways through, the sides swap ends (mapit.js, mapmil.js, mapmil_data.js, net.js, lobby.js, newsban.js)
- The owner's word after v7.3: 「밀리샤랑 이탈리아 맵은 좀 더 변형이 필요할듯」. Asked how far, the owner chose 「동선까지 크게 (추천)」: three or four key areas rebuilt, ways blocked and new ones opened, the spawns moved — different enough that someone who knows the originals would take them for other maps. Everything below is in the maps' own coordinates (before the mirror, so "west" here is east in the game).
- 붉은 메사 목장 Red Mesa Ranch, the grid (mapmil_data.js): edited by a one-off script (scratchpad q103/mil_edit.py on milgen.py, which first rebuilt the generated file byte for byte from the grid): the culvert's tail → rock; every channel cell west of x -17.5 → dirt (the west channels, the culvert, the wash tunnel's trench); two gaps through the rock ridge between the west side and the canyons (x -13.2..-9.3 z -6.1..-2.9 and x -12.2..-8.8 z 5.9..9.1) → dirt; the south channel (z 24..27.4) → dirt. One channel is left (the east one, x 1.25..4.25), with a stair up at each end (z 3.75 → .25 to its head, z 20.75 → 24.25 to the filled south strip).
- Red Mesa Ranch, mapmil.js: gone with them — the culvert, the west water decals, the wash tunnel (portals, lamp), the timber cribbing, the channel-edge fence and its sign, the west and south channel lamps, the fence at x -28.1; the north deck and its outside stair, landing, posts, rails and lamp, so the two upstairs doors onto it (north wall x 12..13.8, east wall z -12.2..-10.4) are windows now. The garage: the east wall's window is gone and its parapet split for a 12-step plank stair from the yard to the roof (x 23.2..24.5, z 12.6 → 7.6, a rail on the open side; the sandbag moved to z 10.4..12.4). Props moved off the new ways (yard crates, one work lamp, the hay wagon). A lamp on the rock over the south alley. milBarn(): a 6.5 × 10 m barn on the west plateau against the rock (x -38..-31.5, z -14..-4), eaves 5.2 m, a gable roof in four steps with barn-red gables (new textures milBarn, milHay), a double door east onto the plateau, small doors north and south, a hayloft at 3.2 m over the north half (z -14..-9) up an 11-step stair along the west wall (z -5 → -9) with a rail along the loft's edge, hay bales, three lamps; camps 'loft' (w .25) and 'barn' (w .1). No opening faces the rock and the loft's one window looks east, so nothing in the barn reaches the rock ledges. The stair first started at z -4.6: its first step clipped the nav cell in front of it (the small clearance box of a floor cell), the stair had no link to the floor and the loft's camp was dropped; it starts at -5 now, and the hay bale that stood inside the south door moved to the south-east corner.
- Red Mesa Ranch, sides: the humans start on the west plateau round the barn (20 points, x -38..-28, z -2.2..5.5, facing east into the canyons), the zombies on the house side (16 points: the yard, round the house, the canyon heads, the channel). Camps: deck, culvert and tunnel gone, chanE down to two points, loft and barn new.
- 하얀 언덕 마을 Whitehill (mapit.js, ITALY_DATA untouched), itEdits: (1) the fork: the wedge house between the two stair lanes comes down row by row — IT_WEDGE holds, for each .25 m row from z 19.25 to 30, the x run of the wedge and the lanes' floor beside it (worked out offline from the boxes: the run of cells with anything more than .3 m over their own floor that holds x -9.25; the floor is the mean of the two lane cells beside it, in sixteenths). Each row: boxCut from -2.25 up to 9 over the run, then a pavers floor box up to the lanes' height, so the square is one slope with the lanes (steps of 6 cm at most). The first row's opened faces take facA (the houses' new south front at z 19.25), the others stoneIt. The wedge's south eave left floating at z 30..30.25 is cut too. A monument (a two-step plinth, a column, a capital; people can stand on the plinth), two planters against the new front, two lamp posts at the bottom. The v7.3 underpass is gone with the wedge (its code removed). (2)–(4) the v7.3 fountain and stalls, the courtyard fountain and trees and the terrace planters stay. (5) an arched passage from the dead end at the top of the middle street (x -4.5..-.5, z 2..5.75, ceiling 2–2.25 m) north to the cross street at z -19: a tunnel x -3.5..-.5 z -6..2.25, a well court x -6.5..-.5 z -10..-6 open to the sky (cut to 16.5 m, the shaft walls facA), a tunnel x -6.5..-3.5 z -19..-10 — offset, so no sight line runs from one end to the other. Tunnels 2.6 m high, stoneIt walls, woodFl ceiling, cobble floor, two lamps; in the court a stone well with water and a wooden frame, a wall lamp; camp 'well' (w .08). The nook's back wall is a solid 25 cm thick at z 2..2.25: cut only to 2.0 the passage had no nav link into the nook (A* went round by the west street), cut to 2.25 it joins. (6) a wall across the lane from the middle street to the east courtyard (x 4..4.5, z 9.5..12.25, 7 m, a door on each face that does not open, a lamp): the courtyard is reached from the market side only. (7) the sides change ends: the humans start on the north terrace (16 points at z -45.9 and -43.4, y 3.69 / 3.5, clear of the planters, facing south into the town), the zombies come from the south (16: the low street 5, the west walkway, the square, the west street 2, the middle street 2, the east courtyard, the far-west streets 2, the market street, the cross street).
- Whitehill textures: woodIt and doorIt are painted now (bakeItTex) — woodIt never was, so the v7.3 stalls' counters and posts rendered plain white.
- Net: NET_VER r20 → r21 (the geometry of both maps changed).
- Nav (mirrored, as played; nodes / reachable on foot from the first human spawn): Red Mesa Ranch 2,607 / 2,577 → 2,564 / 2,560, Whitehill 2,310 / 2,101 → 2,389 / 2,180. Every spawn and camp point has a node (Whitehill's hi4 / hi9 on the 6.5 m roofs zombie-only as before, hi7 / hi8 dropped by campsToNav as before); every zombie spawn has a path to the humans' start. Above 4.4 m nothing new on either map, compared node for node with the v7.3 build (both maps, mirrored and not): the ranch keeps only the two sandbag tops on the garage roof's parapet, the barn's roof and the rock ledges stay out of reach. Whitehill, unmirrored: from the low street to the terrace the way runs through the new passage (117 nodes; 131 round by the west street), and the two ends of the walled lane are 127 nodes apart.
- Tests (one page each — no multiplayer here, the owner tests that): bot matches of 600 s, the v7.3 build against this one (rounds humans–zombies): Classic Infection Whitehill 0–4 → 1–4, Red Mesa Ranch 1–4 → 0–4; Evolved Infection Whitehill 1–3 → 0–4, Red Mesa Ranch 1–2 → 2–2 — the bots favour the zombies on both maps before and after, nothing turned into a fortress for either side. Smoke runs on this build (Classic and Evolved Infection on both maps, team deathmatch on both — 42–44 and 48–52 kills —, free-for-all on the ranch, Gun Race on Whitehill, chapter 1): no errors. Screenshots: the square from below and above, the passage, the well court, the walled lane, the terrace; the barn outside and in, the loft, the gaps through the ridge, the south alley, the garage stair. The patch notes, the banner card and the notice panel read back in the page.

## v7.3.1 — the StarTrack with the shotguns (startrack.js, lobby.js)
- The owner's word: 「스타트랙 샷건칸으로 옮겨줘」. WPN.startrack.cat 'special' → 'shotgun' and its BUY_MENU row with it: the shop's 산탄총 tab and the buy menu's shotgun column (9th of 10: after the Jackhammer, before the SPAS-12 Dragon's Breath, which is added later in the build), no longer under 특수 · 근접. Kind, stats, crate-only and ownership unchanged.

## v7.3 — no CSO / Nexon names; Whitehill and Red Mesa Ranch remade (specguns.js = csoguns.js renamed; map.js, mapit.js, mapmil.js, net.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, NOTES.md and the display names in 60-odd files, schema.sql comments only)
- The owner picked recommendation 3 (「3 ㄱㄱ」: clean CSO / Nexon names out before a public release). Asked about the two maps whose layout follows Valve's (cs_italy, cs_militia): 「이름 바꾸고 맵에 어느정도 변형을 주자」. Asked whether sound/round_start.mp3 (the owner's file, used for the 10-second countdown) came from the game: 「일단 그거는 패스」 — left as it is.
- Display names only: every WPN / ZCLASS / MAPDEFS id, the server's ids, saves, prices, owned guns and records stay. The map ids stay 'italy' and 'militia'. A one-off script (scratchpad q102/rename.py) rewrote source/src, source/admin/admin.js, source/pwa/manifest.webmanifest, the READMEs, supabase/tools/prices.py and the comments of supabase/schema.sql (no SQL code changed: running schema.sql again is not needed for this). Not touched: this file's history and supabase/tools/gen_schema.py (stale since v6.18, never run). Korean particles after a renamed name were fixed by the name's last sound (은/는, 이/가, 을/를, 과/와, 으로/로 …); English a / an before the new names; 근하신년 inside English strings became All-Clear.
- Weapons (KO / EN): knife 씰 나이프 → 전투 나이프 / Combat Knife; airb 에어 버스터 → 압축 공기포 / Air Cannon; balrog5 발록-5 → 이그니스-5 / IGNIS-5; balrog11 발록-XI → 이그니스-XI / IGNIS-XI; thunder 썬더볼트 → 아크라인 / Arcline; janus1 JANUS-1 → TWINFACE-1 (the form: TWINFACE; janus2's display name is the owner's and stays, its old tag JANUS-2 → TWINFACE-2); m4dk / m4dk2 M4A1 다크나이트(-2) → M4A1 오닉스(-2) / M4A1 Onyx(-2); thanatos9 THANATOS-9 → MORTA-9; skull9 스컬-9 → 그레이브-9 / GRAVE-9; salamander 샐러맨더 → 파이어웜 / Firewyrm; cbmav 크로우바 매버릭 → 쇼크 크로우바 / Shock Crowbar; killknife 나타나이프 → 정글 마체테 / Jungle Machete; ak60r AK-47 60R → AK-47 드럼 / AK-47 Drum; xbowa 크로스보우 어드밴스 → 관통 크로스보우 / Piercing Crossbow; volc 볼케이노 → 헥사 로터 / Hexa Rotor; bdc 흑룡포 → 흑염포 / Black Flame Cannon; rdc 적룡포 → 홍염포 / Red Flame Cannon (its dragon: 화염룡); ripper 리퍼 → 그라인더 / Grinder; gaebolg 게이볼그 → 나르왈 / Narwhal; xdz 혈적자 → 차크람 / Chakram; mdrill 매그넘 드릴 → 보어 드릴 / Bore Drill; mlaunch 매그넘 런처 → 슬러그 런처 / Slug Launcher; duckfoot 스페셜 덕 풋 건 → 덕풋 권총 / Duck-Foot Pistol; sterling 스털링 바요넷 → 스털링 L2A3 / Sterling L2A3. Kept: real guns' names, plain nouns (크로스보우, 카타나, 해머, 토마호크, 플라즈마 건), the game's own designs (블러드하운드, 센티넬, Q7 백신 발사기, 스타트랙, 이벤트 호라이즌, 크림슨 피스메이커, 블레이즈-8, 관짝 · 소방관 좀비, the chapter bosses).
- Zombies: runner 라이트 → 그림자 좀비 / Shade Zombie, brute 헤비 → 덫잡이 좀비 / Trapper Zombie, scream 부두 → 주술사 좀비 / Shaman Zombie (일반 좀비 stays). Modes: mut 뮤테이션 → 진화 감염 / Evolved Infection, orig 오리지널 → 클래식 감염 / Classic Infection, 좀비 시나리오 → 협동 작전 / Co-op Operation (the word 시나리오 on its own stays); English 'Arms Race' (Valve's name) → 'Gun Race' (무기 레이스 stays). The decoder series 근하신년 → 격리해제 / All-Clear and its letters 근 · 하 · 신 · 년 → 격 · 리 · 해 · 제 (NY_CH; counts are kept by index, so the letters held stay); 초월 → 각성 / awakening (fragments, exchange, the badge). The gore setting's 'Original' (blood) is not the mode and stays.
- Comments that said a weapon's name or numbers follow CSO now only describe the weapon; csoguns.js is specguns.js (build.py; CSO_MATS / paintCsoAtlas / csoProj / csoHud → SPG_MATS / paintSpgAtlas / spgProj / spgHud).
- Engine (map.js): MAPDEFS[id].mirror builds the map flipped east-west — mapMirrorX() after D.build(): boxes (x0/x1, px/nx faces swapped on a copy of o, as o objects are shared between boxes), lights, deco, fires / smoke / spray / dyn, spawns (yaw → −yaw), zspawns, camps, spawnYaw, bounds, the title camera (a proxy records position.set / lookAt and flips them). The sun / moon direction is NOT flipped (the sky texture's glow stays where the light comes from). boxCut(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,{fl,wall,ceil,keep}) splits every overlapping box into the pieces outside the region and re-surfaces the opened faces (a re-surfaced floor drops its top tint). mapFreeSpawns() moves spawns and camp points that an edit buried (rings of .5 m up to 4 m). campsToNav() (mirrored maps, after navPrune): a camp point with no node under it snaps to the nearest node within 2.5 m on its floor or is dropped — the mirror moves box edges across the 1 m sample points (edges on a sample point count on one side only), Whitehill's camp hi7 lost its node that way.
- Net: NET_VER r19 → r20 (the geometry of two maps changed; host and clients must match).
- 하얀 언덕 마을 Whitehill (mapit.js, the id 'italy'): mirror:1; whitewashed — the eight facade textures off-white with blue / teal / navy / green shutters, the per-box side tint from the reference paint keeps only half its lightness and no hue (wash()), flat roofs light grey, bloomThr .95; textures for water, leaves, bark, awnings and fruit painted again for the new props. itEdits() in ITALY_DATA's own coordinates (before the mirror): (1) an underpass through the wedge between the two stair lanes of the south fork, boxCut(-12.3,-1.3,23.9,-6.2,1.45,25.7) floor pavers, walls retW, ceiling ceilT, a lamp; (2) the start street (y −2.1): itFountain(-9.5,-2.1,37.2,2.1) and two stalls (itStall: fruit-crate counter, posts and an awning you walk under) at x −14..−12.4 z 42.2..44.6 and x −6.6..−5.05 z 31.2..33.4; (3) the east courtyard's kiosk (x 14.9..23.1, z 5.9..13.1, up to 4.8 m) cut down to paving, a fountain (r 1.8) at (19, 9.5) and two trees (itTree: bark trunk, leaves canopy not solid); (4) five stone planters (1.4 × 1 × 1.2 m, leaves on top) across the north terrace at z −45.6..−44.6, x −11, −4.5, 2.5, 9.5, 16. The CC BY credit for the geometry reference stays in the v6.0 patch note (now marked 「v7.3 변형」) and in mapit.js's header.
- 붉은 메사 목장 Red Mesa Ranch (mapmil.js, the id 'militia'; the house sign already read 붉은 메사 목장): mirror:1; dusk — texMilDusk (sun glow at azimuth u .07, row 180 = 20° up, indigo → violet → orange / rose at the horizon, the mesas in silhouette), MAP.moon (−.851, .342, .4) '#ffb882' i 1.2, env sun:1 with the sun disc, fog '#5e4450' .015, ambOut '#7a6474'. The garage: the roll-up door is down (a solid milShutter in the opening), the west wall is open 2.4..11.4 m wide under a lintel. milEdits() in MIL_DATA's coordinates: (1) a cleft through the rock between the east canyon and the east channel, boxCut(-2,-1.75,12.4,1.55,14,14.4) with six concrete steps (−1.7 → 0), two posts and a lamp; (2) a water tower in the east yard: a 4 × 4 m plank platform at 2 m (x 34..38, z −6..−2: whole metres, so the nav cells fall inside), rails with a gap where the stair's top steps run along its west edge, the tank on posts 2.3 m over the platform (walk under it), a 7-step stair, a lamp, camp 'tower'. At 4.2 m and touching the east cliff it put the canyon rim in reach (the nav grew 888 nodes on the rock ledges, humans included): 2 m up and 2 m off the cliff no jump from it reaches a ledge (5–6 m) or the tank's frame.
- Red Mesa Ranch, an old bug fixed on the way: exX / exZ built each outside wall whole, and wallX fills everything above a hole up to the wall's top, so a ground-floor door or window under an upstairs one (same x) left the upstairs one solid: the upstairs door onto the garage roof (south wall 17.2..19) and upstairs windows in the south and west walls never existed, and the garage roof had no nav (camp garRoof pointed at nothing). The walls are now built per storey (band(): FL..UP, UP..TOP with their own holes).
- Nav after the changes (nodes / reachable on foot from the first human spawn): Whitehill 2,324 → 2,310 / 2,106 → 2,101 (the fountain, stalls and planters take cells; the underpass adds a row); Red Mesa Ranch 2,416 → 2,607 / 2,285 → 2,575 (the garage roof, the tower, the cleft; nothing above 4.5 m but two sandbag tops on the garage roof's parapet). Every spawn and camp point has a node (Whitehill's hi4 / hi9 on the 6.5 m roofs were zombie-only before and still are).
- Tests (one page each, offline; no multiplayer test here — the owner's): the rename by a word-level review of the diff, a particle check over every new Korean name and a parse of every edited file; the maps by top-down nav renders, the reachability check above and screenshots of every change (and of the white town and the dusk sky from above); bot matches without page errors — 클래식 감염 on Whitehill (240 s) and Red Mesa (200 s), 진화 감염 on Red Mesa (240 s) and Whitehill (200 s), 팀 데스매치 on both (77 and 99 kills in 180 s), 개인전 on Red Mesa, 무기 레이스 on Whitehill, chapter 1; the lobby, the shop, the inventory (격 · 리 · 해 · 제 in the pixel font) and the news cards in Korean and English.

## v7.2 — bot AI: PvP bots counter-strafe, pre-aim corners and reload in cover; zombie-mode human bots kite along walls (ai.js, pvp.js, lobby.js)
- The owner picked 3 of the recommendations (「3 ㄱㄱ」: bots that cannot fight round walls). Measured headless against the v7.1 code: a test-only init script (not shipped) gives every other bot on each side the v7.1 methods (PVP.bot / botInit / botSpawn / botDie / botFight, AI.human / kite) through Object.create(PVP / AI) wrappers keyed on a.bot.old, the player dead for the match; 대인전 6 v 6 bots on Italy, Harbor, Q7 and Militia, each map twice with the halves swapped.
- PvP counter-strafing (PVP.botFight / botAim): `shoot` = B.cs and the reaction done, the line clear, rounds in, no draw / reload, 6 m or more, not pellets / melee, not in the pause between two long-range bursts. Then the strafe is .2 of full speed (under the 1.2 m/s that curSpread counts as moving: an M4A1 .005 → .04 rad), no hops, and within 1.4 × the gun's range the bot fights from where it stands instead of walking closer; botAim lets go of the trigger while its own movement (mvx/mvz, not knockback) is over 1.2 m/s or it is airborne, for guns whose moving spread is over 3 × the standing one, past 6 m. B.cs is rolled each life: PVP.CS by difficulty .15 / .55 / .85 / 1. The strafe loop now keeps the move's length (the slow sidestep) and, blocked, tries the other way in the same frame.
- Corners (AI.edge / edgeTo / lookAt / lookAhead, ai.js): along a path (every other node, 28 m, ≤ 14 line checks) the first node the eye cannot see at chest height (round the corner the path turns), else the farthest seen if 3 m or more. PvP bots on the move with nobody in sight look at a fresh (≤ 6 s, ≤ 45 m) last-known place in view, else the corner ahead (botLook, ~4 Hz); zombie-mode human bots walking with no target look at the corner ahead (lookAhead, ~4 Hz).
- Cover to reload (PVP.botCover / coverSpot): mag 0 with the enemy ≥ 12 m (or the sidearm empty / in hand), or ≤ 15 % past 10 m → a node 1.2–5 m off on this level, reachable (hreach), ≥ 4 edges, hidden from the enemy's eye at 1.15 and 1.62 m, path ≤ 7 steps; reload on the way and there (crouched), out .2–.8 s after the reload; given up when he is within 4 m, after 3 s, or with no path; one every 4–8 s. Rare (1–5 a match for 12 bots).
- Tried and left out: running for cover under 40 health mid-fight (3 in 4 were shot on the way: 87 deaths in that state over 4 matches) and holding the corner the target went round (as a rule, then only when hurt): without them the new bots did better (kills 66.3 % vs 63.1 %).
- Results, hard, 8 matches each (v7.1 / new): every bot an M4A1 — without the reload cover kills 519 / 1021 (new share 66.3 % ± 2.4), K/D .68 / 1.31, all 8 won; the shipped set (the rare reload cover in) 556 / 974 (63.7 % ± 2.4), K/D .72 / 1.29, all 8 won; the shipped set with the loadouts rolled as usual (shotguns and snipers do not counter-strafe) 613 / 881 (59.0 % ± 2.5), K/D .82 / 1.18, 7 of 8 won.
- Cost (12 bots, Italy, 125 s, all v7.1 vs all new): AI.update 1.2 → 1.5 s (~ +2.6 ms per second of play); the rest of the extra time in a bot-only match is the extra hits and kills (fireGun .56 → 1.38 ms a shot with its hit effects, the kill events), not the AI.
- Other modes run headless without errors: 개인전 (Italy), 폭파미션 (Harbor), 무기 레이스 (Q7), 팀 데스매치 on easy (Big Tree: 2 of 10 bots counter-strafing), chapters 1 and 9 with three allies, Original (Hospital).
- Zombie-mode kiting (AI.kite / kRoom): twelve headings round the way away, each walked in .6 m steps to 3.6 m (fits, floor ≤ .4 m down or up); score = the distance gained from the zombies kiteScan keeps (B.kzl; weights 1/d²; capped at 12 m) + .25 × room + .3 × along the way away + .3 for the last heading; a heading whose line passes nearer a zombie than now and within 1.3 + 1.2 × its half-width is out. Kept .18 s unless its first step is blocked. Before: straight away, else ±45°, ±90°, .6 m ahead only.
- Zombie-mode matches (Mutation, normal, 15 bots, the player a bot too and left out; Q7, Italy, Yeongdong Rest Stop, each twice with the halves swapped; 189 human-bot lives a side): time as a human 105.7 → 115.8 s, infected or killed 130 → 114; kite() with no way out (a zombie within 3 m) 13.7 % → 8.0 % of calls. Five matches of six better.
- Kiting micro-test (zkite, 8 maps × 25 spots, a node with 3–5 neighbours = a wall or a corner behind, a regular zombie 6–8 m off on the open side, the human with only a knife, the first claw hit within 8 s): caught 127 / 200 (v7.1) → 103 / 200; mean time to the first hit 5.42 → 6.02 s; kite() finding no way out with a zombie within 3 m 5.9 % → 1.4 % of calls. kite() costs ~.04 ms a call (v7.1 ~.01).

## v7.1 — spectator free camera (main.js, touch.js, minimap.js, ui.js, pvp.js, i18n.js, style.css, lobby.js)
- Main.fc = {x,y,z,yaw,pitch} while the free camera is on (null otherwise). Main.toggleFree() (V, the touch .tFree button) starts it from the current camera and needs specOn(); Main.specClick() (left click, the touch fire button) leaves it, else nextSpec(). Main.freeMove(dt) in playerCmd: WASD along the view incl. pitch, Space up, C / Ctrl down, Shift fast, the touch stick; each axis is tested with rayCast so walls stop it, clamped to MAP.bounds ±2 and y .4–90.
- Main.camera: in the dead branch, fc sets the camera directly (no death tint); fc is cleared when the player is alive, in the menu or no longer spectating. mousemove / TOUCH.turn turn fc while dead. The minimap centres on the camera (a prototype copy of the spectated actor with c / yaw replaced). hSpec text in ui.js and pvp.js (specFree / specFreeK / specFreeT, specHintT). Touch: .tFree is shown by #touch.dead.spc; the update bitmask has 16 = specOn, 32 = fc.
- Not in newsban (a small feature). Tests: headless dust tdm, dead then V, W / Space / D / Shift+S (stopped by a wall at x 29.5, held in bounds), click back, revive clears fc; phone: the button, turn, stick, fire button.

## v7.0 — season 2 completion rewards: the achievements 「시즌 2 완주」 I–III and 「1차 회수팀」, the hall of fame by season, the season bar over the chapter list, the results banner; chapters 6–10's first-clear titles fixed (epseason.js new; build order: epseason after ach; ach.js, hof.js, rank.js, episode.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, style.css, test/mock.js, schema.sql)
- The owner picked 2 of the recommendations (「2 ㄱㄱ」): rewards for finishing season 2 — titles by difficulty, and a season section in the hall of fame.
- Seasons are five chapters each: season 1 = chapters 1–5 (격리구역 Q-7: ALEPH, ARGUS, KHEPRI, LYREBIRD, TIAMAT), season 2 = 6–10 (1차 회수팀: LEGATUS, LORELEI, CHARON, DAEDALUS, ELPIS). Season s is chapters 5s−4 … 5s on both sides (the server's (ep + 4) / 5, the game's EPSZ.of); a season is "out" when all five of its chapters are (the server: qz_ep_max / 5; the game: EPS without soon).
- The achievements (schema.sql ach_defs, category 챕터; the server pays them, qz_ach_claim; the records so far count — a player who had already finished sees them ready):
  - 「시즌 2 완주」 (grp s2c, ord 101 — right after 챕터 정복): I s2c1 chapters 6–10 all cleared on any difficulty (stat s2a, goal 5) — 3,000 coins, 2 근하신년 decoders, the title rt2 「2차 회수팀」 / Recovery Team 2 (grade 3); II s2c2 all on hard or expert (s2h) — 6,000, 3, unyielding 「포기하지 않는 자」 / The Unyielding (4 — ELPIS's 「아무도 포기 안 해」); III s2c3 all on expert (s2x) — 10,000, 5, dawn 「Q-7의 새벽」 / Dawn of Q-7 (5, the pair of epx2's 「Q-7의 악몽」).
  - 「1차 회수팀」 (grp tags, ord 102): tags1 all seven of RT-1 brought down — chapters 2 (ARGUS, 07), 4 (LYREBIRD, 05), 6 (LEGATUS, 01), 7 (LORELEI, 03), 8 (CHARON, 06), 9 (DAEDALUS, 04), 10 (ELPIS, 02) on any difficulty (stat rt1, goal 7) — 4,000 coins, 3 decoders, dogtags 「일곱 개의 군번줄」 / Seven Dog Tags (4; chapter 10's last line: 「1차 회수팀 일곱 명, 전원 확인」).
  - qz_ach_stats reads s2a / s2h / s2x / rt1 off ep_records (distinct chapters with a clear: 6–10, on diff ≥ 2, on diff 3; 2, 4, 6–10). Each tier of 시즌 2 완주 reads a number of its own — the first ladder that does: the server always went by each row's stat (ready, hits, claim), the game's ACH.groups took the first tier's for all; now each tier keeps its own (d.v), the card shows the next tier's number and its pips light by their own (a page from before v7.0 shows tiers II–III by tier I's number until it updates: a claim it offers too early is answered not_done and the list reloads). ACH_GRP's d(n, tier) gets the tier so one ladder can say three things. The ids keep the results line's id → ladder rule (/^(.*?)(\d+)$/: s2c1 → s2c, tags1 → tags).
- The hall of fame (hof.js page, the ranking window): a tab a season, the latest first (시즌 2 · 1차 회수팀 · 6~10챕터 / 시즌 1 · 격리구역 Q-7 · 1~5챕터; EPSZ.hs keeps the pick, data-ra hsz). On top, the season's finishers: three columns — 난이도 무관, 어려움 이상, 전문가 — each with the first ten (a crown on the first, 2 and 3 in silver and bronze, the date), how many have finished and this account's place (underlined when it is in the ten); under it the season's titles. Then that season's chapters with their first-clear teams, as before (only that season's). Season 1's lists fill from the records so far. The page's line at the bottom says how a season counts.
  - qz_ep_seasons() (new; anon and authenticated, like qz_ep_firsts): for each season out and each level (0, 2, 3) the accounts whose five chapters all have a clear at that level or harder; when = the latest of the five chapters' earliest first_at at that level (the clear that made the five); suspended accounts left out (back in when the ban ends); n, the first ten (nickname, at), me (rank, at) for the account asking. One SQL statement off ep_records (no table of its own: nothing to keep in step, and the past counts). It is defined after qz_ep_max (a SQL function's body is checked when it is made: the first version, above it, failed on a fresh database).
- The season bar (epCards, room settings — 방 만들기, the first thing in the chapter list): S2 시즌 2 완주 · 1차 회수팀, the tiers I · II · III (gold when done, the next one outlined), the five chapters as squares in the colour of the hardest difficulty cleared (grey: not yet; a tooltip each), then the next tier's ask, its count (n/5) and its title chip; all three done: 「전문가까지 완주 ✔」 and the gold frame. Signed in it reads the server's records (ACC.me.eps — the ones the rewards read), a guest this browser's (EP.rec). It opens the achievements on their chapter page (UI.act szach; the news card's button too).
- The results (the claim's ach_hit): a match that reaches s2c1–3 or tags1 gets a banner under the hall of fame's (.szRes, the dawn icon or the dog tags): 「시즌 2 완주!」 (「어려움 이상으로 …」, 「전문가로 …」 for the highest tier reached), 「서버에서 N번째 완주」 once qz_ep_seasons has answered (asked right after the claim), 「1차 회수팀 일곱 명도 전원 확인」 when that came too, and the titles waiting in Achievements; the achievements line under the coins lists the tiers and has the claim button. The phone's sideways results put it in the left column with the other boxes.
- Fix (every page): a first-clear title of chapters 6–10 (codes 60–103: ep × 10 + difficulty) never showed — HOF.valid took 10–53 (chapters 1–5) and TTL.isHof two digits; the server had been giving them all along (qz_title_worn → 'hof:103', qz_firsts_of). Now 10–303 (chapters 1–30, as the records' check) and 2–3 digits: the lobby profile, the waiting room, name tags, chat, the scoreboard and results, the ranking, the friend list and the titles page all show them; the network's checks go through HOF.valid too (net.js, latejoin.js, chat.js), so the code rides to the other pages again (an older page still drops it — it only shows nothing).
- test/mock.js: the four rows, the four numbers, qz_ep_seasons (the same rules), the records' first clear (first) and time, eps in qz_me / qz_claim (the server's qz_eps shape) — and chapter claims up to 10 (they stopped at 7).
- Tests (one page, headless, the mock server; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it):
  - Postgres 16: fresh (stub → schema → schema again → the claim test), upgrade from v6.99's schema with records made on it (→ the checks → schema again → the claim test). The four rows (80 in all); the numbers of five accounts (a: 1–10 normal, 6–10 hard, 9–10 expert → s2a 5, s2h 5, s2x 2, rt1 7; b: all expert → 5 5 5 7; c: 1–9 → 4 0 0 6; d suspended; e: season 1 on easy → 0 0 0 2); ready tiers; s2c1 claimed (3,000 + 2 + rt2), s2c3 refused not_done, wearing rt2 then dawn refused no_title, claim all, wearing dogtags (worn as everyone sees it, qz_titles_of); the seasons as a guest (season 1: 4 finished, the suspended one left out; season 2: b then a on any and on hard, b alone on expert), a's places (2, 2, none), the suspended account back once the ban ends (first of all), then out again. The claim: an account with 1–9 on expert clears 10 on expert → ach_hit s2c1, s2c2, s2c3, tags1 at once, its places, claiming them, wearing dawn.
  - The game: the bar (4/5 on any with 6–8 hard and 9 normal; after 10: 「모두 어려움 이상 3/5」 with 「포기하지 않는 자」), its click (the achievements, 챕터); the hall of fame's two tabs with four other accounts in the mock (the lists, the counts, 「나: 3번째」 on season 1); the achievements' chapter page (시즌 2 완주 I 4/5, 1차 회수팀 I 6/7 with their rewards); a chapter 10 clear forced in a real match (EP.finish) → the claim → 「시즌 2 완주! 서버에서 5번째 완주 · 1차 회수팀 일곱 명도 전원 확인」, the achievements line (시즌 2 완주 I · 1차 회수팀 I · 명예의 전당 I); back in the lobby the automatic title is now the hall of fame's 「엘피스 보통 최초 클리어」 (hof:101 — before this patch: nothing); claim all, the titles page (the new ones owned), wearing 「2차 회수팀」 on the profile. 1280×720, 1100×620, a phone sideways (844×390, the results' left column) and upright (390×844, the lists one under another), Korean and English; no errors, no CSP reports.

## v6.99 — zombie scenario chapter 10 「엘피스」, season 2's finale (elpis.js, mapep10.js new; build order: mapep10 after mapep9, elpis after daedalus, before episode; design.js, game.js, scenario.js, episode.js, ai.js, nyw.js, net.js, map.js, minimap.js, daedalus.js, account.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, test/mock.js, schema.sql; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it)
- The owner's word: 「그럼 준 클라이맥스 급으로 고퀄리티로 만들어주고 그 이후는 추후에 푸는걸로 하자」 — chapter 10 as a near-climax, made with care; the bigger answers left for later (season 3).
- The story: chapter 9's last log — 「억제제가 듣지 않는다. 원액은 Q-7 중앙 연구소에만 있다.」 「혼자 간다. 04, 기다리지 마.」 Chapter 10 is that lab, at the season's first dawn (6–9 were nights of snow and storm): Quarantine Zone Q-7, where the series began. RT1-02 ELPIS (엘피스, Greek "hope" — what stayed behind in Pandora's jar), RT-1's medic, who carried the suppressant and gave it to the others. The feeling it held down: hope — she could not give anyone up. She came back alone for the pure stock, found the vault empty (a release record, the recipient erased), and has kept Q-7's infected "alive" in the B5 ward since, bringing them back again and again. Her logs on the atrium's terminals (voice3 「02의 기록」: Q-7 reached, the stock on B5 / the vault empty, the recipient erased / 「괜찮아. 내가 만들면 돼. 환자는 여기 많으니까.」); her voice on the elevator's PA in the blackout (「환자분들, 조금만 기다리세요. 곧 치료해 드릴게요.」); at the meeting 「다쳤어? 이리 와. 치료해 줄게.」; in phase 2 she talks to RT-1 as if they were there (01 … 07 — nobody answers: 「누구랑 말하는 거야…? 아무도 없잖아.」); phase 3 is the last vial, kept for 04 (「04, 미안. 이건 내가 쓸게.」); her death 「…괜찮아… 다들… 살 수 있어…」. The ending (left open for season 3): the recorder in her vest — 02's last log (twelve vials taken out the day before the lockdown, the recipient erased), one more line at the very end of the release record 「2차 회수팀 — 투여 완료. 관찰 기간 30일.」, RT-2's 「투여? 우리가 맞은 건 출동 전 예방 주사뿐인데.」, CONTROL's 「…기록 장치는 열지 말고 그대로 가져와라. 복귀하라.」 The season card: 「시즌 2 완결 · 1차 회수팀 일곱 명 — 모두 끝났다. 원액은 이미 Q-7을 떠났다. · 다음 이야기 — 시즌 3 · 준비 중」; chapter 9's card now names chapter 10 (「다음 이야기 — 10챕터 「엘피스」 · 시즌 2 최종장」).
- mapep10.js 「격리구역 Q-7 중앙 연구소」 (MAPDEFS.ep10, ep:10; the first dawn of the season — its own sky texture with the sun low in the east-north-east, fog #8a8496 .013 up top, north is −z; two levels: the surface at y 30 and B5 at y 0, joined by the freight elevator): A the decon checkpoint in the yard (x ±34, z 101..158: the army's decon lane, tents, barriers, the lab's front and its sign) → gate gA (the lab's glass doors, z 100.8) → B the atrium (x ±24, z 57..100.6: two storeys under a skylight; the reception desk, the mezzanine at 36 round three sides with two stairs — the control room on the west gallery, the elevator's machine room on the east —, 02's terminals; the freight elevator in the north wall, gate gB) → the shaft (x ±3.6, z 45.6..56.4; landings' lips every 6 m, the floors' signs B1–B5) → C B5's antechamber (x ±14, z 6..45.2: the decon showers, the nurses' station over the ward's window, the ward's doors gW in the west wall, the vault door gV north — a round door under the sterilization sign) → the ward (x −30..−14.4, z 8..42: her patients' beds with their drips, curtains, 02's corner with her notes and RT-1's board) and the service corridor (x −30..−22.4, z −28..8: red lamps, pipes; the incinerator's vents — two across the corridor, two in the ward, two across the antechamber — their grates in the floor) → D the stock vault (x ±22, z −44..2: the stock column in the middle — STOCK, its drawers all empty —, the catwalk at 5 along the north and east walls with a stair, cryo tanks along the north wall, beds, crates, RT-1's things on a table south of the column, the four UV banks' tubes in the ceiling and their wall switches, the supply lockers; the service door gS in its west wall). B5 has its own air: fog #0e1218 .024 whenever the camera is below 17 m (the chapter swaps it every frame, so the ride down and the ride up cross into it).
- Containment (the flood fill from chapter 9 — a 0.25 m grid of where a body 0.6 m wide fits, each zone's floor from its starts, gates shut): every level reaches only itself, and nothing stands in the shaft between the landings but the car. Found by the paths: the lab's door had no floor under its opening (no nav link from the yard into the atrium; the bots stood in the yard through stage 2's start) — a threshold now; two cryo tanks stood 0.86 m off the vault's north wall (a pocket a bot and the zombies were lost behind) — flush now.
- The chapter (CHAP[10], season 2, k 'elpis', code RT1-02; voice2 ELPIS, voice3 02's logs):
  - 1 waves ×3 in the yard (the Patient first, with the radio's line about it; expert's waves .62 the size) → the lab's glass doors open.
  - 2 'act' in the atrium: three overrides, any order, 10 s each (the reception's security console, the control room's server on the west gallery, the elevator's machine room on the east; the trickle .46, hard .42, expert .31, spaced 1.4×; the Hazmat first, with its line); 02's logs after the first two and the clear. Then the freight elevator: everyone in (40 s; the bots get a place each and one at its door is lifted in; the trickle .6 meanwhile, expert .45 — the car is a dead end), the ride down (24 s, EP.ph 'ride'): the gates shut (the car's boxes move with it: the riders are shut in and carried; another page's riders are drawn where its own car is), 9.5 s down to B3, the power cut there (3.5 s in the dark under a red lamp, her voice on the PA), backup power, on to B5; 5 s down RT-1's first-aid kit on its cart patches the riders up to 75.
  - 3 'hold' 75 s in B5's antechamber while the vault door runs its decontamination (the trickle .6, expert .44, spaced 1.3×; the AEDs' line when the first one lands). The first meeting at 10 s: the ward's doors open and ELPIS steps out onto the nurses' station (its lamp stutters on over her) — darts, an AED on the team's kills, the defibrillator; back into the ward at 85 % or after 35 s. From the meeting on the infected come out of the ward too.
  - 4 'boss' in the vault (prep: she kneels at RT-1's table, the vault door open; it shuts behind the team at the start).
  - 5 'escape' 75 s: the sterilization protocol — the vault door locks, the service door opens, the klaxon; her gas, mines and AEDs go with her and so does everything on the floor; 2.6 s in, her medic bag where she fell patches whoever is up to 60 (「그녀의 구급 가방이다 — 다들 써! 그리고 뛰어!」). Through the service corridor (its vents fire ahead of the team: one within 20 m of someone every 1.6–2.6 s — its grate glows and spits embers, an orange strip 1.4 s, then a sheet of fire up out of it 2.4 s: 14 a second), the ward and the antechamber to the car; after 25 s the vault burns behind them too (a ring 3.4 m on whoever is still in it, 1.5 s, then 4 s of fire: 16 a second). The trickle .6 (expert .45) from the ward's, the antechamber's and the vault's spawns, Patients and infected only, spaced 1.5×. The bots go a door at a time (the vault → the corridor's end → the ward's doors → a place in the car). Everybody alive in the car: the bonus, and the car takes them up into the dawn in the ending (the atrium's glass, 「우리는 내일을 연구합니다」).
- ELPIS (ZCLASS.elpis 22000 nominal; the chapter sets 28,000 × (0.6 + 0.4 × humans) × difficulty — expert 1.2, not the scenario's 1.4, and her own attacks there ×1.5, not ×1.73; a zombie rig in RT-1's grey-green fatigues under a medic's vest — three pockets, the white patch and green cross on its chest, RT1-02 on the pack on her back —, the white armband with its green cross, a stethoscope, the sky-blue surgical mask pulled down under her chin, her black hair up in a bun, blood-soaked nitrile gloves, a tourniquet on the vest's strap, the thigh pouch, knee pads; the stock's green veins under pale skin, green eyes and tear tracks; 1.1×; the dart pistol in her right hand, the paddles for the shock, the IV bag on her pack glowing with the stock, the dog tags in her left hand from phase 2, the vial; her own poses eased one into the next — aiming, throwing, the paddles out, kneeling, the dog tags to her chest, calling, the vial at her neck, singing, the lunge, down, blinded, getting up; the scalpel 24 / 32 heavy, 30 / 38 after the last vial):
  - phase 1 「응급 처치」 (100–65 %) round her ward's beds, cover to cover (10–16 m from the team, never in a burning quarter): sedative darts (a green strip 1.1 × 26 m, .7 s, three darts: 24 and dazed — knocked out cold if already dazed; every 6 s, enraged 4.5), the AED (thrown onto a body killed in the last 12 s within 20 m — not one finished cleanly; two at most; 3.2 s later it shocks the body back up, whole, green and 1.25× faster, unless it is shot apart first), the defibrillator up close (a ring 3.6 m, .7 s: 24 and knocked flat), anaesthetic gas (a canister lobbed 5–22 m: a pale green cloud 4 m for 7 s — 3.5 a second and slowed .65). Where two clouds or two patches of fire overlap they do not add up (one at a time), and the bots keep a step clear of their edges.
  - 65 %: the dog tags (3.5 s, invulnerable). Phase 2 「기억」 (65–30 %): every 7.5 s (enraged 5.5) she calls on one of RT-1 as if they were still with her, all six before any comes again — 07 ARGUS's laser (on the farthest one she sees, 1.5 s, then 36 if she still sees them), 01 LEGATUS's artillery (a ring 3 m on each of the team and one near their middle, 1.8 s: 30 and knocked flat — once each, however the rings overlap), 06 CHARON's flare (a ring 3.2 m, 1 s, then 6 s of fire: 9 a second — the infected burn in it too), 04 DAEDALUS's mines (three, chapter 9's), 03 LORELEI's song (2.6 s: everyone in sight within 24 m pulled to her — crouched, a third as hard — then the defibrillator for whoever is close), 05 LYREBIRD's fog (she slips away in it to a far cover point); darts and AEDs between.
  - 30 %: the last vial of the stock, kept for 04, into her own neck (2.8 s, invulnerable). Phase 3 「마지막 한 병」 (30–0 %): 1.3× pace, straight at them (to about 7 m): the lunge (a red strip 2 × up to 14 m along a clear line through one of them, .8 s, then 22 m/s: 30 and knocked flat; 1.2 s to get up, ×1.3 taken meanwhile), mass resuscitation every 20 s (enraged 15: she kneels and slams the floor — a green shockwave 22 m, jump it: 14 — and AEDs onto up to three bodies at once), a memory every 14 s, AEDs (three at most).
  - The UV banks (useNear 0–3: one over each quarter of the vault, its switch on the wall): hold E 3 s (two at it 1.25×) → its tubes burn 10 s, then 20 s to cool. Caught in it as it lights, she is blinded (2.5 s, +40 stagger) and takes ×1.25 while she stays in it (she leaves it); the infected in it burn (10 % of their health a second; an AED in it fizzles) and nothing in it can be brought back up. One bot has the UV duty (the quarter she stands in, ready: the bot nearest its switch); the rest stand round her at 11 m (13 in phase 3) out in the open — never against a wall, at the tanks, on a stair or behind the column. The trickle in the fight: .22 / .25 / .26 by phase (×1.4 enraged, ×.6 on expert); the bots count her as .4 of her distance (a zombie closer than that comes first). Ammo boxes out of the supply lockers (10 s in, then every 30 s wherever none is lying).
