  - Stagger (gauge 100, 1.25× after each down): the IV pack on her back (×1.3), HE +10, frost +10 (slowed 3 s), the UV +40; full: down 6 s (×1.5). Every phase change: invulnerable, the team back up. Enraged after 240 s (expert 180): cooldowns ×.7, 1.2× pace.
- The Patient (ZCLASS.patient: 1250, armour 10, 5.3 m/s; Q-7's patients from the ward, still on her drips — a pale blue gown open at the back with Q7-B5 on its chest, bare legs and feet, the wristband, a cannula taped into the arm with its cut line, a shaved, stitched scalp, the stock's green veins, green eyes; its head lolls): put down, it gets back up once, 3 s later at 45 % and green — unless it was finished with a headshot, a blast (HE, the grenade launcher) or fire, or in a burning UV quarter.
- The AED (ZCLASS.aed, an actor that never moves, like chapter 9's sentry: no physics, nobody shoves it, no blood, its own small hit box; 260 × difficulty; drawn where it lies with its leads to the body, its light and beep faster as it charges; the bots go for it first): shot apart, its body stays down for good (the radio's line, once).
- The Hazmat (chapter 5's researcher) here: its tank bursts into her gas (3.2 m, 5 s, 3.5 a second) instead of chapter 5's spores.
- Sounds (rendered at start like the rest): eldart (the dart pistol), elshock (the defibrillator charging and firing), eluv (the UV tubes striking), elklax (the sterilization klaxon).
- Network (host-authoritative): the snapshot's boss block (bsPack: phase, the stagger gauge, flags — 1 a memory, 2 down, 4 invulnerable, 8 enraged, 16 the first meeting, 32 the last vial taken, 64 blinded, 128 singing, 256 lunging, 512 getting up, 1024 hidden in the fog, 2048 throwing an AED, 4096 in a burning quarter —, her pose, the memory, the UV banks' states); events 'e10' (patterns, throws, gas, fire, the vents' warnings, the AEDs landing and firing, a body brought back up, the banks, the ride's first aid, the escape's clearing), 'e10r' (the ride: who was pulled into the car, the zombies gone); a client's shot on a mine goes to the host ('e10s'); the car's place on a client comes from the ride's clock. NET_VER '-r19' (three zombie classes added to ZALL).
- HUD: the boss bar's status line (invulnerable, down, blinded — now!, getting up — now!, her song — crouch, the memory's name, enraged) and its phase line (first contact; the triage, the memories, the last vial); markers: the car while boarding, the UV switches (hot when she is in that quarter) and a burning quarter's seconds, every AED (「⚡ 제세동기 — 쏴!」); the ride's progress; notes: sedated, the UV on, her song, the car's first aid.
- minimap.js / map.js (every map): a map may name its minimap's floors (MAPDEFS.miniName — the label had always said 1F or 2F): chapter 10's B5 / 1F / 2F.
- nyw.js / ai.js (every scenario): the Voodoo zombie's heal (20 % to every infected within 8 m) healed the scenario's boss too — a sim's ELPIS went from 4.7 % to 23.9 %, and every chapter's boss could have been healed the same way. It leaves a scenario's boss alone now (the skill and the bots'-side trigger).
- Balance (4-bot simulations, hand-stepped headless, one page; the "player" is a bot too and never buys: a pistol only): on the last builds the whole chapter on easy 6:28–6:59, three wins in three (all S); on normal 7:28–9:36, five in five (four S, one A); on hard 6:06–11:51, seven in seven (four S, one A, two B — one of them wiped in the escape before her medic bag, no bonus); on expert 6:13–12:06, four in five (one S, one A, two B; lost once, in phase 3), and six in six the round before (the bots' priority .45 then). Stage times on normal (each with its move): the yard 97–118 s, the atrium with the boarding 49–62, the ride 24, the antechamber 81–82 (its 75 s and the walk), the boss with its 12 s prep 2:28–4:25 (phase 1 34–59 s, phase 2 38–52, phase 3 44–143), the escape 29–36 (the car with 39–46 s to spare). Phase 3 is where the lives go on hard and expert (most of their wipes), as it should be. Drafts and what changed:
  - the first runs' boss bar jumped back up (4.7 % → 23.9 %): the Voodoo zombie's heal (above);
  - the bots stood in the yard at stage 2's start: no floor in the lab door's opening (above); a bot and the infected lost behind the cryo tanks (above);
  - the logic checks: the car went up the wrong way in the ending (its height upside down), mass resuscitation's wave came twice on the host, the elevator's top landing never opened (the stage's gates), the infected never came out of the ward (the escape and the meeting do not go through stageStart, where the spawns were set), the lunge's hit was wider than its strip, and LEGATUS's rings hit one teammate four times where they overlapped (dead from full) — once each now;
  - easy ran out of the escape's 75 s: the first vent stood in the service door and the vents blocked the whole corridor — shorter, re-placed, their grates in the floor; each of the escape's waypoints past the next leg's line; then a bot's place just past the service door snapped to the vault's side of it and it stood there 20 s — the first one is out in the corridor now;
  - hard lost one run in two, its wipes at the west stair's foot and by the north tanks: the bots' places round her had been clamped to the vault's edges — out in the open now;
  - expert lost its first twelve runs (the yard twice, the atrium once, the boarding's dead-end car once, the boss mostly — the infected did most of it, then ARGUS's shot at 62–73 a hit): the yard's waves .72 → .62, the boarding's trickle .45 and the atrium's .31, the hold's .44, the fight's trickle ×.6, her health 1.4 → 1.2 and her own attacks ×1.73 → ×1.5 on expert, ARGUS's shot 42 → 36 on every difficulty, fire and gas no longer adding up where they overlap (the bots had stood at their edges, in and out — they keep a step clear now) — ten wins in eleven since;
  - the bots counted her as .36 of her distance and let the infected at them while they shot her; at .45 hard lost two of four in phase 3 (her fight ran long and the mass resuscitations piled up) — .4, and phase 3's trickle .3 → .26;
  - a hard run came out of phase 3 on 9–54 hp and the infected wiped it in the ward (no bonus): her medic bag at the escape's start.
- The server (schema.sql, run again by the owner): public.qz_ep_max() = 10; the episode columns' checks (ep_records, ep_firsts) 1–9 → 1–30, room for the seasons to come; qz_ep_min row 10: 240 / 240 / 250 / 260 s. Four bots that could not be hurt and hit three times as hard cleared it in 5:38 easy, 5:20 normal, 5:56 hard, 5:45 expert, and most of that goes as fast for anyone — the yard's three waves, the 24 s ride, the 75 s hold, the preps, the run back to the car come to some 4:30 with her own invulnerable seconds — so the row is about 0.75 of the bots'. Until it is run, ACC.epSrv claims a chapter 10 match as the free scenario. test/mock.js: epmax 10.
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): the containment probe (above: no way out of any level, the shaft clear between the landings); the paths (the yard → the lab door → the atrium → both galleries → the car; B5's car → the antechamber → the vault → the service door → the corridor → the ward → the antechamber → the car: all found); the logic checks on one page (t99u, 132 checks: the Patient — up again at 45 % after a body shot, down for good the second time or after a headshot, a blast, the grenade launcher, fire; the overrides and the boarding — the top landing, a place each, one at the door lifted in; the ride — shut in and carried, the first aid to 75, stopped at B3, out at B5 with nobody fallen; the meeting — out onto the nurses' station at 10 s, the ward's doors, the infected out of the ward, 85 % and back in, untouchable on her way; the boss — kneeling at the table, her health, the vault door in the prep and the fight; the AED — a body fit to bring back and not a head-shot one, the one she can see, 260 × difficulty, never moved, the bots on it first, the shock 3.2 s later: up whole and faster, shot apart: down for good; the UV — 3 s of E, blinded and +40, ×1.25, her bodies no use, the infected burn and stay down, 10 s then 20 s to cool; the stagger — the IV pack ×1.3, blasts +10, frost +10 and slowed, a full gauge down 6 s, the next 25 % more, down ×1.5; the darts — hurt and dazed, out cold if dazed already, nobody off to the side; the defibrillator; the gas; the six memories — ARGUS's laser and shot, LEGATUS's rings, CHARON's fire, DAEDALUS's three mines, LORELEI's pull (crouched a third as hard), LYREBIRD's fog and her slipping off in it, all six before any again; the phases — the tags at 65 %, invulnerable, the team back up, the vial at 30 %, ×1.3; the lunge — its red strip, hurt and knocked flat in its path, untouched 1.6 m off its line, ×1.3 while she gets up; mass resuscitation — the wave, AEDs on three bodies; the escape — the doors, her mines and the infected gone, the spawns, the banks off, her medic bag to 60, a vent's warning then its fire, the bots' legs and each leg's goal past the next line; everybody in the car — the bonus, the results waiting for the ending, the car going up). Found by them besides the drafts above: two of its own checks' set-ups (a zombie's claws in the lunge's check, the dart's daze running out before the second dart). Pictures (the yard at dawn and a Patient getting up green, the ride in the blackout and B3 going by on the way up, the meeting, her kneeling at RT-1's table, the darts' strip, an AED by a body, a burning UV quarter, the dog tags, the song, the flare, the vial, the lunge's strip, a vent's warning and its sheet of fire, the car opening onto the dawn-lit atrium); the schema on local Postgres 16 (fresh, run twice, and over v6.98's with a chapter 9 record: qz_ep_max 10, the checks 1–30 — a 30 taken, a 31 refused, an 11 counted as 10 —, a chapter 10 clear with its hall of fame, the hard minimum: 240 s refused, 255 counted; chapter 9's records kept); regressions on the release build: every chapter boots and reaches its boss (eleven chapter cards, five of them S2), the global chat, the phone layout, the CSP (no violations), the modes' smoke run, the weapons' sound and step checks, chapters 7, 8 and 9's logic checks (36, 78 and 138, all passing) and HUD runs, chapter 10's HUD (the boss bar, the UV switch's prompt, the boarding and the ride, the first meeting, the minimap's B5 — found by it: the phase line read 「응급 처치」 through the vial's 2.8 s; the last vial's line now from the start of phase 3).

## v6.98 — zombie scenario chapter 9 「다이달로스」, season 2's fourth chapter (daedalus.js, mapep9.js new; build order: mapep9 after mapep8, daedalus after charon, before episode; design.js, models.js, game.js, scenario.js, episode.js, ai.js, net.js, charon.js, account.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, test/mock.js, schema.sql; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it)
- The owner's word: 「다음챕터 ㄱㄱ」 — the next chapter after CHARON.
- The story: chapter 8's flight log ended on 「06: 02 · 04 수송. 목적지 설령산 기상 레이더 기지.」 Chapter 9 is that station, on top of Mount Seolryeong, reached by its cable car; RT1-04 DAEDALUS, RT-1's engineer, who kept FERRY 06 flying. The feeling the suppressant held down was grief: he could not bear to lose anything more, so he walled the summit in with containers and guns and waited at the radar for 06. RT1-02 left before the team got there — her logs on the compound's radio (voice3, 「02의 기록」: the suppressant running low, 04 building walls, 04 sitting at the radar whenever he hears a rotor, 06 is not coming and she could not tell him); her last log after the clear: 「억제제가 듣지 않는다. 원액은 Q-7 중앙 연구소에만 있다.」 「혼자 간다. 04, 기다리지 마.」 — the season finale's hook (one of RT-1 left: 02, gone back to Quarantine Zone Q-7's central lab). The season card: 「9챕터 클리어 · 1차 회수팀은 이제 하나 — 02, 홀로 격리구역 Q-7 중앙 연구소로 · 다음 이야기 — 시즌 2 최종장 · 준비 중」; chapter 8's card now names chapter 9. (ep_records takes chapters 1–9: chapter 10 will need the check widened.)
- mapep9.js 「설령산 기상 레이더 기지」 (MAPDEFS.ep9, ep:9; a night of thundersnow — its own sky texture, snow, the storm's flashes, fog .018, north is −z; two levels: the valley at y 0 and the summit plateau at y 40, joined by the cable car): A the valley station's plaza (x ±28, z 166..214: the lot inside ploughed banks, the road south blocked by a plough and the army's barriers, parked cars, the shuttle bus, the bus shelter, the ticket office, the map board, lamps, pines) → gate gA (the station hall's doors, z 164) → the hall (the car waits at its dock, z 150: the platforms either side, the guide rail, the bull wheel, turnstiles, the operator's booth; the portal north) → the cable (level out of the hall to z 140, up the slope to z 68, level into the summit dock at (0, 40, 56); a lattice pylon beside the line at z ≈ 104 with its arm over it) → B the summit station's hall (over the slope on its rock face) → gate gB (z 48) → RT1-04's maze (containers in four bands, the way snaking west, east, west, east; two stacked in band 2 — the meeting's perch 5.2 up; his words sprayed on the steel, low cover, work lamps on the container tops) → the goal box before gate gC (the compound's south gate, z 13) → C the station compound (x ±36, z −20..12: the operations building west with the radar console, the generator house east with the transformer, the fuel tank north-east with its valve; the courtyard: the snowcat, a Stevenson screen, weather instruments, pallets, a pickup) → gate gD (the blast gate, z −21.5) → D the radome plaza (x ±44, z −90..−23: the radome on its 14 × 14 × 8 m tower — the catwalk at 48 with its railing and two stairs up, the dome on top —, FERRY 06's helipad west, the antenna mast east with a platform at 46, two sheds, RT1-04's workshop by the west one, the observation deck over the north cliff, the rods' masts, floods). Textures painted on load (bakeEp9Tex; chapter 6's snow and chapter 8's apron, crates and generators reused): the granite, the sky of torn cloud lit from within, the halls' precast panels, the tower's ribbed white concrete with rust runs, the radome's panels, the deck's boards, the snowcat, the Stevenson screen, the operations building's cladding, the turnstile, the bull wheel's chevrons, the booth, the sprayed words, the radar consoles (the scope's rings and echoes), the transformer, the rods' switch boxes, the signs. The mesh (ep9Mesh): the mountain (a 400 × 600 m heightfield every 5 m, kept 7.5 m under the car along the cable and under both stations), the pines, the radome (¾ of a sphere), the car's ropes. ~1,020 boxes (556 solid), 40 lights, ~270k triangles, 13,856 nav nodes; a first load (headless) 8.4 s against chapter 8's 6.8 s — most of it painting chapter 6's and 8's textures it reuses (with those painted, its own map 0.6 s, its light bake 2.5 s, the nav 1 s).
- Containment (a reachability probe: from each zone's start (the maze from its middle), every nav step not through a gate; a node more than 5 m outside the zone's box is an escape): the valley lot let out at both ends of the hall's fences (the banks stopped 2 m short of the fence line, and from a bank's end the fence could be stepped over), the radome plaza at both ends of the blast wall's invisible wall (2 m short of the plateau's edge: round the outside of the compound). Bots had been found standing outside the lot (−31, 164) through the boarding. Now invisible walls run along the fences and the blast wall reaches the edge; every zone reaches only itself. A finer check after a hard run's bot stood out the escape's last 55 s west of the maze (a flood fill on a 0.25 m grid of where a body 0.6 m wide fits, every gate open, from inside the zones): a 0.7 m slot between the compound's fence and the ends of the maze's side rocks let it out round the side — the rocks now reach the fence; nothing else gets out, on the summit or in the valley.
- The chapter (CHAP[9], season 2, k 'daedalus', code RT1-04; voice2 DAEDALUS, voice3 02's logs):
  - 1 waves ×3 in the valley plaza (the Static first, with the radio's line about it; expert's waves .75 the size; the infected come in from the sides and the hall's end) → the station's doors open: everyone into the cable car (40 s; the bots get a place each and one at a door is lifted in — four in one cabin jostled in its doorways and the last ran out the 40 s) → the ride (26 s, EP.ph 'ride'): the doors shut (the car's boxes move with it: the riders are shut in and carried; another page's riders are drawn where its own car is), up the cable; at 10.5 s lightning strikes the pylon — the car stops 3 s short of it (his voice on the radio: 「…내려가. 여긴 아무것도 없어.」), backup power, on into the summit dock; 5 s up the car's first-aid box patches the riders up to 75 (they came up the mountain on what the plaza had left them, and the maze and the compound wore them down to nothing: three of five full runs had lost every life in the compound).
  - 2 'breach' 180 s through the maze to the station gate (three sentry guns, one to a stretch — band 2 from its east end, band 3's west half from its west end, its east half from two stacked containers over it —, six tripwires; the trickle .45, expert .32, its spawns spaced 1.3×); the meeting at 10 s: DAEDALUS on the stacked containers (nails and mines into the maze), away north at 85 % or after 35 s. A bot still out in the maze while the rest (a player among them, or at least as many as are out) stand at the gate is brought up to it after 8 s.
  - 3 'act' in the compound: three charges (the transformer, the fuel tank's valve, the radar console), 9 s each, 02's logs after each and the clear (the trickle .44, hard .4, expert .33, its spawns spaced 1.7×; the bots keep together, on the open charge nearest the team — alone at charges 40 m apart they were picked off one by one).
  - 4 'boss' on the radome plaza (prep: the bots gather at its gate).
  - 5 'escape' 90 s back to the car (75 at first: from the plaza's far corners the bots made the car with 2 s left): every gate open, the radome coming down (his dead man's switch: blasts on the dome, it sinks and tilts), his mines and whatever was on the plaza gone when he falls; the trickle .6 (expert .45) from the compound's and the plaza's spawns, Statics and infected only, spaced 1.6×; lightning on whoever is under the open sky every 9–13 s. The bots go a gate at a time (the plaza → the compound → the maze's end → the station hall → a place in the car: one path the whole way was more than the path search looks through, and they stood on the plaza). Everybody alive in the car: the bonus, and the car goes back down with them in the ending.
- DAEDALUS (ZCLASS.daedalus 22000 nominal; the chapter sets 26,000 × (0.6 + 0.4 × humans) × difficulty; a zombie rig in RT-1's grey-green fatigues, the welding mask flipped up, ear defenders, a tool belt with a hammer, knee pads, the frame pack (the cable drum, the yellow battery), FERRY 06's red band on his left arm, RT1-04 on his back; 1.15×; his own poses eased one into the next — kneeling, throwing, aiming the nail gun, welding, perched, gliding, down, the wings, landing):
  - phase 1 「작업장」 (100–70 %) on the plaza, cover to cover, behind a steel plate held in front of him: a shot from within ±63° of where he faces, below his chin, does 40 % in sparks (the battery on his back, from behind: 1.4× and the stagger gauge; his head over the plate: all of it; blasts: all of it). Sentry guns set up (kneeling 1.4 s, two at most, every 18 s; 400 × (0.6 + 0.4 × humans) × difficulty, four bursts and 30 s of battery each, then they fizzle out), the nail gun (three red strips in a fan, .8 s: 16 each; two strips on you and you are nailed down .7 s), mines three at a time (they arm 1.2 s after landing; step within 1.6 m and .35 s later 2.4 m of blast: 34 and knocked flat; a shot sets one off; five live at most, 30 s), the wrench up close (26 / 34), a damaged sentry welded (2.4 s; 2 % of his health meanwhile breaks it off, +30 stagger).
  - 70 %: the plate falls on the plaza (it lies there), he goes up onto the catwalk. Phase 2 「레이더」 (70–30 %): the radar every 17 s (enraged 13) — from the dome a red beam turns twice round the plaza (1 s warming up, then 8 s: 12 to whoever it finds in the open, once a turn; under the catwalk, or something solid between you and the dome — crouched behind it will do — is safe); nails and mines from the catwalk, the welding torch for whoever comes up (a strip 5.5 m, .8 s: 28 and dazed); down among the rest for 12 s when two of the team come up, and every 18 s anyway (enraged 14), then up again — no sentries after phase 1.
  - 30 %: the wings (FERRY 06's spare rotor blades and a tarp on his frame; 2.4 s invulnerable; the radar stops). Phase 3 「날개」 (30–0 %): perch to perch (the catwalk's corners, the antenna platform, the observation deck, the sheds' roofs; after a volley from a perch, one time in five off to the next); swoops (a red strip 2.2 × up to 26 m through or within 2 m of one of them — straight, or turned up to 17° where that is clear for 8 m —, 1.2 s, then the dive off the perch to the strip's start — just below, or 5 m out from a deck or a platform — and the glide along it at 20 m/s: 28 and knocked flat), the landing at its end (3.5 s, 1.5× damage), back up; mines (every 18 s) and nails from the perches; the storm at its worst.
  - The lightning rods (useNear 0–3: three on the plaza and the radome's own, its switch at the tower's south foot): hold E 3 s → armed 25 s; the storm's next strike (within 3.5–5 s) comes to an armed rod: DAEDALUS close to it (the radome's: anywhere on the catwalk) takes 4 % of his health and +40 stagger, out of the air he falls (down 6 s); anyone of the team close to it 30 and knocked flat; 20 s to cool. Unarmed, the storm strikes whoever stands in the open (a blue ring 3 m, 1.6 s: 35 and knocked flat) every 16 s (phase 3: 11, enraged .75). One bot has the duty (the rod he is in reach of). Ammo boxes out of the sheds (RT-1's; 12 s in, every 30 s).
  - Stagger (gauge 100, 1.25× after each): the battery, HE +10, frost +10 (and slowed 3 s); full: down 6 s (1.5×; in the air he falls first). Every phase change: 3.5 s invulnerable, the team back up. Enraged after 240 s (expert 180): cooldowns ×.7, 1.2× pace.
- The Static (ZCLASS.static: 1300, armour 20, 5.1 m/s; the station's maintenance crew, struck in the storm — overalls, scorched skin, sparks coming off): its claws daze (.8 s); it dies in a discharge (a blue ring 3.2 m, .7 s: 22 and dazed); lightning within 10 m supercharges it 12 s (1.35× pace, +12 on its claws, bright).
- The sentry gun (ZCLASS.sentry, an actor that never moves — no physics, no knockback; its own hit box; the turret drawn where it stands): it sweeps its head and sees ±70° of where it points, 28 m (one within 4 m, or one who has just shot it, wherever — shot, it turns its sweep on them); a red laser holds the mark 1 s (lost after .45 s out of sight), then eight rounds (5 each, 4 on expert; DAEDALUS's 4), 2 s rest; blasts 2.5×. The maze's three: 700 × difficulty, never empty. A bot hit by one turns on it, aims at its head (epAimP: the hidden body's head stood over it, and bots in their headshot habit fired over the turret — they had lost two of five runs to the maze's guns), and does not go to get a fallen teammate up in a sentry's sight (C.reviveBad) — DAEDALUS's only within 15 m: two of his up through most of phase 1 kept everyone down for good (an expert run lost its last life with three lying in their sight), and with none of his counted a hard run's bots went down one after another by the same gun.
- The tripwires (six across the maze, a claymore at one end): stepping through the red beam sets it off — .7 s, then 30 and knocked flat within 3 m; jumping it does not, a shot sets it off at once; the bots jump them. (Found by the logic checks: the claymore's own box stood round the blast's sight lines and nobody was ever hurt — the box goes first now.)
- Sounds (rendered at start like the rest): danail (the nail gun), daservo (a sentry's servos), dazap (a Static's crackle), dastrike (a strike right overhead), daradar (the radar spinning up), dachime (the car's chime).
- Network (host-authoritative): the snapshot's boss block (bsPack: phase, the stagger gauge, flags — 1 on the catwalk, 2 down, 4 invulnerable, 8 enraged, 16 the first meeting, 32 the wings, 64 in the air, 128 repairing, 256 the radar, 512 landed, 1024 the plate —, his pose, the rods' states); events 'e9' (patterns, wires, mines, sentries, the radar, strikes, the plate's fall, the ride's first aid, the escape's clearing), 'e9r' (the ride: who was pulled into the car, the zombies gone); a client's shot on a mine or a claymore goes to the host ('e9s'); the car's place on a client comes from the ride's clock. NET_VER '-r18' (three zombie classes added to ZALL).
- HUD: the boss bar's status line (invulnerable, down, landed — now!, repairing — break it off, radar — take cover, enraged) and its phase line (on the radome, the wings, the steel plate); markers: the car while boarding, the rods (hot when he is in reach of one), the exit; the ride's progress; notes: nailed down, a rod armed, the radar, the car's first aid.
- scenario.js (every chapter): a body revived as another kind (SCEN.onRevive) drops what a chapter's own zombie sets on itself every frame — the bots' aim point and height (epAimP, epAimY), their priority (epPri), its own hit volume (epRay, epRayZc), its muzzle (epMuz), hidden (epHide), epDown. Found by the fastest runs (invulnerable, ×3 damage): three of four ran out the 40-second walk to the plaza with a bot circling inside the operations building, a Static at its heels — the Static was in a dead sentry's body and still had the sentry's aim point back in the maze (no line to it: the bots never saw it, and it never died) and its hit volume (nothing above 1.3 m to hit: no head). Chapter 5's centipede (aimed at .45 m, priority .7) and chapter 6's shield trooper (priority 2.2: left for last) had left theirs in the bodies after them too. The sentry's own per-frame hook stops when it is dead and its death drops its hit volume.
- scenario.js / episode.js (every scenario): a bot takes the supply box it needs from its three (SCEN.botPick: under 70 hp the heal; no main gun, a gun; under a magazine left for it, the ammo; under 40 armour, the kevlar; the heal if not whole; else any) — they had taken one at random, and a hard run went into the compound with two bots on 2 and 12 hp (wiped there).
- ai.js / episode.js (every chapter): the last one standing ran from the zombies (AI.fleeSpot) instead of going to its revive target or its objective, so a lone bot never got anyone up — an expert run's phase 3 went on 260 s with one bot running about and three lying by the radome — and the escape's last one ran about the maze till the car left. Now it goes: to a fallen teammate with nothing on top of them (B.epRv; it looks 48 m round instead of 24), or wherever the chapter wants it (B.epObj: the escape, a door, a device).
- episode.js / ai.js (every chapter): a bot got back up by a teammate (AI.onTeam) asks for its chapter goal again (B.epG = null) — it kept its old key, so the goal was never asked for and it stood where it got up (found in the compound: two bots stood 90 s at the fence where they had fallen while the third held the charge alone); C.reviveBad(fallen) lets a chapter keep the bots from going to get someone up somewhere covered.
- Balance (4-bot simulations, hand-stepped headless, one page; the "player" is a bot too and never buys: a pistol only): on the last builds (after the fixes below) the whole chapter on normal 8:52–12:00, seven wins in seven (four S, two A, one B); on hard 8:26–13:45, nine wins in eleven (three S, three A, three B; lost once at the valley's third wave, once at the boss after wipes in phases 1 and 2) — before the fixes two in five; on expert 10:57–14:24, eight wins in twelve (three A, five B; lost in the valley, the maze's band 3, the compound and phase 1) — before them one in six. Stage times on normal (each with its move): the valley 100–108 s, the ride 26, the maze 42–85, the compound 71–88, the boss (with its 12 s prep) 2:17–6:22 (phase 1 32–55 s, 175 with a wipe), the escape 59–66 (the car with 9–16 s to spare). Drafts and what changed:
  - the valley on expert: three lives lost there in one run (and a wipe in others) — its waves .85 → .75 the size; two of its spawns had stood in the lot's south corners, 13 m from where the team starts, and the bots were cornered against the plough: they stand out at the sides now;
  - the escape ran out with the bots stood on the plaza: one path from the plaza to the car was more than the path search looks through — a gate at a time now;
  - four bots jostled in the car's doorways and the last ran out the 40 s — lifted in at the door;
  - the maze's guns: the bots in their headshot habit fired over the turret (two runs of five lost there) — they aim at its own point now; it saw all round — ±70° now; band 3 was a crossfire (the gun on the stacked containers faced across it, the west end's along it: most of the maze's deaths on hard, one run 784 hp in it) — that gun faces along the band's east half now; rounds 6 → 5 (4 on expert), DAEDALUS's 5 → 4 and 525 → 400 health a gun; on expert the maze's trickle .38 → .32 (a run lost all three lives there);
  - phase 1 cost lives (two wipes in one hard run): his guns had no end — now four bursts and 30 s of battery each, two at a time, every 18 s; the bots wait for them to go before getting someone up only within 15 m of one;
  - the radar killed bots at the catwalk's edge and behind low cover: they go in under the catwalk at its foot, crouched cover counts, 12 a hit, every 17 s;
  - the compound wiped whole teams (three of five full runs lost every life there): the bots together on one charge at a time, the trickle .44 / .4 / .33 spaced 1.7×, the ride's first aid to 75 — and then the two finds above, in every scenario: Statics in dead sentries' bodies the bots never saw, and supply boxes taken at random (a hard run went in with two bots on 2 and 12 hp);
  - the escape cost lives to his mines left on the plaza and the infected about it: they go when he falls; its trickle Statics and infected only, spaced 1.6× (expert .45: a run's whole team fell on the plaza);
  - a lone bot ran about phase 3 for 260 s (expert) with three lying by the radome — the revive above.
- The server (schema.sql, run again by the owner): public.qz_ep_max() = 9, qz_ep_min row 9: 200 / 210 / 230 / 250 s. Four bots that could not be hurt and hit three times as hard cleared it in 6:24 easy, 6:21 normal, 7:44 hard, 7:36 expert, but much of the chapter goes as fast for anyone — the waves' pace, the cable car's 26 s, the preps, the escape's way back through the maze — so 0.7 of the bots would have been near what a strong team of four could do (reckoned 4:00 – 5:00: the charges three at once, the boss at four real guns' pace); the row is that less 15 %. Until it is run, ACC.epSrv claims a chapter 9 match as the free scenario. test/mock.js: epmax 9.
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): the containment probe (above: the leaks, then none); the paths from inside the operations building and the generator house to the plaza's starts (all found); the logic checks on one page (t98u, 138 checks: the Static — its claws, a strike's supercharge, the discharge; the boarding — the doors, a place each, one at a door lifted in, one 10 m off not, everybody in → the ride; shut in, carried, the first aid to 75, the strike stopping it short of the pylon, out at the summit; the maze — three sentries at 700 × difficulty, not counted by the trickle, locking on, firing, never moving nor pushed, blasts 2.5×, their own hit box, the bots' aim point, its field of view (not one behind it, one who shot it, one in front, one at its feet), a shot-down sentry's body taken over by a Static with nothing of the sentry's — its head hit, the bots see it —, the six wires, the claymore's blast, jumped over, shot; the meeting from 10 s, away at 85 % or after 35 s; a bot lost in the maze brought to the gate after 8 s; the three charges; DAEDALUS's health; the plate (40 % from in front, all of it over it, 140 % and stagger on the battery, blasts), the stagger gauge and its 25 % more, mines (arming, stepping, the blast, a shot), the nail gun's strips and the pin, a sentry set up with its four bursts, empty and gone, welded and broken off; a rod armed by 3 s of E and the strike's 4 %; 70 %: the plate dropped, invulnerable, the catwalk; the radar (in its beam, a quarter turn off, under the catwalk), the torch, down to the plaza when two come up and after 22 s on his own; 30 %: the wings, the radar stopping, the perches, a swoop's strip and its hit, the landing ×1.5; the radome rod's strike out of the air; bsPack / bsUnpack, the boss bar; the escape — his mines and the infected gone, every gate open, the radome down, the bots a gate at a time and into the car, the bonus, the car going down; a bot's supply box by its need (hurt: the heal; no main gun: a gun; nearly dry: the ammo; no armour: the kevlar); every radio line in both languages; the sounds); pictures: the map from 21 places, the ride (16), DAEDALUS in every phase (11), the plate held, sparking, dropped (8); the balance and fastest runs above; schema.sql on local Postgres 16: fresh, run again, from v6.97's schema with chapter 8 records (kept) and run again: qz_me epmax 9, a chapter 9 ticket and clear → ep_records and the hall of fame row, a ticket for 10 is 9's, ep 10 refused, on hard a 220-second clear does not count and 235 does; regressions on the release build: every chapter boots (1–9: the first stage, then the boss stage with its boss alive and being fought; ten cards, four with the S2 tag; options 1–9 + free), the chapter list at 1280×720 (chapter 9 locked, then open with its S2 tag) with the news banner and the patch notes, the lobby with the global chat (v6.98 in its footer, the news), the multiplayer screen on a phone, the four pages' CSP (none broken), four modes' smoke run, v6.94–96's StarTrack, sound and season 2 tests, chapter 7's and 8's logic checks (t96u, t97u 78), chapter 9's on the release build (t98u, 138), chapter 7's, 8's and 9's HUD (the boss bar behind the plate with the intro card, a rod's switch prompt and progress, armed, phase 2's card with the radar sweeping, phase 3's wings on the plaza, the boarding note, the ride's bar, the first meeting's bar in the maze).

## v6.97 — zombie scenario chapter 8 「카론」, season 2's third chapter (charon.js, mapep8.js new; build order: mapep8 after mapep7, charon after lorelei, before episode; design.js, game.js, scenario.js, episode.js, epbase.js, net.js, lorelei.js, account.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, test/mock.js, schema.sql; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it)
- The owner's word: 「담챕타 ㄱㄱ」 — the next chapter after LORELEI.
- The story: chapter 7 ended on the back of RT-1's roster — 「나머지는 북쪽 비행장으로 갔다. 나는 남아서 기다린다.」. Three of RT-1 were left (02, 04, 06). Chapter 8 is the airfield, Seolryeong, where RT-1 meant to get out; RT1-06 CHARON, the team's pilot. The feeling the suppressant held down was fear: he was afraid to touch the ground among the dead, so he never came down — he still flies RT-1's extraction helicopter, FERRY 06. The flight log in the tower's radio (stage 2) has RT-1's last flight: 02 and 04 on board, 06 flying; its last line (the clear): 「06: 02 · 04 수송. 목적지 설령산 기상 레이더 기지. 돌아와서 기다린다.」 — chapter 9's hook (the weather radar station on the mountain, RT1-02 and RT1-04).
- mapep8.js 「설령 비행장」 (MAPDEFS.ep8, ep:8; dusk in a blizzard — its own sky texture, snow, fog .017, north is −z, the ground y 0): A the car park outside the wire (x ±30, z 57..98: rows of cars, the shuttle bus, the plough truck, the guard post; berms all round) → gate gA (the perimeter fence's two chain-link leaves, z 55) → B the landside plaza (x ±40, z 13..54.6: the control tower — a concrete shaft to the cab at 6, its sill all round, the radio console, a stair up its south side —, RT-1's last stand with their truck at its foot, the operations building's lobby) → gate gB (the airside fence, z 12) → C the airside yard and hangars (x 4..66, z −32..11.6: hangar 1 with a plane and a helicopter under repair and its mezzanine office at 4, hangar 2 the armory, the generator shed, the fuel farm) → gate gC (hangar 1's big doors) → D the apron and the runway (x ±56, z −100..−32.6: the helipad, three AA guns in sandbag pits, containers, baggage carts, a de-icer, a fuel bowser, a light plane, floodlights, the windsock). Textures painted on load (bakeEp8Tex, ep6's snow reused). MAP.ez: zones, doors, tower (the cab's box and window band), aaDev, aa (the guns and their loaders), pad, crash (three sites), fly (the box the helicopter keeps to), enter, meet (the tower's orbit), drifts (where the Frozen lie, per zone), cover. ~1000 boxes, 37 lights, ~280k triangles, 24,421 nav nodes.
- Containment (found by a reachability probe: every nav node walkable from each zone's start with its doors shut, each step checked against the world's boxes): the apron let out at three places — the runway's edge berms stepped up onto the side berms (1.2 → 2), the east berm's 1.4 end piece, a gap where the fence between the hangars met hangar 2 a metre short; and every landside zone let out over piles, a truck and a barrier onto its berms and from there into the snow fields. Now the edge berms stop short, the east berm is 2 m to the hangar, the fence meets the wall, and see-through invisible walls (9 m, 'none' + pass) stand on the far side of every zone's berms and fences, the gates' openings left: each zone reaches only itself and the zones before it. The apron's box reaches the hangar doors (the bots stopped short of it and ran out the 40 s move).
- The chapter (CHAP[8], season 2, k 'charon', code RT1-06; voice2 CHARON, voice3 the tower radio): 1 waves ×3 in the car park (the Frozen: sleepers laid in its drifts at the start, more bursting up out of the snow near the team as the waves come; the radio's line about the snow mounds once one is out) → 2 'hold' 80 s in the tower's cab (the bots hold at the cab; the radio reads RT-1's flight log as it comes in, at 34 s and 62 s); the first meeting at 16 s — FERRY 06 in from the west, circling the tower at 22 m and the height of the cab's windows (7.4), its searchlight on the cab: the door gun's sweep through the cab (a laser 1.2 s and a ring on its floor for the players — TELE nb, the bots told by danger() instead —, then 1.2 s of rounds at chest height: 18 each to whoever stands, nobody crouched below the sills), strafes at whoever is outside; at 85 % or after 40 s, or when the hold ends, away north (invulnerable) and gone → 3 'act' in the hangars: the generator (the yard's shed), the AA shells (hangar 2), the AA fire control (hangar 1's mezzanine office), 9 s each, any order → 4 'boss' on the apron. stageGates: gA from 2, gB from 3, gC at 4 except a phase replay; bossGo shuts gC behind them (whoever is still inside the hangar is put out on the apron — the clients get their places in epgo). Every stage lays its zone's sleepers (2 + .75 × humans; the tower .6 of that). A wipe in the boss stage replays the phase reached at that phase's health (phase 3: on foot by the wreck, in the whiteout; the door gunner stays shot). The ending (endFx): end0 / end1, then the season card (「8챕터 클리어 · 1차 회수팀은 이제 둘 — 02와 04, 설령산 기상 레이더 기지」). Chapter 7's card now says what comes next.
- CHARON (ZCLASS.charon 22000 nominal; the chapter sets 16,000 × (0.6 + 0.4 × humans) × difficulty; a zombie rig in a sage flight suit, a dark survival vest with RT1-06 on its back, the flight helmet with its visor up, an oxygen mask hanging off it, a kneeboard; 1.15× on his feet, 1× in his seat; his own poses eased one into the next — seated at the controls, the flare gun raised, kneeling at his radio, screaming, crawling out of the wreck, running and looking back over his shoulder, down):
  - FERRY 06 (HELI: its own meshes in the weapon material — the hull, the tail boom, the main and tail rotors and the rotor's blur, the door gun on its pintle that swings at its mark, the door gunner, the rocket pods, the ropes, the searchlight's beam, see-through glazing so he shows in his seat; red / green / the beacon, the searchlight's light, the engine fire; smoke, the rotor wash throwing snow; the rotor and turbine a looping 3D voice, CHSND). The host flies it (tx/ty/tz, vmax, a heading: HELI.fly, the pitch and roll from its accelerations); clients ease to the snapshot's pose. Shots hit its own volumes (a.epRay → part 'ball', HELI.ray / partAt): the cockpit (him) ×1, the engine on top ×2.2 (and stagger: 380 × damage / max health), the tail rotor ×1.4 (160 ×), the hull ×.3; HE ×.9 +15 stagger; the door gunner has his own health (6 % of CHARON's): shot, he falls out of the door and the strafes become the nose gun's (14 m instead of 20). No knockback in the air. The bots aim at the cockpit, the hull or the engine by turns (a team's shots spread over it).
  - phase 1 (100–70 %) circling the team at 29 m, 16–19 m up: strafes (a red strip 2.6 × 20 m through one of them, 1.2 s, then the rounds walk it in 1 s: 22, shoved; the nose gun's 14 m in .8 s), rocket walks (six red rings 2.8 m in a line through one of them, landing .12 s apart: 30 × falloff, knocked flat near the middle), the passengers (hovering 8 m up beside the team, ropes out: ⌈humans / 2⌉ zombies slide down, one more in phase 2; every 24 s, 26 in phase 2, only while fewer than (3 + 1.5 × humans) × difficulty are up). Who loads the AA guns is its first target.
  - The AA guns (AAG; three twin guns in sandbag pits): hold E 3 s at the loader box (useNear 0–2; two at once 1.25×) → loaded (green lamp) → it fires the moment FERRY 06 is within 85 m and in sight: 2.2 s of flak (tracers, bursts round it), then 2.5 % of his health and +28 stagger; 30 s to reload. One bot has the duty: the ready gun nearest the helicopter.
  - Stagger (gauge 100, ×1.25 after each): the engine, the tail rotor, flak +28, HE +15, frost +10 (and slowed 3 s), the door gunner's fall +40. Full in the air: it sinks to 5 m, wobbling, 6 s (×1.5 damage, no attacks); on foot: down 6 s.
  - 70 %: the engine catches fire (a blast, smoke from then on, a cockpit alarm). Phase 2 lower (9–13 m, 23 m round), faster: strafes, rockets, passengers, burning fuel dumped along a strip as it flies over (orange 1.1 s, then 7 s of fire: 10 / s), rotor-wash dives (a red ring 7 m round one of them 1.4 s, then a pass at 4.6 m: 26, knocked flat and thrown).
  - 30 %: the tail rotor goes — it spins down onto the crash site furthest from the team (a red ring 9 m, 3.6 s: 55 × falloff, knocked flat, thrown); the wreck (burnt, the boom broken off beside it) is two boxes in the world (cover; whoever is caught inside is moved out; its nav nodes cost 400 so paths go round it) with fire round its engine end; the whiteout (fog .017 → .046, whiter, the sky with it). He crawls out of the cockpit (3.5 s invulnerable).
  - phase 3 (30–0 %) on foot, terrified: he runs from whoever comes within 8 m (at 1.05× his pace, to cover, a crash site or the pad away from them all, out of the fires; stuck 1.2 s, somewhere else); the flare gun (a red ring 2.6 m on one of them, .9 s, the flare's arc, 6 s of fire: 9 / s, blinded a moment); a mayday (every 28 s, 22 enraged, while fewer than half the passengers' cap are up: kneeling at his radio 2.6 s, then 1 + ⌈humans / 2⌉ Frozen burst up out of the snow round the team; 4 % of his health in damage cuts it — 1.5 s staggered, +30); a scream when two are within 6 m or one within 4 (a pink ring 6 m, .7 s: 12, dizzy, thrown); claws when cornered (22, heavy 30). Enraged after 240 s (expert 180): cooldowns ×.7, faster.
  - Every phase change: 3.5 s invulnerable, the team back up.
  - Ammo boxes (a long fight against something in the sky empties the guns): one in each AA gun's pit (beside the gun, across from the loader) from 12 s into the fight, and two at the wreck's cabin doors (RT-1's cargo) 4 s after the crash; every 30 s a new one wherever none is lying (EPK: half a reserve for every gun carried, gone after 45 s; the radio says so once for the wreck's). A bot with under a magazine left in reserve (main gun, else the pistol) goes for the nearest box within 50 m unless it has the AA duty. (Found by the balance runs: in a long phase 3 the bots had emptied their guns and were finishing him with knives, axes and frost grenades — 220 s at under 1 % of his health; with the boxes the next runs' phase 3 took 18–51 s.)
- The Frozen (ZCLASS.frozen: 1500, armour 30, 5 m/s; SCEN.SPD .86; the airfield's ground crew in orange hi-vis parkas with reflective bands, ear defenders, frost-white skin and hair, snow on the shoulders, icicles off the hem): asleep (a.frDorm) it lies at y −1.45 with no brain and no physics, hidden from the bots (epHide + epHid), not a zombie close by (zNearPt), not counted by the trickle; every page draws its drift instead of its body (a heaped snow mound, a hand and its orange cuff out of it) by where it is. It wakes when a human comes within 5 m, fires within 18 m (HEAR: a shot's reach × .42; running feet 6 m), hits it — or the moment it was given (a burst): up out of the snow in .75 s (epKin), a burst of snow and ice ('fw'), then 2.6 s rushing at 1.45×. Fire and blasts 1.6×; its claws chill (−25 % speed 1.8 s: frChillT, the host's own actors at once, a remote player by 'ech' chl). It shatters (ice, 'fs'). A wave's or a trickle's Frozen (SCEN.spawn with no place) comes up 10–15 m from one of them, 8 m from all, in the open, in the zone, .25–1.1 s after; a wave's last ones still asleep get up when the rest are down to one. Its own unstuck check (the scenario's skips zombies with a brain of their own).
- Sounds (rendered at start like the rest): chrotor (blade slaps at 4.5 / s over the turbine's whine, a 2 s loop), chrkt, chflak, chburst, chcrash, chalarm, chmay (a man's voice through a radio band), chflare, frburst, frshat, chrope.
- Network (host-authoritative): the snapshot's boss block (bsPack: phase, the stagger gauge, flags — 1 flying, 2 down / sinking, 4 invulnerable, 8 enraged, 16 the first meeting, 32 the door gunner in his door, 64 the engine on fire, 128 the whiteout, 256 a mayday, 512 crashing, 1024 the ropes out —, his pose, the gunner's health, the crash site, the AA guns' states, the helicopter's pose and velocities, rpm, smoke, the ropes); events 'ech' (boarding, wind-ups, strafes, the sweep, rockets, the ropes, the fuel, dives, fires, flares, the gunner's fall, flak, downs, the engine, the crash and the wreck, the whiteout, mayday and its break, screams, the Frozen waking and shattering, the chill). A client that missed the boarding puts the helicopter up from the snapshot. NET_VER '-r17' (two zombie classes added to ZALL).
- HUD: the boss bar's second gauge is the door gunner (사수 / GUNNER; hidden once he is down and during the first meeting), the stagger gauge as ever; its status line: invulnerable, going down, sinking / down, mayday, enraged, passengers on the ropes, whiteout; the phase and "in the air". Markers: each ready AA gun's loader (⚡, brighter while the helicopter is within its reach), a loaded gun (◎), the door gunner (◎). The loader's prompt (hold E, its progress; loaded; firing; reloading N s; nothing to shoot at). Notes: the sweep (get down below the sills), a mayday (break it), fire under you, the chill.
- episode.js (every chapter): EP.reviveAll gets the fallen up beside a teammate still standing only where the body can be walked there from the teammate in six steps (EP.fitsTo, charFits at each), else on the teammate's own spot — the random metre either way had put a bot through the car park's shut gate (the teammate pressed against it), out of the zone: the zombies crowded the gate and the wave never ended (found twice in 60-odd expert runs of stage 1; t97u now revives three 40 times beside one at the gate: always on its side).
- episode.js (every chapter): a stage replayed after a wipe asks for each bot's goal again (B.epG = null in stageStart). The goal's key had not changed when the same goal was left (the devices still to do), so botPre never asked, and the new round's camp spot stood: in stage 3's replay one bot walked back to the tower's cab and another wandered the yard.
- Balance (4-bot simulations, hand-stepped headless, one page; the "player" is a bot too, and it never buys: a pistol only): the whole chapter on normal 5:49–7:17, S with no life lost (five runs); on hard 6:38–10:32, five wins in six (three S, two B with two lives lost) and one loss (wipes in the tower, the hangars and the boss's phase 2); on expert none of seven through the drafts below (four lost in the car park, two in the hangars before its trickle was cut, one at the boss) — the boss stage cold (the default weapons) on expert: two wins in three, one wipe each. Stage times on normal (each with its move): the car park 113–121 s, the tower 87–111 (80 s held), the hangars 47–55, the boss 2:03–2:49 (the bots' bought guns: phase 1 in 32–42 s); cold, 2:33–3:12. In the fights the AA guns put in 5–16 % of his health (3–7 bursts), the gunner was shot out in three of nine, he went down (a full stagger gauge) 0–1 times, and the bots broke one mayday in fifteen (humans will do better: it takes 4 % in 2.6 s). Drafts and what changed:
  - phase 3 dragged on (5–10 min, one run 1,105 s with two wipes): the bots had run their guns dry against the helicopter (nothing to pick up in the boss stage) — the ammo boxes above, the bots going for them;
  - phase 1 cost lives (one wipe on normal, three on expert in one run): the passengers did most of the damage — now ⌈humans / 2⌉ a drop in phase 1 (one more in phase 2), down from 1 + ⌈humans / 2⌉;
  - stage 3 on hard: two wipes in one run, one in another — the trickle .85 → .7 (and the bots' goals after a wipe, above); on expert the trickle there is .5 (at .7 three runs lost every life in it — the bots kept together on one device at a time did no better —; at .5 eight runs cleared it, six with a wipe);
  - expert's car park: 4 clears in 12 runs (chapter 7's square: 5 in 6) — not the Frozen (none at all: 2 in 6), nor the spawn points (round the edges now, none among the cars by the start: 2 in 6); its waves on expert are now .8 the size (at .85: 18 clears in 24; at .8: 6 in 8, five without a wipe). The Frozen in its waves 14 / 20 → 12 / 16 of the mix, bursting 10–15 m from one of the team and 8 m from all (was 8–13 and 6).
- The server (schema.sql, run again by the owner): public.qz_ep_max() = 8, qz_ep_min row 8: 180 / 180 / 210 / 230 s (four bots that could not be hurt and hit three times as hard cleared it in 4:29 easy, 4:30 normal, 5:08 hard, 5:30 expert; ×0.7 — the tower's 80-second hold keeps it from going faster). Until it is run, ACC.epSrv claims a chapter 8 match as the free scenario (as for chapter 7 in v6.96). test/mock.js: epmax 8.
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): the containment probe (every nav node walkable from each zone's start with its doors shut, each step checked against the world's boxes: the leaks above, then none); the nav-node check (places whose nearest node is through a wall: the hangar crates, now flush); the paths from the yard's east end to the apron (all found, the bots there on the apron in 10–15 s); the logic checks on one page (t97u, 78 checks: the Frozen asleep — still, unseen by the bots, no zombie nearby —, waking at 5 m, at a shot 16 m off but not 21, at a hit, rising, rushing, fire 1.6×, the chill; a wave's Frozen bursting up 8–15 m from the team; three fallen got up 40 times beside one pressed against the shut gate: always on its side; the meeting's sweep through the cab (crouched below the sills: no hit) and the retreat at 85 %; the helicopter's volumes (a ray at the cockpit, one beside it), the cockpit ×1, the engine ×2.2 and its stagger, the tail rotor ×1.4, the hull ×.3, the gunner's own health and his fall, a strafe hitting the one on its strip and not one 6 m off, rockets, the drop, an AA gun loaded in 3 s firing and hitting, its reload, a full gauge sinking it (×1.5) and its climb back, 70 %: phase 2 (invulnerable, the fuel's fire, the dive), 30 %: the crash on its site, the wreck's boxes, the whiteout, him on foot; the ammo boxes (the three pits and the wreck's two, clear of the wreck and its fire; a bot short of ammo going for the nearest and topping up on it); on foot: a hit, the mayday's Frozen and its 4 %, the flare's fire, the scream, the flight; bsPack / bsUnpack; the boss bar's text; the death, the clear, the whiteout lifting; every radio line in both languages; the eleven new sounds rendered, none silent); pictures: the map from 17 places (the car park and its gate, the plaza, the tower's stair and cab, the operations lobby, both hangars and the mezzanine, the yard, the apron, the pad, the runway, the AA pits, from above), CHARON in his seat (through the glass) and on foot in each pose, the helicopter (the rotor, the door gun and gunner, the ropes, the smoke, the searchlight), the wreck, the Frozen asleep in a drift and up; schema.sql on local Postgres 16: fresh, run again, from v6.96's schema with chapter 7 records (kept) and run again: qz_me epmax 8, a chapter 8 ticket and clear → ep_records (A) and the hall of fame row, a ticket for 9 is 8's, ep 10 refused, on hard a 200-second clear does not count and 215 does; regressions on the release build: every chapter boots (1–8: the first stage — chapter 8's with its five sleepers —, then the boss stage with its boss alive; nine cards, three with the S2 tag; options 1–8 + free), the chapter list at 1280×720 (chapter 8 locked, then open) with the news banner and the patch notes, the lobby with the global chat, the multiplayer screen on a phone, the four pages' CSP, four modes' smoke run, v6.94–96's StarTrack, sound and chapter 7 tests, chapter 7's HUD, chapter 8's (the boss bar in the air with the gunner's gauge and the AA markers, an AA loader's prompt and progress, the first meeting in the cab, phase 3's card).

## v6.96 — zombie scenario chapter 7 「로렐라이」, season 2's second chapter (lorelei.js, mapep7.js new; build order: mapep7 after mapep6, lorelei after legatus, before episode; design.js, game.js, scenario.js, episode.js, net.js, legatus.js, account.js, lobby.js, newsban.js, style.css, test/mock.js, schema.sql; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it)
- The owner's word: 「3하고 5 ㄱㄱ」 — after v6.95 (3), the fifth of the suggested next patches: season 2's chapter 7, a new map and a new boss after LEGATUS.
- The story: chapter 6 ended on a signal from beyond the line — 「…북쪽으로 와라. 우리는 기다리고 있다.」. It comes from the arts center of Hanseol, outside Containment Line 1, a shelter once; the voice is RT-1's negotiator, RT1-03 LORELEI, who read its evacuation broadcasts. The feeling the suppressant held down was loneliness: she cannot bear to be left alone, so she calls everyone to her. Her vest holds RT-1's roster (01, 05, 07 crossed out — the three RT-2 put down; 02, 04, 06 not) and on its back: the others went north, to the airfield.
- mapep7.js 「한설 아트센터 대피소」 (MAPDEFS.ep7, ep:7; a snowy night, north is −z, the floors y 0): A the snowed-in square (x ±33, z 40..90: the fountain and its bronze singer, three relief tents, two buses and an ambulance, the PA poles, benches, shops, the park) → gate gA (the chained glass doors, sliding apart) → B the shelter foyer (x ±27, z 4..39.4: cots in rows, the box office, the café, the decorated tree, a mezzanine at 4.8 up a grand stair) → gate gB (the fire door in the east wall) → C backstage (x 27..48, z −66..10.4: the corridor, the rehearsal room, the scene dock with its catwalk at 6.5, the loading door, the dimmer racks, the dressing rooms, the stage manager's desk) → gate gC (the side doors into the house) → D the main hall (x ±24, z −70..2: the stalls as the shelter left them, the orchestra pit, the stage at 1.4 behind the proscenium and its curtain — gate gD, rising — the fly galleries at 9, balconies at 4.4, boxes at 7.8, the rear platform with its seats, four speaker stacks, the chandelier on its chain). Textures painted on load (texE7*, ~50; ep6's snow and curb reused), E7GLOW the foyer tree's lights. MAP.ez: zones, doors, dfind (the trace points), dim (the dimmers), meet (the catwalk), stageP, perch, floorP, spk, chand, cover, pad.
- Nav: two doorways had no floor (the facade and the hall's wall stood on nothing between two floors) and three stairs began 0.1 m from a nav cell's centre (the first step's edge blocked that cell: the grand stair, the fly galleries', the rear platform's) — no path from the square into the foyer, onto the mezzanine or into the hall (the bots ran out the 40 s moves; found by a nav probe of 97 paths between every place the chapter uses: now all found). Sills under gA and gC, the stairs moved to whole metres.
- The chapter (CHAP[7], season 2, k 'lorelei', code RT1-03; voice2 LORELEI, voice3 the evacuation broadcast): 1 waves ×3 in the square (the shelter's PA babbles from the poles every 10–13 s — 'lrpa' — and the radio's line about the Listeners once one is out) → 2 'terms' in the foyer: trace the broadcast from three points in turn (the box office, the mezzanine, the café; 20 s each, nobody infected inside) → 3 'act' backstage with the power off (EP.setPow(0): powLo .34 / .05, flashlights on): the three dimmer racks (9 s each, any order); the first meeting at 9 s — LORELEI on the scene dock's catwalk, singing (a short song, 20 m, no burst) and screaming down at them, clawing whoever climbs up; at 85 % or when the power is back she walks off through the fly hatch (invulnerable) → 4 'boss' in the hall. stageGates: gA from 2, gB from 3, gC at 4 except a phase replay; gD only at the go (it rises at the start of each try) and gC shuts behind them. A wipe in the boss stage replays the phase reached at that phase's health; the speakers and the chandelier stay as they were. The ending (endFx): end0 / end1, then the season card (「7챕터 클리어 · 1차 회수팀은 아직 셋 남았다 — 북쪽 비행장」). Chapter 6's season card now says what comes next.
- LORELEI (ZCLASS.lorelei 22000 nominal; the chapter sets 13,000 × (0.6 + 0.4 × humans) × difficulty; a 1.2× zombie rig — white smock, the orange EVAC vest, long black hair, a glowing sac at her throat — with her own upper-body poses eased one into the next and a bullhorn at her hip, at her mouth to scream):
  - phase 1 (100–70 %) on the stage, walking between six marks: her song (3.2 s; everyone within reach is pulled toward her at 2.6 m/s × (1 − .4 d/R), a crouched one at .35 of that; beyond 12 m only who has a clear line to her; the reach 34 m with the four speakers on, 31 / 27 / 22, 16 with none; 4 % of her health in damage breaks it — down 1.5 s, +30 stagger —, else a pink ring 8 m round her bursts at its end: 40 and knocked flat, dizzy further out); the bullhorn scream (a red strip 5.5 × 22 m for 1 s: 30, dizzy, shoved; stopped by walls); calling her audience (Listeners from the hall's spawns, cd 22 s, not past 2 + humans of them).
  - 70 % 「막간」: 3.5 s invulnerable, the team back up, a leap up to a fly gallery. Phase 2: perches (the fly galleries, the upper boxes, the balconies, center stage; picked about 20 m from the team where most of them see her) for 5–7.5 s, then down into the stalls for an 11–14 s hunt after the loudest of them, by turns. The embrace (on the floor: a laser 1.2 s, then the marked one is pulled at 4.8 m/s while she walks in; within 2.1 m they are held 2.6 s — 10 every .5 s, ×1.25 taken — until 3 % of her health frees them), the follow spot (8 s: a beam from the lighting bridge and every zombie goes for whoever it lights — h.epMark), lighting bars cut down onto the stage from a gallery when anyone is on it (two red strips across it 1.3 s: 34, knocked flat; the bars lie there), songs, screams.
  - 30 % 「피날레」: the blackout (EP.setDark: emergency strobes, flashlights). Phase 3: she hunts by sound like her audience (HEAR) and can hardly be seen — dithered away (uTint −.9), shown while she sings, screams, lunges, calls, holds someone or is down, for .5 s after a hit, within 7 m (−.55) or in the player's flashlight cone within 18 m (−.45); her humming as she moves (in 3D, every page). The lunge at the loudest (a strip .8 s, 15 m/s: 38, knocked flat), the shriek ring 7.5 m round her (1.2 s: 30, dizzy), short songs (2.4 s, .8 the reach, 3.5 % breaks them), decoys (her humming and footsteps from up to three places — the speakers still on, else spots in the stalls — 8 s, every page), calls. Enraged after 240 s (expert 180): cooldowns ×.7, faster.
  - Stagger (gauge 100, ×1.25 after each down): a broken song +30, a speaker gone +30, the chandelier +60, HE +15, frost +10 (and slowed 3 s). Full: down 6 s (×1.5). Her leaps (epKin) go through the proscenium's opening or by way of the stage's front / the stalls' front (crossOK, VIA): never through its walls.
  - The speakers (SPK; four stacks, 360 × difficulty each): shot to pieces (CHAP.boxShot 'spk'; a client sends 'elrs', the host rate-limits it) or their power cut at the box beside each (hold E 3 s: useNear 0–3). A red power lamp while on; blown: a torn black front that smokes. The chandelier (CHAND): its chain shot through (150) or the winch on the west wall held 2 s (useNear 4) — a ring 5.5 m round where it lands, 1 s; under it LORELEI loses 8 % of her health and +60 stagger, humans 50 × falloff and knocked flat, zombies 3000; then it lies smashed and tilted as a low box in the world (cover; whoever is inside it is moved out), its glow off.
  - Bots: phase 1 at cover 14–22 m from her; later a ring round her in the stalls; one bot has a duty — the nearest speaker she is not by (not while she sings), else waiting at the winch for her to come under the chandelier (it pulls when she is within 5.5 m); out of her song's reach crouched, out of the embrace's pull (danger).
- The Listener (ZCLASS.listener: 1700, armour 20, 5.4 m/s; SCEN.SPD .86; a citizen from the shelter in a padded coat and a red scarf, eyes bandaged): blind — its own ears instead of the zombie AI's eyes (EPZ.listener.ai, host). HEAR (once a frame): every human's noise as a reach — a shot 44 m fading over a second (silenced 9, a blade 5), running feet 15, walking 7, crouch-walking or the walk key 2.4, a reload 6, a device being worked 11, 1.8 m felt anyway; a wall between: as if 60 % further. Heard in the last 2.5 s: the zombie AI hunts that one (B.target) at a zombie's pace, a gasp ('lsalert', and an arc on the sound display); quiet: it shuffles at half that to where it last heard something and stands there, head on one side, turning (its own pose eased in and out); 12 s of silence: toward the nearest of them; 32 s and nobody within 30 m: moved to a fresh spawn. It hums now and then (lshum, 3D).
- Sounds (rendered at start like the rest): lrsong (a wordless vocalise: sawtooth voices through three formant bands, vibrato, breath; two melodies), lrhum, lrscream (the bullhorn), lrcresc, lrbreak, lrpa (the shelter's PA babble), lshum, lsalert, lrchain, lrcrash, lrspk, lrbat. The look: rings of sound out from her while she sings, the sac's pink light, a pink haze on the screen of a player she pulls (#epLure), the follow spot's beam, falling bars, the chandelier's light.
- Network (host-authoritative): the snapshot's boss block (bsPack: phase, gauges, flags — 1 singing, 2 down, 4 invulnerable, 8 enraged, 16 the first meeting, 32 shrouded, 64 holding someone, 128 pulling someone in, 256 perched, 1024 showing herself —, her pose, the song's progress, reach and pull, the speakers' mask, the chandelier's state, who she holds, who is lit, the power boxes' and the winch's progress); events 'elr' (wind-ups, the song, breaks, the burst, screams, rings, downs, the embrace, the spot, bars, decoys, speakers, the chandelier, leaps, landings, phase cards, the Listeners' gasp), 'elrs' (a client's hit on a speaker or the chain). Every page pulls its own player; the host pulls its bots. NET_VER '-r16' (two zombie classes added to ZALL).
- HUD (episode.js useHud, style.css): while a hold-E prompt is up the note line (HUD.note) sits under it (#hud.uOn; on touch under its 58 % too) — her song's hint ran through the prompt's progress bar at a speaker's power box.
- The server (schema.sql, run again by the owner): public.qz_ep_max() = 7, qz_ep_min row 7: 200 / 200 / 200 / 260 s (four bots that could not be hurt and hit three times as hard cleared it in 4:55 easy, 5:07 normal, 5:11 hard, 6:18 expert; ×0.7 — the foyer's three 20-second points keep it slow). Until it is run, ACC.epSrv claims a chapter 7 match as the free scenario (as for chapter 6 in v6.92). test/mock.js: epmax 7.
- Balance (4-bot simulations, hand-stepped headless, one page): whole chapter on normal: 7:22–9:20, wins with 0–1 lives lost (S, A, A); on hard 8:59–16:03 with 1–2 lives lost (four wins), and three losses in stage 3 before the catwalk's dimmer moved — the bots to it walked the whole catwalk past LORELEI (now at its south end by the stair; her walk and the hatch in the north half), with backstage lighter too (the trickle .7, .45 during the meeting; fewer Listeners in its mix; her scream there 20 and her song 20 m). Stage times (each with its move): the square 110–170 s, the foyer 82–95 (167 with a wipe), backstage 58–96 (161 with a wipe); the boss stage started cold (no supply boxes before it): normal 1:19–9:53 (the bots' weapons decide — a minigun from a supply box clears it in under two minutes), expert 6:12 with two wipes (two losses on earlier drafts). Drafts: the Listener at 5.9 m/s and ×1.15 hunting outran everything (the most damage in every run) — now 5.4 and a plain zombie's pace; phase 2 dragged (perched half the time where the bots could not see her) — now perched 5–7.5 s and down hunting 11–14, by turns, her perches picked for being seen; the finale's ring 9 m / 34 → 7.5 m / 30, fewer called in it, the trickle .34 → .3; the bots never broke a song at 5 % (now 4 %); the chandelier only fell once a bot waited at the winch (the duty). In the whole-chapter wins 3–20 % of her health went in while she was down, the chandelier fell on her every time (8 %), and the bots broke one to three of her songs a fight (none of 22 at 5 %).
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): the nav probe (97 paths: zone to zone, every trace point and dimmer, the meeting's walk and its hatch, the power boxes, the winch, every perch to the floor, cover points, every spawn to its zone; three doorways and stairs fixed, then all found); the logic checks on one page (t96u: the Listener deaf to a still or crouch-walking human at 8 m, hearing running feet there and a shot at 25 m, not a shot through the shut doors, going for the noise and giving up 2.5 s after; the song pulling the player toward her, broken by 4 %, adding 30 stagger, its burst knocking down within 8 m; a speaker down shortening the song, a shot taking a speaker's health, its power box cutting it in 3 s; the chandelier on her — 8 % —, nobody left inside its wreck, the wreck a box in the world; the embrace catching the one marked and 3 % freeing them; 70 %: phase 2, invulnerable, up a perch; 30 %: phase 3 in the dark, shrouded, down to the floor; bsPack / bsUnpack; a wipe replaying phase 3 in the dark with the props as they were; stage 3's meeting on the catwalk, the retreat at 85 % and the hatch; every radio line in both languages; the twelve new sounds rendered, none silent); pictures: the map from 16 places (lit, the backstage brighter after the first look), LORELEI in each pose (idle, singing, the bullhorn at her mouth, calling, reaching, holding, lunging, perched, bowing, down), the Listener listening, a speaker on and blown, the chandelier hanging and down, a bar on the stage, the dark with and without the flashlight; schema.sql on local Postgres 16: fresh, run again, from v6.95's schema with chapter 6 records (kept) and run again: qz_me epmax 7, a chapter 7 ticket and clear → ep_records (A) and the hall of fame row, a ticket for 8 is 7's, ep 10 refused, on hard a 190-second clear does not count and 205 does; regressions on the release build: every chapter boots (1–7: the first stage, then the boss stage with its boss alive; eight cards with chapter 7's S2 tag, options 1–7 + free), the chapter list at 1280×720 (chapter 7 locked, then open), the lobby with the global chat, the multiplayer screen on a phone, the four pages' CSP, four modes' smoke run, v6.94–95's sound tests, the StarTrack's.

## v6.95 — 사플 2: every sound in 3D, another's reloads and switches heard, a sound direction display (audio.js AU.at · can3 · play's 3D count; game.js othSnd, actorWeapons, equip; net.js netPuppet; weapons.js; startrack.js stPupRel; sndviz.js new, built before quick; ui.js CFG.sndViz; lobby.js options, patch notes; newsban.js; no network change)
- The owner picked 3 (and 5, chapter 7, next) from the suggested next patches.
- AU.at (every positional sound: shots, zombies, blasts, impacts — 300 call sites) goes through the head too: an HRTF PannerNode at the sound while there are 3D voices to spare (AU.can3: the option on, the browser able, fewer than 32 playing — 16 on a touch device; each one is a convolution; counted when it starts, given back when it ends), its level and filters as before. Without one: the stereo pan as before, and what is behind (cos < −.2) under 6.5 kHz. AU.stepAt keeps to the same cap.
- Another's gear (othSnd: AU.at at the body + a ping for the display): a bot's magazine out at the reload's start (.55, 25 m; was .4 at 15 m) and in at its end (.5, 25 m, when rounds went in), a shell at a time (.45, 22 m); a weapon drawn (a human's, not a claw: .45, 20 m). Another page's body the same from its flags: a weapon drawn when its weapon changes, the magazine out when the reload flag comes, a shell each time its shell clock runs out, the magazine in when the flag goes with the reload still its own. The StarTrack's reload on another page plays the sheet's five sounds at their times (stPupRel, nyLater) instead of the engine's magazine.
- 소리 방향 표시 (CFG.sndViz, Options → 오디오, off): SNDV.ping from another's steps, push-offs and landings (stepSnd), shots (fireGun), gear (othSnd, stPupRel, stReload) — only a foe (foe(): the other team, or in 대인전 the other side) within the sound's own range; one arc a body and kind at its newest place, up to 24. Drawn from HUD.update on a 280 px canvas over the crosshair: an arc at radius 104 px on the side it came from (up = ahead), ±.2–.3 rad (wider when further), white a step, orange a shot, blue gear; alpha the nearness × what is left of .7 s.
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): AU.at 3D under the cap (31) and stereo at it (32), behind under 6.5 kHz without HRTF; a bot's reload — magazine out and in in 3D, a pump gun's three shells, a weapon drawn; a puppet's weapon change, reload out / in, the StarTrack's reload with the sheet's five sounds in order and no magazine; the display — an enemy's step on the right drawn on the right, a teammate's not at all, a shot from behind at the bottom, all gone after .8 s, and on a real frame round the crosshair; a bot round with every sound rendered (397 positional sounds, 314 of them 3D, the cap reached, the count back to 0 three seconds after, at most 38 voices, 312 pings, no error); regressions on the release build: every chapter boots, the lobby with the global chat, the multiplayer screen on a phone, the four pages' CSP, four modes' smoke run, v6.94's footstep tests, the StarTrack's B and sound tests.

## v6.94 — 사플: footsteps you can find someone by (audio.js AU.stepAt · AU.listen · play's o.p3; game.js footstep / stepSnd / zStepK, jumps and landings; net.js the puppets' jumps and landings; hskill.js and lyrebird.js through AU.stepAt; main.js; ui.js CFG.stepVol · CFG.hrtf; lobby.js options; newsban.js; no network change)
- The owner's word: 「발소리 추가해줘 사플 가능하게」. Footsteps were there (by surface, a zombie's zstep, none from a walking or crouching human, replayed for other pages' bodies) but quiet and short — AU.at's (1 − d/28)^1.6 / (1 + .06d) put a step 20 m off at −41 dB — and only left / right.
- AU.stepAt (every step, push-off and landing that is not the player's own): its level 2 / max(2, d) — 6 dB less each time the distance doubles from 2 m — times (1 − (d/R)^4) down to nothing at the range (40 m a human, 45 a zombie): 1.32 / .65 / .33 / .16 / .08 at 2 / 5 / 10 / 20 / 30 m; a lowpass from 18 kHz down to 3.5 kHz at the range (air) and more room (the reverb send + .25 d/R); through a wall (losClear) 750 Hz and half as loud; late by its travel time. Its direction: an HRTF PannerNode at the step (its own distance model off: the level above), the head (ctx.listener) set from the camera every frame (AU.listen, after AU.L in main.js; one HRTF panner made early so the browser has its head data before the first step); with 3D sound off, the stereo pan (±.9) and what is behind (cos < −.2) under 5.5 kHz.
- Levels: a human's step vol 1.7 (× .6 a jump's push-off, × 1–2 a landing by its speed / 5, at pitch 1.1 / .85), a zombie's zstep 1.8 × its build (w = hw × h / .54: pitch 1 − .3(w − 1), level .7 + .3w — the Heavy .87 / 1.13, the Light 1.02 / .97; bosses up to .7 / 1.36). The player's own as before (.4, a zombie .35) and now a soft step when its own normal jump lands. CFG.stepVol scales them all (Options → 오디오: 발소리 크기, 50–200 %).
- Who: as before, everyone on the ground moving faster than 1.5 m/s (a walking or crouching human silent); a bot's push-off from its jump, a landing from its impact speed (> 3 m/s); another page's bodies from their flags — the push-off when the ground flag goes and the newest sample is higher, a landing after .3 s in the air — NET_DELAY late, as their drawn motion is. The out-of-breath skill's heavy steps and the Lyrebird's decoy radios step the same way.
- Options → 오디오: 발소리 크기 (50–200 %), 입체음향 (HRTF) — 헤드폰 권장 (on), a line on what carries how far. The new-content banner's button opens that tab (UI.act 'sndopt').
- Tests (one page each, offline; no two-page multiplayer test here — the owner's): a human bot's step at 2–41 m in front, behind, left and right — the levels above, none past 40 m, the filters and delays; without HRTF the pan ±.9 and 5.5 kHz behind; through a wall 750 Hz and half; 50 / 200 %; a walking or crouching human silent, a walking zombie not; five zombie builds (the Heavy .87 / 1.13); the player's own step plain (no 3D); a bot jumping in place — push-off and landing; a puppet's push-off and landing from its flags (.134 s late at 8 m); the head as the game sets it (AU.listen, a camera turned 90° left, a click from four sides through HRTF): ahead L = R, behind as loud but duller (high-frequency share .45 against 1.07), to a side 12 dB more in its ear; real voices through the graph with and without HRTF (made, played, ended); a live bot round with every sound rendered (131 HRTF steps, at most 12 voices, no error); the audio tab (slider, toggle, cancel puts them back) and the banner's button; regressions on the release build: every chapter boots, the lobby with the global chat, the multiplayer screen on a phone, the four pages' CSP, four modes' smoke run, the StarTrack's B and sound tests.

## v6.93 — 스타트랙 (StarTrack), a crate shotgun built to the owner's design sheet (startrack.js new, after m4dk2.js in the build; net.js NET_VER; shop.js SHOP_NOTSOLD; supply.js; lobby.js; newsban.js; schema.sql gun_prices; multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it)
- The owner's word: 「이거 적힌 코드상 그대로 적용시켜줘 총 하나 추가하는거야 샷건」 with startrack_final.json (its jsonPriority: the sheet's structured values over its prose).
- The sheet as data (const STK: every number as written — stats, firstDraw, points, pivots, materials_new, the ammo rule, A, B with its tiers / beam / lasers / timeline, the motions, the reload rule and timeline, the sounds, glow, drawFx, fpPlacement, buyDesc); its 446 parts unchanged as rows (ST_ROWS: x y z w h d material rx rz tag). 'red' is drawn in the view model as 'stRed' (the same pixels under its own alpha, 236) so the draw's red flare finds it. GUNS.startrack (the view model: the drum and the four tubes / arms tagged, the arms on their pivots) · startrackw (world, A) · startrackwb (world, B: the motions baked). The five new materials painted with the glow pattern (atlas 172 / 256).
- A (fireGun → tbShot, W.rail + stA): one round from the next barrel in the sheet's order (UL, UR, LL, LR, three each, from UL again after a reload), its hit rule as written — one line from the eye, no random spread; the part it reaches takes aMode.fullHitDamage (body 240 · head 372 · arms / legs 144) × the falloff (straight lines between 3 m 1, 5 m .7, 10 m .35, 15 m .2, 20 m .1; .1 after), up to 110 m. The barrel that fired kicks back 3.5 cm and comes home in .13 s, its flash at its end (.11 m, #FFA050, .13 s), nine orange lines out along the aim (only drawn); the engine's muzzle flash, light and barrel smoke left out. The 'f' event's last number is the barrel (1–4): the other pages flash the same one.
- B (right click, .55 s, smoothstep): the four tubes out .05 / .05 and the arms turned .45 rad about their pivots, in to the point in front; the left hand .05 lower. A pull once it is all the way in charges by the rounds left — 1–5 white .4 s, 6–11 orange .7 s, 12 red 1 s — four lasers from the barrels to the point (.012 × the progress, .5 through the beam), the gun's light in the tier's colour, the heat 1 → 2; at the end of the charge one hitscan line from the eye: no falloff, no piercing, no wallbang, the first wall or zombie stops it; it takes bMode.damagePerRound (head 250 · body 180 · arms / legs 110) × the rounds, and every round is gone. The beam (the tier's width, 30 % thinner by its end; drawn from the gun's start point to where the eye's line stopped, 60 m when nothing) and the cross flash (three boxes, .4 + .6 × width / .046) for .45 / .6 / .9 s; the heat 2 → 1; then back to A by itself, silent. Moving is allowed. The weapon's name on the HUD says · B 레이저.
- The reload (2.41 s, the sheet's eight phases): up (pitch .45, roll −.3, lift .05), the drum pulled .28 m and thrown away at .71 s with whatever was in it (in the world's frame from where it was pulled to: the sheet's velocity, gravity, spin, 1.3 s), a new one from below, full at 1.61 s from the reserve (to 12; the reserve pays for what goes in), two taps, down. R in B leaves B first (silent), then reloads; an empty pull asks for a reload as in A; no dry click (an empty gun with no reserve does nothing). Put away after the throw, the drum stays gone (0 in the gun).
- The draw: up from .35 m below and −.25 rad in .7 s (a quick draw in its shorter time), the glow pulse (.43 s up, .77 s down) — the glowing parts .55 + 1.5h + 2.5p, the gun's light .4 + 3h + 5p (#FF3320 at the muzzle, falling off over ~30 cm), the red parts' flare 1.6p (h = the heat, else B's progress). The first draw is the sheet's (spin, +.4 s, heavy .6) without its motor sound.
- Sounds: the sheet's referenceImplementation (tone / noise / hum) with the events' layer values, rendered once into the bank (OfflineAudioContext, mono; noise new for each of up to three variants) and kept as rendered — clipped at ±1 as an output would, not brought to a common peak — then played at its own level and pitch straight to the volume: none of the game mix's EQ, compressor, limiter or reverb (a blast's muffle, AU.shock, still dulls them).
