Headless (the mock: qz_match_start, qz_nick_free, blocks, nickname rules, referral requests, p_token recorded / refused on __MOCK_NOTOKEN): x9 — sign-up refused for ALPHA after Alpha, setNick taken / short texts, the ticket asked once with mode, difficulty and G.mt, tok on both stats and in the claim body, an old database's p_token dropped (claim and round), no ticket on __MOCK_NOTICKET or the range, blocking from the friends window (the row's confirm, 0 friends, the list, unblocking), the invite popup's request text and the inviter's incoming request, nickAsk for 6 digits. Movement: x11 (a fake link through netOnState: walking 7 m/s free, a 50 m teleport → 16 m then 9 m/s, a speed hack held, +20 m up → 6, falling free, a shove credited, a respawn's late state held, a dead player unchecked) and x10 (every player-like actor of accelerated bot matches through ngMove at 30 Hz: mut, orig, tdm, ffa, bomb, race, every chapter — about 2 million states, no state held back; also with no shove credit at all, after the under-the-map rule). Regressions: smoke, markup (x2), host checks (x3), snapshot rows (x8), deletion (x6a / x6b), the tutorial, CSP (pages and sockets), the admin page.

## v6.73 — blood & gore locked at 15세 (util.js GORE, ui.js CFG, lobby.js options; no server change)
- The owner's 2번 (with the weapon names, which wait for his OK on a name table: the first table was lost from the working record, a new one goes to him first). For the public release 15세 is the most: GORE.init / GORE.set read anything but 0 as 1, CFG.gore is put to 1 unless it is 0, and the options' 피 표현 offers 15세 (순화) and 피 끄기 only. The lv-2 drawing (dismemberment, bursting heads, pools, torn skins) stays in the code, unreachable.
- Tests: a saved 원본 (qz_cfg gore 2) loads as 15세 (GORE.lv 1, CFG.gore 1), the options show the two choices, GORE.set(2) gives 1.

## v6.72 — security patch, part 2 (net.js host checks, flood limits, host migration; account.js / shop.js re-sign-in to delete; schema.sql qz_account_delete, referrals)
- The owner's 2차 ("1번 다하고 2번 ㄱㄱ": this, then the weapon names). From the audit of v6.70: hit claims trusted in the zombie modes (C1), NaN in claims and effects (C2), no per-link message limit (H1), the host migration that one page could steer (H2), claws and effects with no range (M1, M2); deleting an account with a token alone (DB H4) and the invite cap undone by deleting the invited accounts (DB H3).
- The host's checks (net.js, before HOSTH): ngTrack (NET.frameEnd, host, in a match) keeps every actor's last 0.8 s of positions (every 1/30 s, a ring of 24 with the eye height) and, for a remote player, when each weapon was last in its inventory or reported in hand (ngW) and when it was last a living human (ngHum); netOnState notes a grenade seen thrown (ngThr). ngClaim(src, t, h): the weapon index an integer naming a WPN entry; the weapon held within 8 s (16 s for what flies or lingers), a grenade's blast only within 8 s of a throw; the shooter a living human within 1 s (8 s for a blast / splash / thrown); 대인전: foe + PVP.claimOk as before; the zombie modes: a zombie target, damage ≤ ngProf(w).cap (the weapon's biggest damage-like number anywhere in its entry — dmg, spDmg, cmbDmg, swDmg, bayDmg, hsDmg, gDmg, nested gMode… — at least 60, × max(head multiplier, 3) × 2.2; a blast ≥ 1,000), and one time budget a player (ngSpend: a claim costs 1 / per s, per = shots a second (the biggest rpm-like number; a blade 1 / its fastest swing) × pellets / spFan × pen / pierce / fan / slugs (× its widest cleave) × 2, 24 targets a shot for splash weapons — proj, cone, flame, air, cluster, slam, spFlame, special, grenades — a blast at least 48 a second; 1.5 s held at a time); every mode: the target within reach of the shooter's recent positions (bullet 110 m + 6, W.range for the railgun, a blade's longest reach + 3, 170 m for splash / blasts) and, for a plain bullet (pistol / smg / rifle / mg / shotgun / sniper with no pen, pierce, rail, wallK, trans or alt) unless the target is very big (epRay / xRay / boss / ZBIG / over 0.9 m wide or 3.2 m tall), a clear line (losClear) from one of the shooter's eye positions to the hit point — when that point is by one of the target's positions — or to its chest or head. The numbers taken are bounded: direction ±1, knockback ≤ 2 × the weapon's biggest kb-like number + 2 (16 for a blast), lift ≤ its biggest up-like number + 3 (8 for a blast), stagger ≤ 3, the hit point finite. NG.rej counts the reasons. Claims go 48 a message from a client (the host takes 64).
- Claws: a living zombie past its first-strike lock, 4 a second, within CLAW_R heavy + the target's half width + 2.2 m of the human in the last 0.8 s, in sight. Effects (eff): within 120 m; a shove (kx, kz ≤ 40 m/s) or a lift (≤ 22) on anyone (a zombie bomb launches zombies), statuses only on the other side (freeze ≤ 5 s, root / stagger / dizzy / shriek ≤ 4 s, grab / hold ≤ 2 s, burn ≤ 6 s with a real weapon, fields other files add such as THANATOS-9's slow ≤ 10 s). pong's timestamp must be a number. A client's state with anything but numbers in its 13 slots is dropped whole (positions within 2 km, speeds 60 m/s); at most 48 effects ride one state, each ≤ 24 entries of numbers / short words. A client skips a snapshot row with anything but numbers in its first ten.
- Links (netRx): on the host a message over 64 KiB is dropped unread (counts as 200), about 300 events a second (600 at once) and 90 states a second get through; the dropped count fades over about 4 s and past 1,200 the link is closed with bye 'flood' (T mpFlood on that page; its player goes on as a bot). A client takes 512 KiB a message from its host; the queue holds 5,000.
- Host migration (H2): the pages an asker found gone count only when it asks from further down the line than this page (so its word can make this page or one ahead of it the host, never itself); migto is followed only to a page ahead of the one that sent it, else this page marks that one passed and goes on in its own order. NET_VER r11 (everyone in a room on this version).
- Not changed (accepted for P2P, said to the owner): positions are not checked (speed / teleport), every page receives everything (wallhack / ESP), a remote player's crate / EH guns are trusted (loadout.js crateOK), the claude.ai room transport's self-chosen keys.
- Account deletion (schema.sql qz_account_delete): only a session signed into in the last 10 minutes — auth.sessions.created_at of the JWT's session_id; where that cannot be read, auth.users.last_sign_in_at — else 'reauth'. The game: an e-mail account's password step is a new sign-in already; a Google / Kakao account (no password) gets 「Google로 다시 로그인」 when this browser's last fresh sign-in (qz_signAt: password, OAuth return, e-mail link, sign-up) is over 9.5 minutes old; ACC.oauth(p, 'del') notes qz_after and, back from the provider, the delete window opens by itself (ACC.takeAfter, 10 minutes). The mock keeps each session's sign-in time (born, kept on refresh; window.__MOCK_SES_AGE ages them).
- Invites (schema.sql): profiles.ref_paid counts the invite rewards an account has been paid, for good (filled from the rewarded rows on the upgrade); qz_ref_check counts the cap as the larger of it and the rewarded rows and adds one when it pays; a suspended inviter is not paid; qz_ref_info / qz_me show the same count. Run schema.sql.
- Tests: the host handlers driven straight (a bot made a remote player on a fake link): a rifle body hit and head hit taken, 5,000 refused (dmg), a gun never held refused (held), bad weapon indexes (weapon), a grenade blast before / after a throw seen (throw / taken), junk numbers bounded (the hit stands), 60 pistol claims at once → 20 taken (rate), a target behind a wall refused even with a hit point said to be in the open (los), 300 m away (far), a human target in a zombie mode (team); a claw from 40 m refused, from 2 m taken; effects: a freeze from a zombie's page on a zombie dropped, a shove bounded to 40 m/s, a dizzy on a human bounded to 4 s; a state with a word in it dropped, a good one sampled; junk effects riding a state dropped; 700 events → 600, a flood kept up → the link closed and its player a bot; an 80 KiB message dropped. Every human bot's real hits sent through the checks as claims in a real match (Mutation 428 claims, TDM 66, the chapter 3 scenario 40): no damage, rate, reach, held or sight refusals — only a few 'dead' (shots in the frame a bot came back, fixed: a living human now counts) and 대인전's own claimOk. A client taking snapshot rows (hp, armour, the stats block; a row with a word skipped). Deleting: an e-mail account (password → deleted), a Google account with an hour-old sign-in (the window shows 「Google로 다시 로그인」, the server answers reauth), back from Google (the window opens by itself, deleted). Regression: the v6.71 markup / CSP checks, the tutorial all the way, Mutation / TDM / 폭파미션 / scenario starts, no CSP violations. Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.71 (rewarded invites counted into ref_paid) and a re-run; an old session / no session id refused, a fresh one deletes; auth.sessions gone → last_sign_in_at (old refused, fresh deletes); cap 2: two paid, the third not, a paid one deleted, the next still not; a suspended inviter not paid. Multiplayer between real pages not tested here (the owner tests it).

## v6.71 — security patch, part 1 (net.js, ui.js, lobby.js, pvp.js, bomb.js, scenario.js, episode.js, shop.js; build.py CSP; vercel.json headers; admin.js; schema.sql)
- From the owner's audit request (보안 · 치팅 점검), the first of three parts he okayed ("1차 ㄱㄱ"): markup from another player, the New Year letters with no daily limit, unbounded text in the coin log, the owner's address in these notes. Part 2 (the host checking what clients claim in every mode, account deletion and the referral count, message rate limits) and part 3 (server match tokens and the rest) wait.
- What another page sends (net.js): netRx runs netScrub on every message before anything reads it — the characters < > " ' and the backtick taken out of every string at any depth, a chat line's text (t 'chat', m) the one exception (always shown escaped), past 8 levels dropped. netHandle dispatches only own properties of HOSTH / CLIH. The client handlers make the host's values the type and size they should be: netCleanLob / netCleanCfg (keys `[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,23}`, 48 at most; numbers finite, strings `[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]` ≤ 24; mode one of NET_MODES, map a MAPDEFS key), netCleanPl (name ≤ 16, skin from HSKINS, zombie class from ZLIST, ping −1..99999), netCleanRo (≤ 96 rows of ≤ 9), netCleanPlan (round number, placements as numbers); hosts / infect / rv zombie classes via netZ; end's score as two integers; netOnSnap's g, hp / armour / level / money and the stats block (kills, infections, deaths as integers, ping −1..99999). Scenario / chapter values the host sends (lives, wave, best, end record, an upgrade's weapon and level) are numbers / checked keys. A relay reason (room kind) must be a short lowercase word; relayWhyT shows only its known texts.
- Where they are shown (defence in depth): esc() also escapes ' and the backtick; the round result card, results, scoreboard, the 대인전 / 폭파미션 boards and the level HUD print numbers through |0; the map thumbnail's data-m escaped; the waiting room's ping |0. The unused waiting room in net.js (UI.mpLobby / UI.mpRender, overridden by lobby.js) is gone.
- CSP (build.py with_csp): the standalone index, admin.html, privacy.html and terms.html get a `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy">`: script-src only the SHA-256 of each inline script (index 3: three, peerjs, the game; admin 1; legal 1 — the build stops if the count is off), style 'self' 'unsafe-inline', img / media 'self' data: blob:, font 'self' data:, connect-src 'self' + the Supabase URL read from sb.js (+ https / wss 0.peerjs.com on the game page), worker-src blob: (net.js's timer worker), object-src 'none', base-uri 'none', form-action 'self'. Inline event handlers can never run, whatever markup gets in. The claude.ai artifact (dist/index.html) gets none. vercel.json: frame-ancestors 'none' + X-Frame-Options DENY (no framing — to embed the game somewhere, that site's origin goes in frame-ancestors), nosniff, strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy off for camera / mic / location / payment / usb.
- Admin page: the sign-in (qz_adm_ses) lives in sessionStorage — this tab only, gone when it closes; an older copy in localStorage moves over once and is wiped. 게임 설정 → 해독기: 「하루 근·하·신·년 글자 상한」 (ny_day_cap).
- Letters (schema.sql): qz_ny_add keeps at most gacha_config.ny_day_cap letters (default 60) a Korean day on top of the per-match quota (profiles.ny_day / ny_day_n); answers day_left and day_cap. The game (shop.js) toasts 「오늘 받을 수 있는 글자를 다 받았어요 (하루 N개)」 when the day's cap is what kept some back (an older server without day_left: no toast). The mock models it (window.__MOCK_NYCAP).
- Text into the database: qz_claim / qz_round_claim keep the coin_log mode as `[a-z]` ≤ 12; qz_invite keeps map `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` ≤ 24 and mode `[a-z]` ≤ 12; qz_err_report (anyone may call it), qz_inquiry_send, qz_dm_send and qz_claim's match id cut their text to a few times the kept length before any regex work (over-long messages are still refused as before). Run schema.sql.
- NOTES.md no longer carries the owner's private address.
- The local harness (play.py, not committed) polls __ready with evaluate: wait_for_function evaluates a string in the page, which the CSP refuses.
- Tests: two layers of the multiplayer markup (straight into CLIH: lobby map, ping, a bad skin / class; the stats block through netOnSnap; a scenario 'scf' through netRx) → nothing runs, values come out typed; netScrub on deep / chat messages; markup injected straight into the page → blocked by the CSP (script-src-attr); no CSP violation on boot, through the full tutorial, Mutation / TDM / 폭파미션 / scenario starts and on the admin, privacy and terms pages; fetch and WebSocket reach only the hosts each page allows (DevTools network block to tell CSP from network); the admin session move, same-tab reload, new tab → sign-in, the cap field; the letters toast (cap hit, cap zero, normal, older server). Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.70 and a re-run; 8-letter matches → 60 then 0, the next day again, the owner sets 10 (8 + 2), a negative refused, staff refused; modes, invite map / mode, error report, DM and inquiry lengths; anon-callable functions unchanged. Multiplayer between real pages not tested here (the owner tests it).

## v6.70 — 기초 훈련, the tutorial (tutorial.js, built last; range.js keys; lobby.js range card button; newsban.js feature buttons; account.js me.tut; schema.sql qz_tut_claim)
- The owner picked it from the launch list (2 of 1, 2: the weapon renames wait for his OK on the name plan). A first-time player had nothing that showed the controls or the rules.
- It is the shooting range with TUT.on: Main.startTut (hides the lobby and the news banner, TUT.pending through Main.startRange) → the RANGE.begin hook runs TUT.setup: the range's record panel, its K / J keys (range.js) and moving targets are off, chat is off (CHAT.where null: Enter belongs to the tutorial), the top bar says 기초 훈련, the player gets AK-47 / USP / knife. #hTut (in #app, z 8 over the touch layer, pointer-events only on its button) walks through TUT_STEPS, each done only when it is really done: look (|Δyaw| + |Δpitch|/2 ≥ 2.4 rad), move (6 m on the ground), jump + crouch, 10 hits on the range dummies, 3 headshots, a reload (the magazine cut to 3 on entering; started and finished), a pick from the buy menu (Main.buyItem hook: the hand, the bag or the armour changed; the menu closes itself), the knife (a brute training dummy put behind the line at z 3.4, 3,000 hp, standing; out + a knife hit), a grenade (2 HE given; thrown, 1.4 s), three training zombies (rager, 330 hp, at 36–46 m walking up at .5–.62 of their speed to the player's x in front of the counters; they never strike), the rules (T('rules') + the human skills + zombie keys; Enter / 계속). A step done: 「✓ 좋아요!」 for .9 s. Enter (a button too, on a phone the only way) skips after 10 s; skipped steps show – at the end. Keyboard and phone wording differ (TOUCH.on: the stick, drag to look, the ↑ ↓ ↻ buttons, 구매, the weapon bar, auto-fire when it is on). Markers (.tutMk, not .off — that class hides anything) over the dummy and the zombies with the distance; behind the camera they sit at the bottom edge (「↓ 뒤에 있어요」). The panel hides under the pause menu and the buy menu.
- The end: LS qz_tutDone; #tutEnd holds the game as Main.overlay 'tut' (letting the mouse go does not pause; the game's keys wait; Enter / Esc → the lobby): the steps, the reward line, 「사격장에서 계속 연습」 (the plain range again: the tutorial's actors removed, the panel back, movers on) or 「로비로」. Leaving half-way (pause → 나가기) cleans up through RANGE.end.
- Reward: qz_tut_claim (schema.sql; profiles.tut_done; 1,000 coins once per account, coin_log 'tutorial', admin page 「기초 훈련 보상」; banned accounts get qz_guard's error; anon cannot). qz_me tut → ACC.me.tut (undefined on an older database: nothing is asked). Signed in: claimed at the end (+1,000 / already had it / failed → tried again on the next load). A guest is told to sign in; on the next sign-in an ACC.sub hook claims it once (toast). Run schema.sql.
- Lobby: the range card's 「▶ 기초 훈련」 (gold, pulsing, +1,000 badge while it is to be had; gone after it is done). A player who has never played (recGet().g 0), has not done it and was not asked on this browser gets #tutAsk once on 빠른 시작 / 방 만들기 / 멀티플레이 (기초 훈련 시작 / 바로 할게요 — the latter goes on with what was clicked). The news banner's first card is the tutorial with its own button (newsban.js: e.act / e.btn → UI.act).
- Tests: deterministic stepping (gameUpdate with the loop stopped) through all eleven steps → the end → more practice → the lobby (button plain); a touch run 844×390 (skip and continue tapped through the touch layer, the end window, the lobby); English texts; a signed-in end (the reward +1,000, me.tut); the real loop with real keys (W, Space, C, R, B → 2 → 1, 3, 4, Deadshot 6) for the input paths; leaving from the pause menu (actors, markers, panel gone), the plain range afterwards; the question once (not after playing, not after finishing); a guest's reward on sign-in once; an older database. Local Postgres 16: fresh, upgrade from v6.69 and a re-run; the claim twice (1,000 then 0), banned, anon refused. No page errors.

## v6.69 — M4A1 다크나이트-2, a crate gun built to the owner's design sheet (m4dk2.js after m4dk; schema.sql gun_prices row; m4dk.js dkOwnN)
- The owner's word: his sheet (id m4dk, name M4A1 다크나이트 — the v6.41 transcendence gun, which stays) made as a new weapon named M4A1 다크나이트-2, the design applied as written. id m4dk2; special (kind and buy-menu category), slot 1, hold rifle, a crate gun (WPN.crate, CRATE_GUNS — now nine, the same share of the 35 % —, the buy menu's special list for an account that owns one, lent in the range, never in 대인전), SHOP_NOTSOLD, gun_prices ('m4dk2', 0, not sold): run schema.sql (checked on Postgres 16 as an upgrade of v6.68, again, and fresh; an admin-set price elsewhere kept).
- Model: the sheet's 561 parts as data rows ([c, s, material, rx, rz, fillet or null = the engine's default, tag]), numbers unchanged (three decimals at most); tags chg (the charging handle, also the rear sight), mag (35 parts) and ghorn (the eye-and-horn structure: never moves). The sheet's five materials (DK2_MDEF: knGold brushed, knSpike noise, knLine brushed, knPlate brushed, knEye glow 232; gloss ≥ .3 → 248..254); 151 of 256 atlas slots. Points as the sheet (muzzle [0, .045, −.739], sup [−.005, −.01, −.33]); no pivots. View model 34.5k triangles; the world model m4dk2w the same parts as plain boxes (r 0: 6.7k). VM_POS: the M4A1 다크나이트's (not in the sheet).
- A: dmg 90, hs 150 / 90, limbK 50 / 90 (head 150 · body 90 · arms and legs 50), 780 rpm full auto (720 at 60 fps: the engine's frame steps), mag 50 + 1 (ch 1) / 300, spread [.006, .04, .11], rec [.017, .01], kb 3.4, stag .6, reload 2.5, draw .8, speed .9, shell (rifle), kickX the rifles' [.42, .6, .3] (the sheet: a slight lift). The sheet's buyDesc (W.bd).
- B (right click, W.alt 'dk2'): dk2Set — a.dk2T .25 s in which the actorWeapons hook clears fire and reload; then DK2.WB (dmg 180 → 300 / 180 / 100, rec [.006, .004], spread [.004, .03, .08], kickX × .35, sound dk2fireb) at 600 rpm (nextFire 60 / 600 − 1e−4: no dropped frame), two rounds a shot (noMag, then mag − 2; with one left B reloads: the hook shows the game an empty magazine for that frame and puts the round back, relCh kept). dk2Shot (tbShot hook, WB.rail + dk2p): rayCast to the first wall, dkLine (m4dk.js: head / body / legs / arms, the bosses' own volumes) up to 3 zombies, × .75 each next one, stag .6 on all, kb 3.4, a 퍽 (dk2pierce) where it goes on through; dk2Beam: a white core and a pale glow to where it stopped, faded by TB over .6 s. maxSpeed × .85. The view: Main.camera hook narrows to 52° over .2 s (the sheet's zoom [52] — right click is the mode switch, so not W.zoom). Measured: B 300 → 225 → 169 (heads) and 180 → 135 → 101 (bodies), the fourth untouched, stagger 1.1 s on all three; A 150 / 90 / 50.
- Flash (the sheet's sizes): the first-person flash scaled per shot (face .24 m → .05 / .04 m, side .2 m → .09 / .12 m), orange / white-yellow, its light with it (dk2Flash); in the world FX.muzzle is swapped for the shot (dk2Muzzle: a few sprites along the barrel).
- The eyes: the view model's material for this gun gets FS_VM with uGlow (glowing texels × 1.9 in B, over the .25 s switch: a.dk2K); third person: the actor's gun material uEmisA the same (ensureGun hook, back to 1 on any other gun). The structure itself never moves.
- Reload (rlBuild hook → rlDk2, the sheet's timeline over 2.5 s, either way: no extra charge from empty): the hand at the magazine and it out a little (0–.35 s), dropped and tumbling to the floor (.35–.8, gone there with a 쿵), a fresh one from the belt (.8–1.5), in (1.5–1.9, 찰칵), one tap (톡) and back (1.9–2.5). First draw: GUN_DESIGN charge, side u (the left), x .5, d .06, heavy .3, roll .15.
- Passive: the Deadshot +1 s (m4dk.js dkOwns wrapped: either Dark Knight, not two seconds for both; this one owned by the account or carried); dkOwnN names the one the note mentions.
- Network: ['dk2', id, 0 | 1]; a B shot is replayed from the 'f' event while that player's B is on; equip / turned / NY.clear / death put it back to A. Bots: B for a zombie over 10 m, back to A under 6 m (1.2 s apart). The weapon's name gets · B 관통 in B.
- Sounds (the sheet's words): dk2fire (the M4A1's, a .6 s tail), dk2fireb (the M249's, lower, .5 s, steady), dk2on / dk2off (a .25 s 우웅, the off one backwards), dk2pierce, dk2draw (leather and metal, the handle's 철컥 at .25 s, a low hum), dk2mrel / dk2mdrop / dk2min / dk2tap.
- Tests: studio renders (both sides, front, rear, top, the eyes, the muzzle, the player's view, the world model); first person: A and B (the eyes brighter), the flashes, the trail, the reload frame by frame, the first draw; unit: 12 shots a second in A, 50 → 51 on a reload with rounds left (2.5 s), the switch (nothing fired in it, .25 s), speed 4.64 → 3.94, the view 74 → 52 over .2 s, B 10 shots a second taking 20 rounds, the pierce numbers above, one round left → reload → 51, the Deadshot 5 → 6 s; a 150 s Mutation with every bot carrying it (23 switches, 404 B shots, the world model's eyes, reloads, no errors); 20 000 crate picks (each of the nine about 11 %). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.68 — 「이태훈 딕건」 rebuilt to the owner's second design sheet (janus2.js rewritten; janus.js: per-weapon timings, calm; no server change)
- The owner's word: the sheet applied as written, nothing of ours changed in it. Its id / name (janus1 / 야누스-1) are the template's: the gun stays janus2 / 이태훈 딕건. The v6.55 build's own changes are gone (Christ moved back, the bridge, the longer barrel with a bore, our Mary). Only the id, the name, the cost, VM_POS, kickX, the blast radius, the chapter ×2.5, the HUD (ring, crosshair classes) and the grenade models are kept from before — things the sheet does not cover.
- Parts: the sheet's 304 parts as data rows ([c, s, material, rx, ry, rz, fillet, tag index, ax]), numbers unchanged (J2_P). Materials: the sheet's 14 (J2_MDEF: three tones, pattern brushed / engraved / glow / grain / noise / stripes, gloss, scratch — hood_black is defined but no part uses it); the old j2Blue / j2Gold / j2Green / j2Skin / j2Glow are gone. Alpha: glow 232; gloss ≥ .3 → max(248, 239 + 15 g) (lower would light itself in the view model); under .3 matte. 146 of 256 atlas slots.
- Points and hinge: the sheet's (muzzle [0, .03, −.21], pivots.break [0, .015, −.02] = G.hinge). Stats: dmg 120, rpm 60 single, mag 1, res 0, spread [.01, .04, .08], rec [.3, .08] (the game's largest: the next is .16), kb .8, stag .5, hs 1.5, reload 1, draw .9, speed .94; the first draw (break, x .3, d .15, side t, heavy .7, roll .4); buyDesc.
- res 0 with reloadType immediate = it loads itself: after every shot (mag 0, not in the B form, not drawing / frozen / pinned) the reload starts at once (VM.reload: rlBrk1 — the barrel breaks 45°, the case is lifted clear of the frame's face and flung back over the shoulder, the free hand brings a grenade from the belt to the chamber, the flick of the wrist); am.res is kept at 1 so a reload always has one; the HUD shows / ∞. The supply / ammo box / range top-ups of v6.55 are gone (nothing to top up).
- First-person rig (j2Rig): 'mag' = break_inner on the hinge; under it j2U (break_outer_up / top, scope_part), j2DL / j2DR (dn, bot), j2T (thorn_base, thorn_s, thornB: one mesh, each part's box built once about its centre and placed again whenever the growth changes, j2ThornSet), shell; the figures under j2U on the door [0, .07325, −.025]: j2J (Christ), j2E (his eyes, glow_white), j2EY (the same eyes in glow_jesus, with two additive halo sprites — the view model only), j2M (Mary and her eyes).
- The B form (VMX.janus2 / j2State): u from the right click; g (thorns, halves) = smooth(u / .5); f (the figures) = smooth((u − .3) / .55); 0.85 s in all (JNW oT), no firing until then (janus.js's lock), then JN.B 5 s. The halves: up +5 mm (with the rails, the sight, the figures), the lower ones ∓4 mm x, −5 mm y; thorns and vines by their root's height: t = (y − .030) / 9.5 mm clamped ±1 (the big thorns' roots sit at .041 / .019: they go whole with their half; the small ones near the line in between; the vines at .030 stay). thorn_s length × (1 + g), ax with it, the root fixed; thornB whole × g. The door: Christ −π f, Mary π − π f; Mary shows from f .5, Christ until f 1; the gold eyes from the signal until f .5. Closing (not in the sheet): the figures back over .55 s, then the thorns and halves over .5 s ending at .85 s (JNW cT). openK = f (the world model 'b' past 90°).
- The same shot either form: J2WB is WPN.janus2 with proj j2b, noMag only (same rec, kickX, spread, sound); j2Flame for both (a big muzzle flash, a fireball, smoke; the view model's flash larger and orange). The j2b grenade is the same model and speed; it does not count toward the signal (as JANUS-1). No violet: no light while open, no form-start burst / kick / shake (JNW calm, lit null), no jitter or tint in the form, no closing motes.
- Right click during the reload (the signal up) is kept (a.j2Q) and opens the form when the barrel shuts; a bot with the signal and a zombie in sight queues the same way (else its 1 s reloads would never leave it a free frame).
- World models: janus2n (Christ, white eyes, no thornB), janus2s (gold eyes), janus2b (the form baked at g = f = 1: Mary, thorns grown, halves apart); parts without fillets (3.2k triangles; the view model is 39k with the arms). The kill-feed / buy-menu icon draws janus2n.
- Sounds (the sheet's words): j2fire (a heavy blast, a 1 s tail), j2open (철컥), j2shut (텅), j2draw (kept), j2awake (wakes over 0.85 s: the halves unlock, the thorns grind out, the figures turn, a choir and a rush of air as it ends), j2end; j2fireb is gone.
- janus.js: jnOT / jnCT (JNW oT / cT, default JN.OPEN / JN.CLOSE) in jnOpen, jnRemote, jnOpenFx, jnClose; jnOpenK uses JNW openK if there is one; jnBStart and the world-model swap skip the burst, kick and shake when calm; the open light only with lit. JANUS-1 unchanged.
- Tests: studio renders of every state (A, signal, B, the flip at 45 / 90 / 135°, broken open, the world models); first person: idle, the reload frame by frame (the case clears the frame, the hand at the chamber), the first draw, the transition, B, closing; logic: one shot → reload at once, 6 shots in 6 s, B 0.85 s locked then 18 shots in 5 s with the magazine untouched, the form 6.7 s in all, five counted blasts → the signal, a right click mid-reload opens when it shuts, blast 120 at the centre (armour takes its half), 73 at 4.3 m, nothing at 4.5; a 150 s Mutation with every bot carrying it (24 forms opened, n / s / b models, no errors). Multiplayer not tested here: the owner tests it.

## v6.67 — the terms, the privacy policy and 회원 탈퇴 (legal/ → privacy.html · terms.html; account.js LEGAL / deleteAccount; shop.js ACCW 'del' / 'bye' and the sign-up box; schema.sql qz_account_delete)
- The owner picked 1 and 2 of the launch set: the documents (the game collects e-mail, nicknames, records, the browser in error reports and inquiry contacts with no policy; Google's Branding wants a privacy policy and home page) and account deletion (there was none). Not 3 (locking 원본 gore) yet.
- Pages: source/legal/shell.html (header with 한국어 / English, footer back to the game and to the other page, a small script: ?lang= or navigator.language, the toggle rewrites ?lang on the a[data-keep] links), page.css, privacy.html / terms.html (two <article lang> each), legal.json (operator, operator_en, email, db_region, effective). build.py: {{key}} from legal.json (escaped), <!--if:key-->…<!--/if--> / <!--ifnot:key-->…<!--/if-->, fails on a {{key}} left over, writes privacy.html / terms.html into dist and dist/standalone (copied to the repo root with index / admin); the game js's '%LEGAL_VER%' becomes the effective date. The email is empty for now (the policy points to 문의하기 only) — the owner fills a public address and the region; the owner's private account address never goes in.
- Privacy policy (PIPC order): items by source (e-mail sign-up, Google, play, 문의, error reports, access logs; what others see; room chat not stored, friend messages the latest 100; P2P shares the IP with the room), purposes, retention (account data until deletion; inquiries 180 days after closed; errors 2,000 kinds; admin log kept unlinked with nicknames removed; access logs per the providers), destruction, no third parties, processors / transfers abroad (Supabase, Vercel, Google, Cloudflare, PeerJS — from net.js: Google / Cloudflare STUN, peerjs.com signalling and TURN), rights (in-game: see, rename, delete, error reports off; else 10 days via contact), under 14 cannot sign up, security, no cookies (localStorage), the person responsible, the four remedy bodies (분쟁조정위원회 1833-6972 kopico.go.kr, 침해신고센터 118 privacy.kisa.or.kr, 대검 1301 spo.go.kr, 경찰청 182 ecrm.police.go.kr), changes 7 / 30 days ahead. Terms: 15 articles + 부칙 (14+, accounts and nicknames, game currency has no cash value and is not traded, prohibited acts, restrictions with appeal through 문의하기, P2P, change / close 30 days ahead with no compensation, deletion, liability limited except intent / gross negligence, rights, Korean law). Both in 해요체 Korean with an English version (Korean prevails).
- Game: LEGAL (account.js: links(L) for footers, sent(L) for sentences, ?lang=en from the English UI); the lobby footer, ACCW 'in' (a line under the form), the providers' line ("계속하면 … 동의하는 것으로 봐요"), 'up' gets the required box (만 14세 이상 + 동의; the sign-up sends data.terms = LEGAL.ver), 'me' gets the links and 회원 탈퇴, the 문의하기 window's ctx line links the policy. The phone layout hides the lobby footer: the windows carry the links.
- 회원 탈퇴: ACCW 'del' (what goes, a password box when ACC.hasPw() — app_metadata.providers has email; ACC.refreshUser() on entering, so a Google-only account is not asked —, type 탈퇴 / DELETE) → ACC.deleteAccount(pw): a password grant first (wrong → invalid_credentials), qz_account_delete('delete'), then the session, qz_me, qz_recFor and the qz_nickAsk entry go and the window says goodbye ('bye'). qz_account_delete: not_signed_in / confirm_needed / admin_account, takes the rename old/new and room_close host_name out of the admin log lines about it and its nickname out of other players' gift 'to' and ref_reward 'from' coin-log details, then deletes the auth.users row (every table cascades or sets null — checked by a census of all 40 foreign keys to auth.users). Supabase's April 2025 auth-schema restrictions cover DDL only; deleting rows from auth.users in a security definer function still works.
- Tests: local Postgres 16 fresh / re-run / upgrade from v6.66; the deletion: refused with a wrong word, for the owner (admin_account) and for anon (no execute); after it the census finds no row pointing at the account, the admin log lines are unlinked with the nickname keys gone, B's gift line and C's referral line lose the name (another gift line keeps its own), B keeps the gun A gave, friends / referrals / rooms gone, the others intact; the old token: qz_me refused (no profile for a gone account), a second delete not_signed_in. Headless (mock: qz_account_delete, app_metadata in sessions): the footer and window links, the sign-up refused unticked and through ticked with meta.terms 2026-10-17; 회원 탈퇴 for an e-mail account (wrong word, no password, wrong password refused, admin refused, then deleted: signed out, local state gone, chip back to 로그인), for a Google account (no password box, DELETE accepted); the links open the pages in a new tab and a link inside the box's label does not tick it; the pages in ko-KR at 1280 and en-US at 390 (no placeholders left, no sideways scroll, the toggle and its ?lang links).

## v6.66 — Google sign-in and 문의하기 (sb.js oauth, account.js providers, shop.js nickname window; inquiry.js; schema.sql qz_on_signup / inquiries; admin 문의)
- The owner asked for Google login and a contact channel before launch. The client already did the OAuth implicit flow (ACC.oauth → /auth/v1/authorize, init reads #access_token); what was missing: the buttons, a safe nickname and a way to write to the operators.
- Google: sb.js oauth:['google']. ACC.loadProviders (init) asks the project's public auth settings (GET /auth/v1/settings → external.google) once; ACCW and the admin gate show only providers switched on there (a failed lookup shows SB.oauth as is), so nothing shows until the owner turns Google on in Supabase. oauthUrl adds prompt=select_account for Google (GoTrue passes unknown query params on to the provider): the account picker every time (PC방). Admin API.oauth the same. init returns kind 'oauth' after a provider return (welcome toast).
- Nickname privacy: qz_on_signup used the metadata name / full_name / e-mail prefix — a Google sign-up would have shown the real name to everyone. Now only raw_user_meta_data.nickname of an e-mail sign-up (raw_app_meta_data.provider = email) counts; anything else (or a cleaned nickname of 생존자) → qz_auto_nick(): 생존자 + 4 digits, 8 tries for one nobody has. qz_me's late profile uses the same rule. The client: an account named /^생존자\d{4}$/ gets ACCW mode 'nick' once per browser (localStorage qz_nickAsk = uids), after the what's-new window and when the lobby shows; 'nick' validates 2..16 and closes with a toast; Enter in the nickname box (nick / me) changes it.
- inquiries (table near the top with RLS on and no table access; functions in the 「문의」 part at the end): qz_inquiry_send(kind, body 5..1000, contact ≤100, ctx ≤800 chars) — anyone; signed in 5 an hour / 20 a day (inq_slow), guests 60 an hour all together and 300 waiting (inq_busy); a banned account may write; ticket = gen_random_uuid hex (a guest's browser keeps it); closed rows older than 180 days deleted on each send. qz_my_inquiries(tickets, mark): the account's rows + the tickets' (30), new = answers unread, mark sets seen. qz_me inq_new, qz_ping inq (the account's unread answers). Admin: qz_admin_inquiries(status new|done|all, before, limit; contact only for the owner, has_contact for staff), qz_admin_inquiry_reply(id, text, done) (seen false → the dot), qz_admin_inquiry_done(id, done), qz_admin_inquiry_del (owner); all in admin_log (inq_reply / inq_done / inq_open / inq_del); qz_admin_whoami inq_new for the menu's count.
- inquiry.js (after gunpop.js): INQ — lobby .tutil button 'inq' (ICO.ask; only with accounts on; in the 더보기 menu on a small screen) and the ban window's 문의하기 (kind account). Tabs 문의 쓰기 / 내 문의; kinds with their own hint; the guest's contact box; the ctx line shown (version · browser · screen · language); keys stay out of the game (keydown / keyup stopped). Dots: ACC.me.inqN + tNew (ticket-only rows, asked once by peek() when the account is known) on the button and on .tmore; a toast when the count goes up. Opening 내 문의 marks them read. Added inqWin to MBOX.busyUI and the window watchers (mailbox, gunpop, newsban, srvnote popup).
- Admin 문의 (#/inquiries[/new|done|all], staff too): cards (kind, player link or 게스트, banned tag, age, body, contact + copy for the owner, the ctx line with the full UA on hover), 답변 쓰기 → a box (draft kept across reloads) with 답변하고 닫기 / 열어 둔 채 답변, 답변 없이 닫기 / 다시 열기, 삭제 (owner, asked). A guest's answer box warns that anyone on that browser can read it.
- supabase/README.md section 3: the Google Cloud (Google Auth Platform: Branding, Audience → Publish, Data Access openid, Clients → Web application, JS origin = the site, redirect URI = the Supabase callback) and Supabase (Sign In / Providers → Google: Client ID / Secret) steps; Redirect URLs https://site/** covers index.html and admin.html; the consent screen shows <ref>.supabase.co without a custom domain. Also the 문의 rules, and SB lives in sb.js (the old text said account.js).
- Tests: local Postgres 16 — fresh, upgrade from v6.65, re-run (with data); sign-ups: e-mail with a nickname keeps it, e-mail without / blank → 생존자NNNN, Google (name, full_name, e-mail, even a nickname key) and Kakao → 생존자NNNN, no provider → e-mail rule; qz_me's late profile → 생존자NNNN. Inquiries: guest send / ticket / mine, short / long refused, anon cannot read or write the table or call admin functions, a player gets not_admin, 5 an hour then inq_slow, 20 a day, banned may send, the owner sees the contact and staff only has_contact, paging, replies (seen false → qz_me 1, qz_ping 1, peek 1, mark → 0), signed in + an old ticket (both rows), done / open / delete (staff owner_only), the admin log, 60 guests an hour then inq_busy, 300 waiting, the 180-day cleanup, the grants. Headless (mock: /auth/v1/settings, oauth_<provider>_<tag> tokens, the inquiry RPCs, __mockInqReply): providers both / kakao off / none / lookup failing / the sign-up tab; oauthUrl; a guest's report with a contact (ctx v6.66 …), the ticket kept, the answer → dot + toast → read; English; a Google return → the nickname window (too short refused, Enter saves, lobby name), 내 계정's Enter, a signed-in inquiry (no contact box, no ticket), the sixth refused with the text kept, the heartbeat's count → dot on the wide bar and on 더보기 → read; the ban window's 문의하기 → kind 계정; the same browser again: no second question, a welcome toast; phone 844×390: the 더보기 menu item (44 px), the window fits, the dot on 더보기; a match start closes the window. Admin (stub): gate buttons by provider, the cards, a draft surviving 새로 고침, reply & close / keep open / close without / delete, the counts in the menu, the log lines, staff without contacts or delete.

## v6.65 — ranking limits and the error log (schema.sql qz_claim / qz_round_claim / qz_import_rec / qz_admin_suspects, err_log; errlog.js; admin 오류 기록)
- The owner asked for the two pre-launch items: the ranking could be faked (a page reports its own match numbers; one call with the per-claim maximums put anyone on top — rec_best 30,000 at once — and qz_import_rec added up to 5,000 games, 400,000 kills and 300,000 xp once per account), and errors on players' pages were never seen.
- Ranking limits: per match (qz_claim) the numbers count only as far as the minutes since the last paid match allow (from the account's creation the first time; counted up to 45, 60 for the scenario): 8 kills (16 in the scenario), 4 infections and 150 score (250) + 300 a minute; per round (qz_round_claim) 12 kills + 3, 4 infections + 1, 300 score + 100 a minute (counted up to 10). Per day (Korean time; profiles.cap_day / cap_xp / cap_kills / cap_inf) 2,000 kills, 800 infections and 150,000 xp (rounds and matches together). Every number is checked as sent: past the limit it is cut, and coin_log.detail.cut keeps {what: [sent, kept]} (match details now also keep mins, rounds secs). Coins, missions and achievements read the cut numbers. qz_import_rec is closed: it marks the account imported and adds nothing (the client's call still succeeds). Normal play stays well under it (a 21-minute match of 45 kills / 2,400 score, a 2.5-minute round of 18 kills / 520 score: nothing cut).
- Admin page 의심 유저: 잘린 기록 (cut: accounts with cut claims in 14 days, how often, the last cut) first, 한 판 점수 (score: the highest scores as sent, with the minutes since the match before).
- Error log: errlog.js (built right after util.js and sb.js, which moved up — sb.js is only the SB settings) listens for window errors, unhandled rejections, console.error with an Error and main.js's boot / frame catches (ERRLOG.report(e,'boot'|'frame')). Each kind once a page (src + message + the stack's top 3 lines), 8 a page, one every 2 s, sent as a guest (apikey only, never the sign-in token) to qz_err_report with the message (300), the stack's first 8 lines with addresses cut to the file name, src, the version (PATCH[0]), ctx {mode, st, map, net solo/host/guest, gore, scale, touch, lang} and the user agent. Network failures and server answers (.net / .status, PeerJS .type, 'Failed to fetch', 'Script error.') stay out. Off without SB, on file://, with Options → 게임플레이 → 오류 자동 보고 (CFG.errRep) off, or localStorage qz_noerr.
- err_log (fp = md5(version | src | message | stack top), n, first / last seen; RLS on, no table access) and err_rate (reports a minute, all pages together: 600, older rows cleared); 2,000 kinds at most, trimmed back to 1,800 by last seen. qz_admin_errors (staff too) / qz_admin_err_clear (one or all, in admin_log as err_clear). Admin page 오류 기록: kinds seen last first, count, version, where, the message; a row opens to the context, the stack and the browser; 지우기 per row, 전부 지우기 for the owner.
- Tests: local Postgres 16 — fresh, upgrade from v6.64 and a re-run; claims: the import adds nothing; a cheat claim (9999 kills / 30,000 score) on a 30-minute-old account → 80 / 4,800 and cut kept; 2 minutes later → 16 kills / 8 infections / 600 score; a normal 21-minute match and a 2.5-minute round untouched; the day's caps (1,990 kills, 149,900 xp counted → 10 / 100 more) and the next day reset; rounds 30 s apart cut to 9 / 3 / 250; the scenario 40 minutes → score 10,300; suspects cut / score; the ranking reads the cut best. Error log: same error counted once, empty message refused, junk clipped, anon cannot read the table, a player gets not_admin, 600 a minute, 2,700 kinds trimmed to 1,800, clear one / all logged. Headless game: reports for a window error, a rejection, a logged error and a failing frame, each once; network errors left out; 8 a page; no sign-in token; the option off sends nothing. Admin page views rendered with stubbed answers.

## v6.64 — the blood & gore setting: 원본 / 15세 (default) / 피 끄기 (util.js GORE; fx, game, models, design, gunart, shaders; no server change)
- The owner wants to put the game up for free, and a non-profit game made by an individual is exempt from rating in Korea only while nothing in it meets the 청소년이용불가 criteria (realistic blood and body damage are what tips a shooter there). So the public build will be the 15세 one; for now the level is a setting (Options → 게임플레이 → 피 표현, CFG.gore 2 / 1 / 0, default 1) so the two can be compared. Later the 원본 code is to be taken out of the public build.
- GORE (util.js): lv read from the saved cfg at load (the atlases painted at boot already use it), set(v) from applyCfg, de(c) turns a blood-red paint to grime below 2 (only red-dominant colours: r > 1.6 × g and b).
- 15세 (lv 1): no FX.gib / FX.limb / dismember / spurts / pools / blood trail of hurt zombies, no 'gib' sound on a kill; FX.blood throws 3 × amt small dark drops and a thin mist, nothing lands as a splat and no decal on the wall; the infection mist is dark rust; the infection screen (FS_POST uGoreK 0) drains to a muted red with a dark-red edge and no veins; the claw slash trail is dark. Zombie designs: no rib / vert / guts / stump parts (design.js defZombie), the runner's top a dark rag; decor (models.js goreOp): flesh → bruise, gash → scratch, soak → grimes (dry, de-coloured, 60 %), blood → spots, ribs / bonep / drip left out (bites and wounds paint through flesh); zskin blots and flesh / meat materials through de(); faces: the gouged eye an empty dark socket, dark tears, no cheek hole, no blood from the mouth. First-person zombie arms (gunart.js gaZArm) bruised and filthy instead of torn, claws (gaClaw) dirty instead of bloody; the Nata Knife's and Skull-9's stained steel weathered instead.
- 피 끄기 (lv 0): as 15세, and FX.blood throws grey dust and grains, the infection mist grey.
- Changing it (goreApply, fx.js): below 2 the torn-off limbs, the blood decals (FX.dec.fr tracks each decal's frame; FX.clearBlood clears 4..7), spurts and pool timers go and every rig is whole again; across the 2 boundary goreRepaint: the zombie-arm / claw slots repainted in place with the boot painter's noise (fbm periodic over the atlas width), paintKkAtlas / paintSkAtlas again, ICONS cleared; every built zombie skin rebuilt from its design (models.js charRebuild: atlas + both geometries, the actors' rigs and any scene mesh switched over, the old geometries disposed, its portraits dropped), the ones on screen first, one per tick (~250 ms each headless), a newer change cancelling the older chain (GORE.gen).
- Tests: boot at the default 15세 (no ribs / guts / stumps, uGoreK 0); headshot and HE kills at 15세: no limbs, bodies whole, no blood decals; at 원본: limbs, stumps, 45–102 blood decals; back to 15세: decals and limbs cleared, bodies whole; 피 끄기: no red particles; seven changes 30 ms apart settle on the last; the options select (원본 / 15세 (순화) / 피 끄기) and Cancel restoring it. Close-ups of 18 zombie kinds front and back at both levels, the infection screen and the claws. Matches at each level (Mutation, Original, TDM): no page errors.

## v6.63 — the view no longer spins on a fast flick (main.js pointer lock and mouse movement; i18n relock; no server change)
- The owner: on a PC a slightly hard sideways move of the mouse sent the view spinning. Two causes, both fixed. (1) The first refused pointer-lock request (Chrome refuses one made within about a second of the user pressing Esc, or before the window has focus) set Main.lockFail for the rest of the page's life: no more lock requests, the cursor free, and the edge turning (main.js: the cursor near the screen edge turns the view at up to 2.6 rad/s) — a quick flick put the cursor on the edge and the view kept turning. (2) Chrome's cursor-based movement: OS pointer acceleration on fast moves and the jumps it throws in when it warps the cursor back (the old filter only dropped events past 500, legit fast flicks included).
- lock(): requestPointerLock({unadjustedMovement:true}) first (raw input: Chrome / Edge on Windows, Mac, ChromeOS) → rawOk / rawMv; NotSupportedError → the plain request (and plain from then on); a browser that returns no promise → plain assumed; one request at a time (lockPend, freed after 2 s whatever happens). onLockFail(e): failures within 300 ms count once (the promise and pointerlockerror both report); a refusal shows T('relock') and the next click asks again; lockFail (edge turning) only when requestPointerLock is missing or four refusals came before the lock ever worked (lockedOnce; a frame that may not lock). pointerlockchange to locked resets the count.
- mousemove: 150 ms after the lock engages skipped (as before); any event past 2,500 dropped; raw input otherwise taken whole; the browser's own movement drops one event past 900, or one past max(180, 5 × the recent average) against the recent direction — never two in a row (a real flick back loses one frame at most). The averages reset after 120 ms without movement.
- Tests: a stubbed lock: 4 refusals before ever locking → lockFail, 6 after a lock → never; NotSupportedError → raw, plain, plain; three lock() calls → one request. Movement: steady 40s pass, a −450 jump amid them dropped, a real flick back loses one frame, 1,000 then 1,000 → the second passes, raw passes everything but 3,000.

## v6.62 — Ctrl+R never reloads the page (main.js; no server change)
- The owner asked that Ctrl+R not reload this page: Ctrl is crouch (main.js c.duck: KeyC or Control) and R reloads, so crouch-reloading reloaded the whole game (the match's beforeunload prompt only asked first). main.js Main.init: a capture-phase keydown listener preventDefaults Ctrl / Cmd + R (Shift too, not Alt; e.code KeyR or e.key r/R for other layouts) everywhere, text fields included; the game's own handler still sees the key (reload works while crouching). The main handler's old exception (every Ctrl combo prevented but Ctrl+R) is gone. F5 and the browser's button still reload; Ctrl+W (crouch + forward) cannot be blocked by a page in Chrome outside keyboard lock, the match's beforeunload prompt still guards it.
- Headless Chromium runs no browser accelerators (Ctrl+R and F5 do nothing there even before the fix), so the test checks the events come out defaultPrevented.

## v6.61 — the five new guns remastered (realguns2.js models and finishes; gunart.js: a 256-slot atlas, deformed parts; no server change)
- The owner found the v6.60 models' design far too plain and asked for three times the quality. Each model was rebuilt from the real gun's proportions with the detail the earlier ones lacked, checked in a studio render (front-left 3/4, side, top), in first person (idle, fire, reload, first draw) and as world models and icons.
- gunart.js: GA.H 2048 → 4096 (8 × 32 slots; the first 128 were all taken; every painter already indexes by GA.P / GA_SLOTS). partDef: a part may carry kz [kx front, kx back, ky front, ky back] (its cross-section scaled along its own z about the anchors ax / ay), sy [dy front, dy back] (sheared along z) and ky [kx bottom, kx top, kz bottom, kz top] (rbox's taper along y); normals follow (inverse transpose of the deformation). gunIcon draws deformed parts with their real outline.
- realguns2.js materials (paintRg2Atlas, chained on paintCgAtlas): fde / fdeG (flat dark earth polymer, stippled), park (parkerized), polyB (black polymer a shade off mtB), plum (AK-74 bakelite), odg / odgD (olive drab), dScale (crimson dragon scales edged in gold), perf (perforated sheet). Gloss alpha 252 on the metals (no glow in the view model: FS_VM glows below .97).
- Models (parts / view-model triangles): Vector 132 / 14 k (FDE upper with the bore low in it, the Super V nose: a short flat with its rail then kz slope to the magazine well, flip-up sights, slotted rail, charging handle tag chg, squared shroud with vents, A2 flash hider, folding adapter, black collapsed M4 stock, a Glock-pattern magazine with its extension), AN-94 123 / 15 k (rivets, pressed panels, ribbed dust cover, drum rear sight, polymer handguards with grooves and vents, tall hooded front sight, bayonet lug, cup brake, AK-74M grip, plum magazine canted 15°), M82A1 164 / 20 k (parkerized upper open at the front: five windows a side with the barrel and recoil springs inside, slotted rail, scope with cone, turrets and dark ocular, fluted barrel and arrowhead brake (tag aux), bipod folded, black lower and buttstock with cheek riser, monopod, big pad), SPAS-12 DB 89 / 10 k (dragon-scale panels framed in gold, gold rib lines, perforated heat shield of five strips round the barrel, gold bands and crown with an ember, ribbed forend tag pump with a crimson line, arm hook stock, shells on the right), M32 161 / 19 k (olive drab frame, cylinder tag mag with piv on its axle: dark olive chambers with a deep yellow band, mouths and noses, star plates, quad rail with slots and a hazard band, reflex sight, vertical foregrip, stock on a tube). World models 7–13 k triangles (the AK-47 60R has 16 k). Anchors moved: Vector sup / muzzle / eject, AN-94 sup / muzzle, M82 muzzle / eject, SPAS muzzle / eject, M32 sup / muzzle (+ piv.mag).
- A showcase sheet (studio renders, first person in the bazaar at dusk, effects in Q-7) was made for the owner (not part of the game).

## v6.60 — five more guns: Vector, AN-94, Barrett M82A1 (shop), SPAS-12 Dragon's Breath, M32 Cluster (crates) (realguns2.js, after glock; schema.sql)
- The owner asked for five: three real guns and two crate-level guns named "a real gun + a modifier". realguns2.js holds the models (boxes on the atlas, no new materials: the atlas is full), stats, effects, sounds and the hooks; pvp.js PVP.GUNS lists the Vector and the AN-94 (a late module must not touch PVP at load: pvp.js comes after, TDZ stopped the whole script once).
- KRISS VECTOR (slot 1 smg, 3,500): 25 a round, 1,150 rpm (4 frames: 15 / s), mag 25, res 125, spread [.01,.026,.08], recoil .005 / .004 (almost no climb), reload 2.4. AN-94 (rifle, 5,000): 33 a round, 620 rpm (6 frames) with W.hyper 2: the first 2 rounds of a pull at hyperRpm 1900 (2 frames: 1,800 effective), a pull is fresh after .25 s off the trigger (game.js non-burst branch); right click gBurst {n 2, 1900 rpm, cd .28, spread ×.45, recoil ×.3}. BARRETT M82A1 (sniper, 8,500): 165 a round, 110 rpm semi, mag 10, res 30, zoom [30,12], kb 26, speed .82; W.pen 4 / penK .75: weapons.js shotTrace hands a W.pen gun to penTrace (every zombie on the line to the first wall via tbLine, up to 4, 165 → 124 → 93 → 70; the tracer to the wall or the last one; no hit → the plain trace); VMX.m82 runs the barrel (tag aux) back .035 for .16 s.
- Frame quantization: nextFire = t + 60 / rpm at 60 fps, so a rate on an exact frame multiple drops a frame (1800 → 3 frames); the rates above are picked off the multiples.
- SPAS-12 DRAGON'S BREATH (crate shotgun): 9 pellets × 21, 230 rpm semi, 8 shells loaded one at a time (shellRel .42), res 48. Every shot: spdFlame (fireGun wrapper; 28 / 16 flame particles at 18-40 m/s, drag 1.5, sparks, smoke, a light, 'salgust'); damageActor wrapper: a pellet (not a dot, not a ghost) sets t.burnT 3 / burnSrc / burnW 'spas12d' on a living zombie → nyw.js updBurn (11 every .25 s, the host deals; a client's burn reaches the host with its effects). Right click = W.gMode (game.js: the second mode is Object.assign({}, W, gMode.o), names n / n0, short s / s0 for the touch button): pump action — 85 rpm (the non-burst branch now takes BW.rpm), 27 a pellet, spread [.038,.05,.08], kb 4.4, stag .6.
- M32 CLUSTER (crate special): six 40 mm rounds (proj 'gl', 150 rpm semi), reload 3.4, res 18; right click alt 'fan' (the Crimson's fanning: the rest at 300 rpm). detonate wrapper: a cluster 'gl' gets M32C numbers (R 4, 200, kb 12, he) and then m32Split throws 5 'glc' bomblets back and out from the hit (impact rounds, fuse .9-1.5 s, mesh m32bomb); a 'glc' detonates as m32Pop (radius 2.6, up to 85 with falloff .4-1, he, LOS) and never reaches the other detonate wrappers. Every page makes the bomblets from the grenade it sees (a remote one is a ghost: harmless). VMX.m32c turns the cylinder (tag mag) a sixth per round fired; FDS.wind (first draw) gives it a sixth by hand.
- Crates: CRATE_GUNS has eight. Bots (AI.rollLd wrapper): 5 % Vector, 5 % AN-94, 2.5 % Barrett. Shop: SHOP_PRICE 3,500 / 5,000 / 8,500, SHOP_NOTSOLD + spas12d, m32c (and dglock). First draws (GUN_DESIGN in the module: W.fd / W.ch): the Vector's handle on the left, the AN-94's and the Barrett's on the right (heavy), the SPAS pumped, the M32's cylinder wound.
- Server: gun_prices rows vector 3500, an94 5000, m82 8500 (sold), spas12d / m32c (0, not sold) — run schema.sql (until then the three answer not_for_sale). Checked on Postgres 16 fresh and as an upgrade of v6.59.
- Tests: unit (Vector 15 / s at 25; AN-94 hyperburst spacing [0,2,8,14 …] frames and the 2-round burst; Barrett through 5 zombies in a line → [165,124,93,70,0]; SPAS burn 2.98 s at 44 / s, pump .71 s apart, shells back to 8; M32 1 grenade + 5 bomblets, damage on 5 zombies, the fan sends 6; a crate gun goes to slot 1; PvP list; bot rolls; buy menu / shop; first draws; icons), first-person / third-person looks, the plume and the bomblets in a match, reg.py.

## v6.59 — the Glock and the Double Glock (glock.js, after m4dk; schema.sql gun_prices rows)
- The owner asked for a Glock with ordinary numbers and a Double Glock with crate-weapon power. glock.js: one model function glkParts(S) (the Glock 17 / 18C shapes in boxes: squared slide with rear serrations, U-notch sights with white dots, barrel hood in the port, one-slot rail, square trigger guard with the safety blade, steep grip (GLK_RK .3 — rx is negative: the bottom back) with stippled panels and finger grooves); the 18C adds compensator cuts, the slide selector and the 33-round magazine. No new atlas materials (the atlas is full at 128): mtB / mtP / tan2 / deGoldP / mtRed / white.
- GLOCK (slot 2, sold 1,000 coins): 27 a round, 450 rpm semi, 17 + 1, reload 1.9, res 119. DOUBLE GLOCK (slot 2, crate gun): two-tone pair (dglock right: black slide on tan; dglockL left: tan slide on black), 30 a round, 1,250 rpm full auto (20 / s at 60 fps: about 600 a second on the body, the crate primaries' range), 33 × 2 + 2, res 264, reload 2.9.
- Right click on both = a burst mode, game.js: W.gBurst {n, rpm in the burst, cd after it, spr spread ×, dm damage ×}; a.gbm[id] keeps the mode per gun (survives switching); gbShot(W) is the gun as it fires in the mode (burst/semi set, cached), gbOn(a,W); the burst loop and the trigger use it (BW). A gBurst gun's right click does not flip the akimbo stance (dualB). Glock: 3 at 1,300 rpm, .42 s after, spread ×1.3. Double Glock: 6 at 2,000 rpm, .32 s after, spread ×.55, damage ×1.1. HUD name: ' · 3점사' / ' · 6점사' (cmbTag); touch: a burst-mode gun auto-repeats like a semi.
- Crates (supply.js): CRATE_GUNS has six; the pick skips a crate gun already in its own slot, and give() puts a gun in its own slot (W.slot: the Double Glock replaces the sidearm, the primary stays; a remote page does the same). Not owned → not kept into the next round (ldKeep), the sidearm of the noted loadout comes back. PvP: the Glock is allowed (PVP.GUNS), the Double Glock not. Bots: the Glock is one of the pistols they may roll (ai.js).
- Server: gun_prices ('glock',1000,sold) and ('dglock',0,not sold) — run schema.sql (until then the Glock's buy answers not_for_sale). Checked on Postgres 16 fresh and as an upgrade of v6.58.
- Tests: unit (semi 1 a press, burst 3 / 6 a press and only one burst while held, mode kept across switches, 17+1 / 66+2 reloads, alternate sides, spread .0123 → .0069 in the burst, 33 a round in it, crate → slot 2 with the primary kept, not kept next round, PvP list, bot roll ~4 %), first-person idle / fire both sides / reload / draw, third-person on bots, icons, shop cards (1,000 / crates only), buy menu row, news slides, a bot match with only Glocks (both fire, no errors), reg.py.

## v6.58 — UI pass (lobby top bar, a lobby that fits, window and phone fixes; no server change)
- The owner asked for an overall UI/UX pass; a screenshot audit (1920x1080, 1366x768, 1280x720, 1100x620, phones 740x360 / 667x375, signed in through test/mock.js) found real breakage first, fixed here.
- Lobby top bar (lobby.js buildTitle, style.css v6.58 block): .tbtns now holds only the main buttons the modules put in (shop, missions, achievements, ranking, inventory, friends, admin); settings / controls / patch notes / fullscreen / landscape / language moved to .tutil (icons with a title, the words hidden); at ≤1180 px .tutil becomes the drop-down of a ⋯ button (.tmore, act 'tmore'; any click elsewhere or Esc closes it; two columns on a phone). Labels never wrap (they broke letter by letter once signed in). .prof is the account button (data-act acc, class pa) when accounts are on; the chip's account button is an icon. lobFit() (after every build, resize, UI.show('menu')): while .tbtns overflows → .lobTop.t1 (smaller logo), t2 (no logo), t3 (main buttons as icons, a title from their label). Phones (≤820 px) drop the logo.
- The lobby fits the window: .lob max-height 100%, the bottom panels shrink first (grid row minmax(0,1fr), their lists scroll inside), ≥1500 px wide → a 1320 px lobby. The record panel (.lrec) shows today's missions (rank.js misLobby: rows with tier, text, bar, count; a click opens the missions window; a guest gets a sign-in line).
- Fixed: the global .mbar rule (lobby card bars, position absolute) also took the missions' progress bars, which were drawn stacked at the bottom of the screen → the lobby rules are .mc .mbar now. The notice panel's NEW button (squeezed to 22 px by the + button's !important width) is a pill 「NEW 신규 소개」.
- News window (newsban.js): past 12 slides a counter (3 / 56) and a bar instead of dots — the dots pushed 「2시간 동안 보지 않기」 out of the window.
- .win windows (room settings, options, waiting room, multiplayer): the title bar and the button row are sticky at every height, the offsets reaching past the screen's padding (-18 px; -8 px on a short landscape; the safe-area on touch) so nothing shows past them. Character / zombie class rows are grids (--n columns) in one line; the section names never wrap.
- Multiplayer screen (net.js UI.mpMenu) is a .win.mpw: name, create (gold), public/private, join by code on the left, the open rooms on the right, the link state in the footer. The name field shows myName() as its placeholder; an empty name joins as the account nickname when signed in (a guest still gets 플레이어NN).
- In game: #zsel a grid (3 × 2; one row of six on a phone, descriptions clamped); the pause menu two columns on a phone (Quit was off the screen); html.touch #hNote at 38 % with width max-content (it sat on the big round text and wrapped at half the screen); the results' button row sticky under a long table; #touch.ov.zt hides the zombie's skill / class / night-vision buttons under an overlay. Difficulty cards short on a phone (OK was cut off). Touch: the coin / mail / account buttons 40 px, the results' shop and mission buttons 36 px.
- Tests: the screenshot audit again at every size (no wrapped or hidden button, the ⋯ menu, EN at 1280 → t1), aud/audit.js on phones (no covered or small targets), the mock missions in the lobby and the window, the profile → account window, guest lobby.

## v6.57 — achievements (업적) and titles (칭호) (ach.js, built last; schema.sql)
- The owner picked it from the suggested next patches. Server (schema.sql v6.57): ach_defs (the list — this file is its source: every run puts the numbers back), ach_claims (tiers taken), profiles.ach (jsonb counters the record does not keep: hs, dmg, wins, mvp, surv, zw, pk, pw, rw, kn, cu, mk, cr, bm), profiles.ach_fill (filled once from coin_log: every match / round paid before, clamped as qz_claim clamps; new profiles default true so it never runs twice), profiles.title (worn: null automatic, 'none', 'hof:<code>', or an achievement title id).
- Stats (qz_ach_stats): the record (kills, inf, games, lv = qz_level(xp) from a table of the game's own recLevel thresholds, hours = play_secs / 3600, guns = owned_guns rows), the chapters (ep, eph = hard or expert, epx = expert, medal = S medals, hof = first-clear teams) and the counters. qz_claim takes p_ach jsonb ({kn, cu, mk, cr, pl, df}: melee kills ≤ 40 and ≤ kills, cures ≤ 20, marks ≤ 200, crates ≤ 10, plants / defuses ≤ 12 each), adds hs / dmg / wins (Mutation · Original) / mvp / pk · pw (tdm ffa bomb race) / rw (race) itself, and answers ach_hit (tiers this match reached: stats before vs after) and ach_ready; qz_round_claim adds surv (survived) and zw (won, not survived) in orig / mut. qz_claim's signature gained p_ach: the old one is dropped first, grants updated; the client drops p_ach first on a PGRST202.
- Calls: qz_achievements (defs, stats, claimed, ready, title, worn), qz_ach_claim(p_id | null = all; coins outside the day's limit, decoders, coin_log 'ach'), qz_title_wear(id | 'none' | null; 'no_title' unless owned), qz_titles_of(names[16]) (public: worn titles by nickname). qz_me / qz_ranking / qz_friends / qz_invites return tt (worn) next to ft. Helpers revoked from the API. Checked on local Postgres 16: old → new with seeded history (backfill sums as expected, junk text in coin_log ignored), claims one / all, already_claimed, no_title, hof wear, p_ach junk (strings, arrays), old 11-argument calls, round counters, rerun idempotent, fresh database, new account defaults.
- 25 ladders / 76 tiers / 48 titles (grades 1–5), coins 155,300 and 70 decoders in all. The list lives in schema.sql (ach_defs rows) and test/mock.js (ACHD); names, descriptions and the title table (TITLES) in ach.js.
- ach.js: TTL — the one title shown next to a name: mine() (me.title from qz_me; a database before v6.57: the hall-of-fame title), hello() (adds tt to HOF.hello: '' = nothing), of(x) (actors: ttC from the roster [8]; waiting-room players: tt; a page without tt keeps the HOF path), line(l) (chat), row(x) (server rows: tt, else ft), ok(t, an) (another player's title only once qz_titles_of lists it for that account nickname; asked in batches of 16, kept 5 minutes; until then a hall-of-fame title stands on the HOF list), chip / mini / tag / prof / slots. HOF.prof and HOF.slots now call TTL's. Changed call sites: rank rows, friends (list + chat head), invites, ui.js results + scoreboard, pvp.js tables, net.js (hello, host keeps p.tt and the account nickname when tt is set, lobby cast, roster [8], name tags), chat.js (relay / add / render: tt before the name in the grade's colour).
- ACH: the window's 업적 page (summary, claim all, category chips with red dots, one card per ladder: next tier, pips, bar (.achB — not .mbar, which the lobby cards make absolute), reward, title chip, claim) and 칭호 page (worn + automatic / hide, owned, still to earn with the tier that gives each); rank.js got the two tabs and PG table. Lobby button 업적 (dot while rewards wait; phones hide it and the 미션 button carries the dot); results box .achRes (ach_hit) under the missions; ACC.claim wrapper → ACH.claimed (reloads ACC.ach, me.achReady; a first clear reloads me for the automatic title). Per-match counters G.achX (startMatch): killZombie / PVP.kill (melee, by me), vaxCure (by me), hndFx (a fresh mark by me), SUP.give (to me), BOMB.planted (me), BOMB.stop on the host (a finished defuse: counts mine, netEv bdf → CLIH.bdf). shopMatchStats carries them (ach) → ACC.claim p_ach.
- Lobby top bar: buttons 6px 9px (was 12px) so the extra button keeps the fullscreen one on screen at 1100 px. admin.js: coin_log reason 'ach' = 업적 보상.
- Tests: mock UI (sign-up, 14 tiers waiting from seeded numbers, claim all → coins + titles, auto title, wear, lobby chip, ranking chip, results box from a match claim with p_ach, chat line with the title, a remote title confirmed / a spoof refused / an old page, old database messages, old qz_claim fallback, no qz_titles_of), counters unit test (melee kill counted / gun kill not, cure, mark once, crates mine only, plant, finished vs unfinished defuse + bdf), phone layout, reg.py, a 200 s Mutation sim with the new hooks.
